[GLG_actual_data_01] ParameterChoice prime: all original numerical guards
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalRingCardinality/DataParams.lean#L62-L332

structure ParameterChoice' (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) : Type where

  CkLow : ℝ
  CkLow_pos : 0 < CkLow

  Ck6 : ℝ
  Ck6_pos : 0 < Ck6

  CfL : ℝ
  CfL_pos : 0 < CfL

  cLL : ℝ
  cLL_pos : 0 < cLL

  CnW : ℝ
  CnW_pos : 0 < CnW

  Crho : ℝ
  Crho_pos : 0 < Crho

  CDelG : ℝ
  CDelG_pos : 0 < CDelG

  CLw : ℝ
  CLw_pos : 0 < CLw

  Cj0 : ℝ
  Cj0_pos : 0 < Cj0

  Cw : ℝ
  Cw_pos : 0 < Cw
  cw : ℝ
  cw_pos : 0 < cw

  CDel : ℝ
  CDel_pos : 0 < CDel

  CCount : ℝ
  CCount_pos : 0 < CCount

  Ch0r : ℝ
  Ch0r_pos : 0 < Ch0r

  Ch0k : ℝ
  Ch0k_pos : 0 < Ch0k

  Ch0N : ℝ
  Ch0N_pos : 0 < Ch0N

  Cgap : ℝ
  Cgap_pos : 0 < Cgap

  CetaF : ℝ
  CetaF_pos : 0 < CetaF

  CF : ℝ
  CF_pos : 0 < CF

  CY : ℝ
  CY_pos : 0 < CY
  CYL : ℝ
  CYL_pos : 0 < CYL

  Cpde : ℝ
  Cpde_pos : 0 < Cpde

  CccK : ℝ
  CccK_pos : 0 < CccK

  qd_pow : L.sc.x ^ (2 * L.Ct + 5) < (D.host.q : ℝ) ∧ L.sc.x ^ (2 * L.Ct + 5) < (D.host.d : ℝ)

  logq_ge : L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.ez / 6) ≤ Real.log D.host.q

  logDerivative_le_power : |L.logDerivativeAtOne| ≤ L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps

  k_low : CkLow * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ jG * D.R.f * L.sc.L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps ≤ D.R.k

  k_up : (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ ((1 : ℝ) / 2) * D.R.n * R0 L

  radius_ge_sqrt_ratio : Real.sqrt ((D.R.k : ℝ) / D.R.m) ≤ R0 L

  k_ge_m2L : (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * L.sc.L ≤ D.R.k

  k_ge_mx : (D.R.m : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ ((1 : ℝ) / 2) * Real.log L.sc.x ≤ D.R.k

  klogk : Ck6 * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 6 * L.sc.L ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.log D.R.k

  logf_le : Real.log D.R.f ≤ Real.sqrt D.R.rS

  log_sq_conductor_le : Real.log D.R.f ^ 2 ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * L.sc.L

  loglogb : Real.log (Real.log L.sc.b) ≤ cLL * D.R.r * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r

  flogk : CfL * L.sc.L ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.log D.R.k

  eta_le : L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.b / ((D.R.t : ℝ) ^ 3 * D.R.f * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3)

  condE_le : (condE D : ℝ) ^ 36 * (D.R.t : ℝ) ^ 150 ≤ L.sc.b ^ ((1 : ℝ) / 2)

  TT_card_le : (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ≤ Nstar L

  rho_le : rhoP L Crho ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ)

  S_le : 4 * L.Sc ≤ L.LaG * L.sc.b' / D.R.k

  Del_le : 4 * CDelG * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * (D.s.Del : ℝ) ≤ L.LaG * L.sc.b'

  Lw_j : ∀ l : ℕ, 11 ≤ l → ∃ j : ℕ, lA L ≤ ((l : ℝ) - 1) ^ j ∧ 2 * j + 2 ≤ D.host.Lw

  Lw_le : (D.host.Lw : ℝ) ≤ CLw * Real.log L.sc.x

  jetIndex_le_log : (L.j0 : ℝ) ≤ Cj0 * Real.log L.sc.x

  bT_le : ∀ t, (L.bT t : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.b' + Nstar L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps * L.LaG * L.sc.b' / D.R.k

  topPlace_first_bound : ∀ τ (t : D.host.TT) (hm : 0 < D.R.m),
    (D.eTau τ : D.R.GG) = D.R.tau1 t →
    (((D.hc τ).b ⟨0, hm⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) = (L.bT t : ℝ) + (⌊L.sc.b' / L.sc.x⌋ : ℤ)

  lows_far : ∀ τ (i j : Fin D.R.m),
    2 * L.sc.b / lStar L + (D.R.t : ℝ) / 2 + 3 ≤ |(((D.hc τ).b i - (D.hc τ).e j : ℚ) : ℝ)|

  X''_ratio : ∀ j j' τ τ', 1 ≤ j → j ≤ D.R.m → 1 ≤ j' → j' ≤ D.R.m →
    wj D j τ ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n * wj D j' τ'

  cloud_place_bound : ∀ τ (h0 : 0 < D.R.k - 1), (D.eTau τ : D.R.GG) ∉ D.host.topPlaces →
    (((D.hc τ).u ⟨0, by omega⟩ - (D.hc τ).v ⟨0, h0⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) ≤ 2 * L.Sc

  uk_extreme : ∀ τ (hk : 2 ≤ D.R.k), (D.eTau τ : D.R.GG) ∉ D.host.topPlaces →
    (D.hc τ).u ⟨D.R.k - 1, by omega⟩ = (D.hc τ).v ⟨D.R.k - 2, by omega⟩ - 1 / 2

  w''_ge : (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ Cw ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n ∧ L.sc.b ^ cw ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n

  Del_ge : (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ CDel * (CDel * D.R.m) ^ 3 / D.div.etaF ^ CDel ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ) ∧
    CDel * D.R.m * D.R.N D.R.m ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ) ∧
    (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ CDel * (CDel * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 4 * L.sc.L) ^ 4 ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ) ∧
    (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 10 ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ)

  Del_count : ((D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * D.R.f * L.sc.L) ^ CCount ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ)

  logDel : Real.log (D.s.Del : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) / 2

  kS_le : (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.Sc ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n

  bm_le : ∀ τ (hm : 0 < D.R.m), (D.hc τ).b ⟨D.R.m - 1, by omega⟩ ≤ 2 * (D.hc τ).b ⟨0, hm⟩

  h0_ge : 12 * L.sc.b / lStar L + 2 * D.R.t + 8 ≤ h0 L ∧
    (D.R.k : ℝ) * D.R.t * L.sc.L ≤ h0 L ∧
    Ch0r * (D.R.r : ℝ) ^ 19 * (D.R.f : ℝ) ^ 5 * L.sc.L ≤ h0 L ∧
    ((D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * D.R.f * L.sc.L) ^ CCount ≤ h0 L ∧
    4 * (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 3 + 2 * D.R.t + 6 ≤ h0 L ∧
    Ch0k * D.R.k * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ ((9 : ℝ) / 2) ≤ h0 L ∧
    2 * (Mrt D : ℝ) ^ 3 + D.R.t + 3 ≤ h0 L ∧
    120 * (D.R.r : ℝ) * (qSig D : ℝ) ^ ((3 : ℝ) / 2) ≤ h0 L ∧
    (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 10 ≤ h0 L ∧
    16 * (qSig D : ℝ) * D.R.N D.R.m ≤ h0 L

  heightBound_ge_rank_cubic : (D.R.t : ℝ) + 2 * ((D.R.k : ℝ) + D.R.m * (D.R.t + (D.R.r + 6) + 1)) ^ 3 + 1 ≤ h0 L

  heightBound_ge_degree_power : (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) ^ Ch0N ≤ h0 L

  em_win : ∀ τ (hm : 0 < D.R.m),
    |((((D.hc τ).e ⟨D.R.m - 1, by omega⟩ - (D.hc τ).b ⟨D.R.m - 1, by omega⟩ : ℚ)) : ℝ) - h0 L / 2| ≤ 2

  nodes_gap : ∀ τ (j : ℕ) (h : j + 1 < D.R.m),
    h0 L ≤ (((D.hc τ).b ⟨j + 1, h⟩ - (D.hc τ).b ⟨j, by omega⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) ∧
    (((D.hc τ).b ⟨j + 1, h⟩ - (D.hc τ).b ⟨j, by omega⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) ≤ h0 L + 1

  e_centred : ∀ τ (j : Fin D.R.m), |((((D.hc τ).e j - (D.hc τ).b j : ℚ)) : ℝ) - h0 L / 2| ≤ 2

  sig_gaps : ∀ τ,
    (∀ (i : ℕ) (h : i + 1 < D.R.k - 1),
      gapS D Cgap < (((D.hc τ).v ⟨i, by omega⟩ - (D.hc τ).v ⟨i + 1, h⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ)) ∧
    (∀ (hk : 2 ≤ D.R.k) (hm : 0 < D.R.m),
      gapS D Cgap < (((D.hc τ).v ⟨D.R.k - 2, by omega⟩ + (D.hc τ).b ⟨0, hm⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ)) ∧
    (∀ (j : ℕ) (h : j + 1 < D.R.m),
      gapS D Cgap < (((D.hc τ).b ⟨j + 1, h⟩ - (D.hc τ).b ⟨j, by omega⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ))

  nW_ge : (4 * (D.R.f : ℝ) + 10) * D.R.r ≤ nW L CnW

  logeta : Real.log L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4)

  t_le_eta : (D.R.t : ℝ) ≤ L.etaSz

  teta : (D.R.t : ℝ) * L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n / L.sc.x

  mteta : (D.R.m : ℝ) * ((D.R.t : ℝ) + 1) * L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n

  mSeta : (D.R.m : ℝ) * (L.Sc + L.etaSz) ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n * L.sc.L

  Neta : (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * L.etaSz ≤ L.de2 * D.R.m * L.sc.w'' D.R.n

  eta2 : L.etaSz ^ 2 * D.R.r * L.sc.L ≤ L.de2 * D.R.m * L.sc.w'' D.R.n ^ 2

  rbS : (D.R.r : ℝ) * (L.sc.b' + L.Sc) * Real.log L.sc.x * lA L ≤ L.de2 * D.R.m * L.sc.b

  lstar1 : L.sc.b' + L.Sc + L.etaSz + L.sc.b / lStar L < lStar L

  lstar2 : 2 * L.etaSz + D.R.t + 8 * L.sc.b / lStar L + 7 < lStar L

  words_le : Fintype.card D.what₂.Word ≤ 2 ^ (D.R.k - 3)

  etaF_eq : D.div.etaF = CetaF * L.sc.x ^ (-CF)

  g_Y : ∃ Y : ℝ, ((D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.b) ^ 8 ≤ Y ∧
    (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 4 * (condE D : ℝ) ^ CY ≤ Y ^ ((1 : ℝ) / 4) ∧ Real.log Y ≤ CYL * L.sc.L ∧
    ∀ i, Y ≤ (D.host.g i : ℝ) ∧ (D.host.g i : ℝ) ≤ 2 * Y

  g_norm : ∀ i, ∃ π : 𝓞 D.host.E, (π : D.host.E) ∈ D.host.K ∧
    (π : D.host.E) * D.host.conj (π : D.host.E) = (D.host.g i : D.host.E)

  g_mod : ∀ i, D.host.g i % condE D = 1

  pde_mod : D.host.pde % 15 = 1 ∧ D.host.pde' % 15 = 1

  pde_split : (Ideal.primesOver (Ideal.span {(D.host.pde : ℤ)}) (𝓞 D.host.E)).ncard =
      Module.finrank ℚ D.host.E ∧
    (Ideal.primesOver (Ideal.span {(D.host.pde' : ℤ)}) (𝓞 D.host.E)).ncard = Module.finrank ℚ D.host.E

  pde_ne : ∀ i, D.host.pde ≠ D.host.g i ∧ D.host.pde' ≠ D.host.g i

  pde_log : Real.log D.host.pde ≤ L.sc.L ∧ Real.log D.host.pde' ≤ L.sc.L

  level_split : ∀ p ∈ D.host.levelPrimes, D.host.cls p = .splitK

  ccK_log : Real.log (Ideal.absNorm D.host.ccK) ≤ L.sc.L

  e_mu : ∀ j : Fin D.R.m, D.host.MuCond (fun τ => (D.eTau τ : D.R.GG)) (fun τ => (D.hc τ).e j)

  ccK_le : (Ideal.absNorm D.host.ccK : ℝ) ≤ CccK * D.host.d

namespace ParameterChoice'

variable {D : D7} {L : SizeData₂ D}

def SgWin (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (C0 : ℝ) : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (-P.CF) / C0 ^ 2 ≤ sgS D ∧ sgS D ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-P.CF)

theorem bT_ge_half (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (τ : D.what₂.Tau) (t : D.host.TT) (hm : 0 < D.R.m)
    (ht : (D.eTau τ : D.R.GG) = D.R.tau1 t) :
    (((D.hc τ).b ⟨0, hm⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) / 2 ≤ (L.bT t : ℝ) := by
  rw [P.topPlace_first_bound τ t hm ht]
  have hb := L.bT_ge t
  have hx : 1 < L.sc.x := L.sc.one_lt_alt
  have hbpos : 0 < L.sc.b' := by
    unfold Scale.b' Scale.a Scale.X' Scale.b
    have : 0 < L.sc.x := by linarith
    positivity
  have hfl : ((⌊L.sc.b' / L.sc.x⌋ : ℤ) : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.b' / L.sc.x := Int.floor_le _
  have hdiv : L.sc.b' / L.sc.x ≤ L.sc.b' := div_le_self hbpos.le hx.le
  linarith

end ParameterChoice'

theorem f_le_m_sq_n (R : Ranks) : R.f ≤ R.m ^ 2 * R.n := by
  unfold Ranks.f
  have h := R.two_le_m
  have : 4 ≤ R.m ^ 2 := by nlinarith
  nlinarith

theorem f_le_r_sq (R : Ranks) : R.f ≤ R.r ^ 2 := by
  have h1 := R.f_le_k
  have h2 := R.two_le_m
  have h3 : R.k ≤ R.r := by unfold Ranks.r; omega
  have h4 : R.r ≤ R.r ^ 2 := Nat.le_self_pow (by norm_num) _
  have h5 : R.f = 2 * R.ord := rfl
  omega

end Formalism.BasicNotions

end


[GLG_actual_data_02] exact SizesAt component bounds and conjunction
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/ElevenLe.lean#L15-L144
Range SHA256: fc4825a055442eca4a6074d7351bdfa7b9183ce5ef2a11872ec018f27a1d5807
def szlA : ℝ := (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps / ((D.R.m : ℝ) * L.de2)

def szlStar : ℝ :=
  (D.R.m : ℝ) * L.sc.b /
    ((D.R.n : ℝ) * (D.R.t : ℝ) ^ 2 * ((D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) + D.R.t) * L.sc.L ^ 2 +
      (D.R.k : ℝ) * (D.R.n : ℝ) ^ 2 * (L.Sc + D.R.t + L.sc.L))

def szCond (_L : SizeData₂ D) : ℝ := (D.host.QS : ℝ) * D.host.q * D.host.d

def SizeN : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (2 + 8 * L.sc.eps - szTh L) ≤ (D.R.n : ℝ) ∧ (D.R.n : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (2 + 8 * L.sc.eps + szTh L)

def SizeM : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (-szTh L) * Real.sqrt (L.sc.x * L.sc.delta1) ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) ∧
    (D.R.m : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ szTh L * Real.sqrt (L.sc.x * L.sc.delta1)

def SizeK : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (9 / 2 + 10 * L.sc.eps - szTh L) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) ∧
    (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (9 / 2 + 10 * L.sc.eps + szTh L)

def SizeKRel : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 5 * D.R.f * L.sc.L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps ≤ D.R.k ∧
  8 * L.CGam * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 5 * D.R.f * L.sc.L ≤ D.R.k ∧
  (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ Real.sqrt L.sc.x * D.R.n * rankBudget D.R L.sc.x L.sc.eps ∧
  Real.sqrt ((D.R.k : ℝ) / D.R.m) ≤ rankBudget D.R L.sc.x L.sc.eps ∧
  (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * L.sc.L ≤ D.R.k ∧
  (D.R.m : ℝ) * Real.sqrt L.sc.x * Real.log L.sc.x ≤ D.R.k ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 6 * L.sc.L ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.log D.R.k ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 3 * L.sc.L * Real.log D.host.rdE ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.log D.R.k ^ 2 ∧
  Real.log D.R.f ≤ Real.sqrt D.R.rS ∧
  Real.log D.R.f ^ 2 ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * L.sc.L ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * Real.log (Real.log L.sc.b) ≤ (D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * L.sc.L ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.log D.R.k ∧
  (D.R.f : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * D.R.n ∧
  (D.R.f : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.r : ℝ) ^ 2

def SizeT : Prop :=
  1 ≤ (D.R.t : ℝ) ∧ (D.R.t : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.Ct + szTh L)

def SizeCond : Prop := szCond L ^ 36 * (D.R.t : ℝ) ^ 150 ≤ Real.sqrt L.sc.b

def SizeQD : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.ez / 6) ≤ Real.log D.host.q ∧
  L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.ez / 5 - L.sc.ez / 100) ≤ Real.log D.host.q ∧
  Real.log D.host.q ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 5 + szTh L) ∧
  L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.ez / 5 - L.sc.ez / 100) ≤ Real.log D.host.d ∧
  Real.log D.host.d ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 5 + szTh L) ∧
  |L.logDerivativeAtOne| ≤ L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps ∧
  1 ≤ D.host.rdE ∧ D.host.rdE ≤ (D.host.QS : ℝ) / 2 * Real.sqrt ((D.host.q : ℝ) * D.host.d)

def SizeTT : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (-szTh L) * D.R.n * L.sc.delta1 ≤ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ∧ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ≤ szNst L

def SizeLa : Prop := L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps ≤ L.LaG ∧ L.LaG ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps + szTh L)

def SizeRho : Prop :=
  4 * szNst L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps * L.LaG / D.R.k + L.sc.x ^ szTh L * Real.log L.sc.x * L.LaG / D.R.k ≤
    L.sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ)

def SizeBot : Prop :=
  ∀ t, (L.bT t : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.b' + szNst L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps * L.LaG * L.sc.b' / D.R.k

def SizeDe2 : Prop := L.sc.x ^ (-L.sc.eps) ≤ L.de2

def SizeLw : Prop := (D.host.Lw : ℝ) ≤ Real.log L.sc.x ^ 2

def SizeEta : Prop :=
  (D.R.t : ℝ) ≤ L.etaSz ∧
  L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.b / ((D.R.t : ℝ) ^ 3 * D.R.f * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3) ∧
  Real.log L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) ∧
  (D.R.t : ℝ) * L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n / L.sc.x ∧
  (D.R.m : ℝ) * ((D.R.t : ℝ) + 1) * L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n ∧
  (D.R.m : ℝ) * (L.Sc + L.etaSz) ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n * L.sc.L ∧
  (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * L.etaSz ≤ L.de2 * D.R.m * L.sc.w'' D.R.n ∧
  L.etaSz ^ 2 * D.R.r * L.sc.L ≤ L.de2 * D.R.m * L.sc.w'' D.R.n ^ 2 ∧
  (D.R.r : ℝ) * (L.sc.b' + L.Sc) * Real.log L.sc.x * szlA L ≤ L.de2 * D.R.m * L.sc.b

def SizeNodes : Prop :=
  12 * L.sc.b / szlStar L + 2 * D.R.t + 8 ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m ∧
  (D.R.k : ℝ) * D.R.t * L.sc.L ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * (D.R.r : ℝ) ^ 19 * (D.R.f : ℝ) ^ 5 * L.sc.L ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m ∧
  4 * (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 3 + 2 * D.R.t + 6 ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * D.R.k * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ (9 / 2 : ℝ) ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m ∧
  2 * ((D.R.r : ℝ) + D.R.m * D.R.t) ^ 3 + D.R.t + 3 ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m ∧
  (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 10 ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m

def SizeLstar : Prop :=
  L.sc.b' + L.Sc + L.etaSz + L.sc.b / szlStar L < szlStar L ∧
  2 * L.etaSz + D.R.t + 8 * L.sc.b / szlStar L + 7 < szlStar L

def SizeDel : Prop :=
  (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 10 ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ) ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * D.R.m * D.R.N D.R.m ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ) ∧
  Real.log (D.s.Del : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) / 2 ∧
  (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.Sc ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * D.R.m * D.R.k * (D.s.Del : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n ∧
  4 * L.Sc ≤ L.LaG * L.sc.b' / D.R.k ∧
  4 * L.sc.x ^ szTh L * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * (D.s.Del : ℝ) ≤ L.LaG * L.sc.b' ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * D.R.m * Real.log D.host.v ≤ D.R.k

def SizeW : Prop := Real.sqrt L.sc.b ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n

def SizeBm (_L : SizeData₂ D) : Prop :=
  ∀ τ (h : 0 < D.R.m), (((D.hc τ).b ⟨D.R.m - 1, by omega⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) ≤ 2 * (((D.hc τ).b ⟨0, h⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ)

def SizeXpp : Prop :=
  ∀ j τ j' τ', 1 ≤ j → j ≤ D.R.m → 1 ≤ j' → j' ≤ D.R.m → wj D j τ ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n * wj D j' τ'

def SizeG : Prop :=
  ∀ i j : Fin 2, ((D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.b) ^ 8 ≤ (D.host.g i : ℝ) ∧ (D.host.g i : ℝ) ≤ 2 * (D.host.g j : ℝ) ∧
    Real.log (D.host.g i : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ szTh L * L.sc.L

def SizeTu : Prop := ∀ u, Real.log ((D.mem u).A.T : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ szTh L * D.R.m * L.sc.L

def SizePde : Prop :=
  Real.log (D.host.pde : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.L ∧ Real.log (D.host.pde' : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.L

def SizeWords (_L : SizeData₂ D) : Prop := @Fintype.card D.what₂.Word D.wordFin ≤ 2 ^ (D.R.k - 3)

def SizesAt : Prop :=
  SizeN L ∧ SizeM L ∧ SizeK L ∧ SizeKRel L ∧ SizeT L ∧ SizeCond L ∧ SizeQD L ∧ SizeTT L ∧
  SizeLa L ∧ SizeRho L ∧ SizeBot L ∧ SizeDe2 L ∧ SizeLw L ∧ SizeEta L ∧ SizeNodes L ∧
  SizeLstar L ∧ SizeDel L ∧ SizeW L ∧ SizeBm L ∧ SizeXpp L ∧ SizeG L ∧ SizePde L ∧
  SizeWords L ∧ 11 ≤ D.host.splitBound

theorem SizesAt.eleven_le {D : D7} {L : SizeData₂ D} (S : SizesAt L) : 11 ≤ D.host.splitBound :=
  S.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2

end Formalism.MainNotions



[GLG_actual_data_03] FamP: one actual instance/comparison/fill family and its constant order
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/ParConsts.lean#L31-L100

structure FamP (eps : ℝ) extends FamCore eps where

  params : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, ParameterChoice' (D x i) (L x i)
  PK : List ℝ
  params_K : ∀ x (hx : x1 < x) i, parConsts (params x hx i) = PK

  parT : ∀ x (hx : x1 < x) (i : Ix x), ParamsT (D x i) (L x i) (params x hx i)
  PT : List ℝ
  parT_K : ∀ x (hx : x1 < x) i, [(parT x hx i).CP, (parT x hx i).eP, (parT x hx i).Cp, (parT x hx i).Cb,
    (parT x hx i).cTO, (parT x hx i).ChT, (parT x hx i).CbN] = PT

  CTu : ℝ

  sizes : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, SizesAt (L x i)

  ties : ∀ x (i : Ix x), Ties (D x i)

  gatT : ∀ x (i : Ix x), GatTies (D x i)

  X : ∀ x (i : Ix x), DivX (D x i)

  Gx : ∀ x (i : Ix x), G.Inst (D x i) (L x i)
  Gy : ∀ x (i : Ix x), G.LatInst (D x i)
  GK : G.Consts
  GK_nonneg : GK.Nonneg

  supp_sat : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ (i : Ix x) w,
    (((D x i).what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ (L x i).sc.X'' (D x i).R.n → w ∈ (L x i).supp

  tw_supp : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ (i : Ix x) w, (D x i).what₂.muAt w ≠ (D x i).what₂.mu0 → w ∈ (L x i).supp

  fills : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ (i : Ix x) w, G.ParametersFill (Gx x i) GK w
  lats0 : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ (i : Ix x) w u,
    G.VV (Gx x i) GK (G.aL (L x i)) (G.bL (L x i)) ((D x i).what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅ →
    (D x i).what₂.places.resChar w ≠ ((D x i).mem u).A.T → (L x i).lenMN u w = 0

  latTied : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, G.LatTied (gatT x i) (Gx x i) (Gy x i)

  Csz : ℝ
  nuDef : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, DV.NuDef (D x i) (L x i)
  nprimeDef : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, DV.NprimeDef (D x i) (L x i)
  rrDef : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, DV.RRDef (D x i)
  h_eq : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ (i : Ix x) u, (D x i).div.h u = DV.hExpl6 (D x i) (L x i) (X x i) Csz u

  cX : ExtraC

  ex : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, Extra (D x i) (L x i) (gatT x i) (Gx x i) (Gy x i) GK (X x i) cX

  rho_le : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, G.rhoB (D x i) GK (L x i).sc ≤ x ^ (-2 : ℝ)

  Cr : ℝ
  one_le_Cr : 1 ≤ Cr
  drop_iff : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ (i : Ix x) p, p.Prime →
    (p ∈ (L x i).drop ↔ NoGain (Gx x i) GK Cr p ∧ ∀ u, p ≠ ((D x i).mem u).A.T)

  fixed : GK.Pr1 = 1 ∧ GK.dl1 = 5 ∧ GK.dl2 = 4 ∧ cX.Ce = 1 ∧ cX.err.c6 = 12 ∧ cX.err.dig = 3 ∧ Cr = 1 ∧
    ∀ x (hx : x1 < x) (i : Ix x), (params x hx i).CnW = 10 ∧ 12 ≤ (parT x hx i).eP

  eta_eq : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x), (L x i).etaSz = (L x i').etaSz
  Sc_shared : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x), (L x i).Sc = (L x i').Sc
  hP_eq : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x) p, (G.hP (D x i) p).val = (G.hP (D x i') p).val
  bs_eq : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x) (h t : G.HH (D x i)) (h' t' : G.HH (D x i')), h.val = h'.val → t.val = t'.val →
    ((Gx x i).bs h t).val = ((Gx x i').bs h' t').val
  off_eq : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x) j (t : G.HH (D x i)) (t' : G.HH (D x i')), t.val = t'.val →
    (Gx x i).off j (false, t) = (Gx x i').off j (false, t')
  kapI_eq : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x), (Gx x i).kapI = (Gx x i').kapI
  ep_eq : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x), (Gx x i).ep = (Gx x i').ep

def DFamP : Type 1 := ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ε < 1 / 52 → FamP ε



[GLG_actual_data_04] Consts, rG, Gp, N1, L1, L2 and exact La=K.La*N1^2*log(a)
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/IsResidueClassZero.lean#L14-L65
Range SHA256: 91b36f31287263dc47c2660a0a99a96ec338e3a33a6e19abe4f35ba8c298a68e
structure Consts where
  La : ℝ
  rho : ℝ
  kII : ℝ
  tII : ℝ
  DII : ℝ
  szII : ℝ
  kII' : ℝ
  DI : ℝ
  kappaThresholdOne : ℝ
  kappaThresholdTwo : ℝ
  kI : ℝ
  dl1 : ℝ
  dl2 : ℝ
  Pr1 : ℝ
  CN : ℝ

def Consts.Nonneg (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  0 ≤ K.La ∧ 0 ≤ K.rho ∧ 0 ≤ K.kII ∧ 0 ≤ K.tII ∧ 0 ≤ K.DII ∧ 0 ≤ K.szII ∧ 0 ≤ K.kII' ∧ 0 ≤ K.DI ∧
    0 ≤ K.kappaThresholdOne ∧ 0 ≤ K.kappaThresholdTwo ∧ 0 ≤ K.kI ∧ 0 ≤ K.dl1 ∧ 0 ≤ K.dl2 ∧ 0 ≤ K.Pr1 ∧ 0 ≤ K.CN

abbrev HH (D : D7) : Type := D.R.A

def hG (s : Scale) : ℝ := 2 + 8 * s.eps

def KG (s : Scale) : ℕ := ⌈8 * (hG s + 2) / s.eps⌉₊

def rG (s : Scale) : ℕ := KG s + 1

def N1 (s : Scale) : ℕ := orbitWindowCount (rG s) s.delta1 (hG s) s.x

def Gp (s : Scale) : ℕ := goodG' (rG s) s.delta1 (hG s) s.x

def cG {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) : ℝ := 4 * L.Sc

def Nm (D : D7) : ℝ := (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ)

def L1 (D : D7) (s : Scale) (p : ℕ) : ℕ := ⌈Real.logb p (((D.R.k : ℝ) * s.x ^ Scale.A) ^ 4)⌉₊

def L2 (D : D7) (s : Scale) : ℕ :=
  2 * sSup {l | ∃ p : ℕ, p.Prime ∧ D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass ∧ l = L1 D s p}

def Nstar (D : D7) (s : Scale) : ℝ := 2 * (D.R.n : ℝ) * s.delta1

def La (K : Consts) (s : Scale) : ℝ := K.La * (N1 s : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.log s.a

def rho (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) : ℝ :=
  4 * Nstar D s * s.x ^ s.eps * La K s / D.R.k + K.rho * (N1 s : ℝ) * La K s / D.R.k

def rhoB (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) : ℝ :=
  4 * Nstar D s * s.x ^ s.eps * La K s / D.R.k + K.rho * K.CN * Real.log s.x * La K s / D.R.k



[GLG_actual_data_05] actual fills, orbits, asks, losses, served sets, gain, mean and Sv
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/IsResidueClassZero.lean#L66-L400
Range SHA256: fa2f4f7f4fc8d965f2073f524df7dcd97868dfa8d675b1a3b76a7b0013d47dc4
def CbLarge (K : Consts) (s : Scale) : Prop := (N1 s : ℝ) ≤ K.CN * Real.log s.x

theorem rho_le_rhoB (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) (hr : 0 ≤ K.rho) (hLa : 0 ≤ La K s) (h : CbLarge K s) :
    rho D K s ≤ rhoB D K s := by
  unfold rho rhoB
  have hk : (0 : ℝ) ≤ D.R.k := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have h1 : K.rho * (N1 s : ℝ) * La K s ≤ K.rho * (K.CN * Real.log s.x) * La K s := by
    apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right _ hLa
    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h hr
  have h2 := div_le_div_of_nonneg_right h1 hk
  rw [show K.rho * K.CN * Real.log s.x * La K s = K.rho * (K.CN * Real.log s.x) * La K s by ring]
  linarith [h2]

def InBand0 (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) (p : ℕ) : Prop :=
  s.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ) ∧ (p : ℝ) < s.X' ∧ ∃ d : ℕ, 1 ≤ d ∧
    s.X' * (1 - rho D K s) ≤ (p : ℝ) ^ d ∧ (p : ℝ) ^ d ≤ s.X' * (1 + rho D K s)

def InBand (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) (p : ℕ) : Prop :=
  s.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ) ∧ (p : ℝ) < s.X' ∧ ∃ d : ℕ, 1 ≤ d ∧
    s.X' * (1 - rhoB D K s) ≤ (p : ℝ) ^ d ∧ (p : ℝ) ^ d ≤ s.X' * (1 + rhoB D K s)

def Qp (s : Scale) (p : ℕ) : ℝ := (p : ℝ) ^ (Gp s)

def gam (p : ℕ) : ℝ := (p : ℝ) / ((p : ℝ) ^ 2 - 1)

def c0num {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) : ℝ := 2 * L.sc.b' + 2 * cG L

def frobSet (D : D7) (p : ℕ) : Set (D.R.GG) :=
  {g | ∃ (P : Ideal (𝓞 D.host.E)) (_ : P.IsPrime) (_ : P.LiesOver (Ideal.span {(p : ℤ)}))
      (σ : D.host.E ≃ₐ[ℚ] D.host.E), IsArithFrobAt ℤ σ P ∧ D.host.galE σ = g}

def hP (D : D7) (p : ℕ) : HH D :=
  D.R.iotaInv (Classical.epsilon (fun g => g ∈ frobSet D p)).2

abbrev IsResidueClassZero (D : D7) (p : ℕ) : Prop := D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass

abbrev IsSg (D : D7) (p : ℕ) : Prop := D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.signal

def HEv (D : D7) (p : ℕ) : Prop := Even (addOrderOf (hP D p))

def IsCs (D : D7) (p : ℕ) : Prop := IsResidueClassZero D p ∧ Odd (addOrderOf (hP D p)) ∧ hP D p ≠ 0

theorem HEv.ne_zero {D : D7} {p : ℕ} (h : HEv D p) : hP D p ≠ 0 := by
  intro h0
  unfold HEv at h
  rw [h0, addOrderOf_zero] at h
  exact Nat.not_even_one h

theorem HEv.not_isCs {D : D7} {p : ℕ} (h : HEv D p) : ¬ IsCs D p := fun hc => Nat.not_even_iff_odd.mpr hc.2.1 h

theorem class_zero_trichotomy {D : D7} {p : ℕ} (h : IsResidueClassZero D p) : HEv D p ∨ hP D p = 0 ∨ IsCs D p := by
  by_cases he : Even (addOrderOf (hP D p))
  · exact Or.inl he
  · by_cases h0 : hP D p = 0
    · exact Or.inr (Or.inl h0)
    · exact Or.inr (Or.inr ⟨h, Nat.not_even_iff_odd.mp he, h0⟩)

theorem IsCs.three_le {D : D7} {p : ℕ} (h : IsCs D p) : 3 ≤ addOrderOf (hP D p) := by
  obtain ⟨-, hodd, hne⟩ := h
  have h1 : addOrderOf (hP D p) ≠ 1 := fun h1 => hne (by
    have h2 := addOrderOf_nsmul_eq_zero (hP D p)
    rwa [h1, one_nsmul] at h2)
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := hodd
  omega

def partner (D : D7) (p : ℕ) (t : HH D) (e : ℕ) : Bool × HH D :=
  (decide (IsSg D p ∧ Odd e), t + e • hP D p)

def IsTopPl (D : D7) (z : Bool × HH D) : Prop := z.1 = false ∧ z.2 ∈ D.host.TT

def IsThickPl (D : D7) (z : Bool × HH D) : Prop :=
  z.1 = true ∧ z.2 ∈ D.host.T2.image (fun s : ℕ => D.R.dig s + D.host.u0)

def IsThinPl (D : D7) (z : Bool × HH D) : Prop := ¬ IsTopPl D z ∧ ¬ IsThickPl D z

structure Inst (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) where
  bs : HH D → HH D → HH D
  bs_orb : ∀ h t, ∃ i : ℕ, bs h t + i • h = t
  bs_step : ∀ h t, bs h (t + h) = bs h t
  off : ℕ → Bool × HH D → ℝ
  off_top : ∀ j z, IsTopPl D z → L.sc.b' / L.sc.x ≤ off j z ∧ off j z ≤ L.sc.b' / L.sc.x + L.etaSz
  off_thin : ∀ j z, IsThinPl D z → L.sc.w'' D.R.n ≤ off j z ∧ off j z ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz
  off_thick : ∀ j z, IsThickPl D z → L.sc.b ≤ off j z ∧ off j z ≤ L.sc.b + L.etaSz + 1
  kapI : ℕ → (n' : ℕ) → Finset (Fin n') → (Fin n' → ℤ) → (Fin n' → ℤ) → ℝ
  ep : ℕ → ℕ

variable {D : D7} {L : SizeData₂ D}

def fill (X : Inst D L) (b : HH D → ℤ) (j : ℕ) (z : Bool × HH D) : ℝ :=
  (if z.1 = false ∧ z.2 ∈ D.host.TT then (b z.2 : ℝ) else 0) + X.off j z

def kaJ (D : D7) (j : ℕ) : ℝ := ((D.R.k : ℝ) + (2 * (j : ℝ) - 1) * ((D.R.t : ℝ) + 1)) / 2

def fillG (X : Inst D L) (b : HH D → ℤ) (j : ℕ) (z : Bool × HH D) : ℝ := fill X b j z - kaJ D j

def stepP (D : D7) (p : ℕ) : HH D := if IsSg D p then 2 • hP D p else hP D p

def nP (D : D7) (p : ℕ) : ℕ :=
  if IsSg D p then 2 * addOrderOf (2 • hP D p) else addOrderOf (hP D p)

def reps (X : Inst D L) (p : ℕ) : Finset (HH D) := Finset.univ.image (X.bs (stepP D p))

def topsOn (X : Inst D L) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : Finset (HH D) :=
  D.host.TT.filter (fun t => X.bs (stepP D p) t = t0)

def Nv (X : Inst D L) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : ℕ := (topsOn X p t0).card

def topsPos (D : D7) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : Finset (Fin (addOrderOf (hP D p))) :=
  Finset.univ.filter (fun i => t0 + (i : ℕ) • hP D p ∈ D.host.TT)

def wPos (D : D7) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) (a : HH D → ℤ) : Fin (addOrderOf (hP D p)) → ℤ :=
  fun i => a (t0 + (i : ℕ) • hP D p)

def IsV (X : Inst D L) (p : ℕ) (t : HH D) : Prop :=
  ∃ i : ℕ, i < nP D p ∧ Even i ∧ X.bs (stepP D p) t + i • hP D p = t

def Good (_X : Inst D L) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : Prop :=
  IsGoodOrbit (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0) (L2 D L.sc) (KG L.sc) (rG L.sc) L.sc.delta1
    (hG L.sc) L.sc.x

def NplusF (s : Scale) (n' : ℕ) : ℝ := 40 * (n' : ℝ) * s.delta1

def DIIf (K : Consts) (L : SizeData₂ D) (p n' : ℕ) : ℕ :=
  ⌈K.DII * NplusF L.sc n' * ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 3 + (rG L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2) *
      Real.logb p L.sc.a + 2 * (rG L.sc : ℝ) * (n' : ℝ) * cG L +
      (rG L.sc : ℝ) * NplusF L.sc n' * c0num L⌉₊

def DIf (K : Consts) (L : SizeData₂ D) (p n' : ℕ) : ℕ :=
  ⌈K.DI * NplusF L.sc n' * (L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ^ 2 * ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3) *
      Real.logb p L.sc.a⌉₊

def tIIf (K : Consts) (L : SizeData₂ D) (p n' N : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  K.tII * (rG L.sc : ℝ) * (N : ℝ) * (La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) +
    L.sc.a * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(L1 D L.sc p : ℝ)) + c0num L + (D.R.k : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p) +
    2 * (rG L.sc : ℝ) * (n' : ℝ) * cG L

def kapIIf (K : Consts) (L : SizeData₂ D) (N : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  min 1 (K.kII * (N : ℝ) * (rG L.sc : ℝ) ^ 2 * (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-Scale.A))

open Classical in

def dMinus {n' : ℕ} (TT : Finset (Fin n')) (y : Fin n') : ℕ :=
  if h : ∃ d : ℕ, 0 < d ∧ d ≤ n' ∧ posAdd y (n' - d) ∈ TT ∧ IsTypeV (posAdd y (n' - d))
  then Nat.find h else 0

def c6f (K : Consts) (L : SizeData₂ D) (p n' : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  8 * ((DIf K L p n' : ℝ) + 1) + K.kappaThresholdTwo * NplusF L.sc n' * (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.logb p L.sc.a +
    4 * NplusF L.sc n' * cG L + 2 * (n' : ℝ) * cG L

open Classical in

def tIf (K : Consts) (L : SizeData₂ D) (p n' : ℕ) (TT : Finset (Fin n')) (a b : Fin n' → ℤ) : ℝ :=
  (∑ y ∈ TT.filter (fun y => ¬ IsTypeV y),
      (if dMinus TT y = 0 then 0 else
        min (b y : ℝ) ((a (posAdd y (n' - dMinus TT y)) : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ dMinus TT y))) +
    K.kappaThresholdOne * (TT.card : ℝ) * (N1 L.sc : ℝ) *
      (L.sc.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2 + La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k)) +
    K.kappaThresholdTwo * (TT.card : ℝ) * (Qp L.sc p * c6f K L p n' +
      (D.R.k : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p +
      (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.logb p L.sc.a + cG L) + 2 * (n' : ℝ) * cG L

def kapLoc (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (p n' : ℕ) (TT : Finset (Fin n')) (a b : Fin n' → ℤ) : ℝ :=
  if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then kapIIf K L TT.card else X.kapI p n' TT a b

def al (L : SizeData₂ D) (a : HH D → ℤ) (s : HH D) : ℝ := (a s : ℝ) - 2 * cG L

def capU (X : Inst D L) (b : HH D → ℤ) (p j : ℕ) (s : HH D) (e : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  fillG X b j (partner D p s e) + cG L

def IsDist (D : D7) (p : ℕ) (s s' : HH D) (e : ℕ) : Prop :=
  0 < e ∧ partner D p s e = (false, s') ∧ ∀ e', 0 < e' → e' < e → partner D p s e' ≠ (false, s')

def Excl (D : D7) (p : ℕ) (s : HH D) (e : ℕ) : Prop :=
  Odd e ∧ ∃ s' ∈ D.host.TT, s' ≠ s ∧ IsDist D p s s' e

def dl (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : ℝ :=
  K.dl1 * (nP D p : ℝ) * Nm D ^ 2 * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) +
    K.dl2 * (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) ^ 2

open Classical in

def g0 (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p j : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : ℝ :=
  (∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, ∑' e : ℕ,
    if Odd e ∧ ¬ Excl D p s e then max 0 (al L a s / (p : ℝ) ^ e - capU X b p j s e) else 0) -
    dl X K p t0

open Classical in

def gU (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p j : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : ℝ :=
  gam p * (∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, al L a s) -
    (∑ s ∈ (topsOn X p t0).filter (fun s => ¬ IsV X p s), ∑' e : ℕ,
      if Odd e then min (al L a s / (p : ℝ) ^ e) (capU X b p j s e) else 0) -
    dl X K p t0

open Classical in

def Pr (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (b : HH D → ℤ) (p j : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : ℝ :=
  if L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ) then
    (∑ s ∈ (topsOn X p t0).filter (fun s => ¬ IsV X p s), (fillG X b j (false, s) - (b s : ℝ))) +
      K.Pr1 * (nP D p : ℝ) * (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D)
  else (rG L.sc : ℝ) * ((Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (2 * L.sc.b' + cG L) +
      (nP D p : ℝ) * (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D))

def Dp (K : Consts) (L : SizeData₂ D) (p : ℕ) : ℕ :=
  if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then DIIf K L p (nP D p) else DIf K L p (nP D p)

def tv (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : ℝ :=
  if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then tIIf K L p (nP D p) (Nv X p t0)
  else tIf K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0) (wPos D p t0 a) (wPos D p t0 b)

def kapv (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : ℝ :=
  kapLoc X K p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0) (wPos D p t0 a) (wPos D p t0 b)

def Cand (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) (p : ℕ) : Prop :=
  p.Prime ∧ (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' ∧ IsResidueClassZero D p ∧ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) ≤ (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) ∧
    (L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ) → ¬ InBand D K L.sc p)

open Classical in

def goodReps (X : Inst D L) (p : ℕ) : Finset (HH D) := (reps X p).filter (fun t0 => Good X p t0)

def Sig (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  (D.R.m : ℝ) * ∑ t0 ∈ goodReps X p, kapv X K a b p t0

open Classical in

def VV (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) : Finset (HH D) :=
  if Cand D L K p ∧ Sig X K a b p ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-L.sc.eps) then goodReps X p else ∅

def ServedOrbitsContained (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) : Prop :=
  ∀ p, Cand D L K p → Sig X K a b p ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-L.sc.eps) → goodReps X p ⊆ VV X K a b p

theorem served_orbits_in_set (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) : ServedOrbitsContained X K a b := by
  intro p hc hs
  have hv : VV X K a b p = goodReps X p := by
    unfold VV
    exact if_pos ⟨hc, hs⟩
  exact hv ▸ Finset.Subset.refl (goodReps X p)

def winA (D : D7) (s : Scale) : Finset ℤ :=
  Finset.Icc ⌈s.a⌉ ⌊s.a + s.a / ((D.R.k : ℝ) * s.x ^ (Scale.A / 2))⌋

def winB (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) : Finset ℤ :=
  Finset.Icc ⌈s.b'⌉ ⌊s.b' + Nstar D s * s.x ^ s.eps * La K s * s.b' / D.R.k⌋

open Classical in

def box (D : D7) (W : Finset ℤ) : Finset (HH D → ℤ) :=
  Fintype.piFinset (fun t => if t ∈ D.host.TT then W else {0})

def meanW (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) (F : (HH D → ℤ) → (HH D → ℤ) → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (∑ a ∈ box D (winA D s), ∑ b ∈ box D (winB D K s), F a b) /
    (((box D (winA D s)).card : ℝ) * ((box D (winB D K s)).card : ℝ))

open Classical in

def boxLoc (n' : ℕ) (TT : Finset (Fin n')) (W : Finset ℤ) : Finset (Fin n' → ℤ) :=
  Fintype.piFinset (fun i => if i ∈ TT then W else {0})

def meanLoc (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) (n' : ℕ) (TT : Finset (Fin n'))
    (F : (Fin n' → ℤ) → (Fin n' → ℤ) → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (∑ a ∈ boxLoc n' TT (winA D s), ∑ b ∈ boxLoc n' TT (winB D K s), F a b) /
    (((boxLoc n' TT (winA D s)).card : ℝ) * ((boxLoc n' TT (winB D K s)).card : ℝ))

def MeanKappaBound (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ p, Cand D L K p → ∀ t0 ∈ goodReps X p,
    meanLoc D K L.sc (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0)
      (fun a b => kapLoc X K p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0) a b) ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-12 : ℝ) ∧
    ((p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k → ∀ a b, kapv X K a b p t0 ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-12 : ℝ))

def Sv (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : ℝ :=
  (⌈∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, max 0 (gU X K a b p j t0 - Pr X K b p j t0 - max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0)⌉ : ℤ)

open Classical in

def tauP (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  if VV X K a b p = ∅ then 0 else
    (X.ep p : ℝ) + ∑ t0 ∈ VV X K a b p,
      (D.R.m : ℝ) * ((⌈tv X K a b p t0⌉₊ : ℝ) + (Dp K L p : ℝ) + 1)

open Classical in

def ioP (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  if h : (VV X K a b p).Nonempty then
    2 * Sig X K a b p * (VV X K a b p).sup' h (fun t0 => Sv X K a b p t0)
  else 0

open Classical in

def gainP (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p j : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  if (IsResidueClassZero D p ∧ hP D p = 0) ∨ L.sc.X'' D.R.n < (p : ℝ) then 0 else
    (∑ t0 ∈ VV X K a b p, max (gU X K a b p j t0 - Pr X K b p j t0) (max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0)) +
    ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p \ VV X K a b p, max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0

open Classical in

def TTP (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) : Finset (HH D) :=
  if IsSg D p ∨ L.sc.X' ≤ (p : ℝ) then D.host.TT
  else D.host.TT.filter (fun t => X.bs (stepP D p) t ∈ VV X K a b p)

open Classical in

def askG (X : Inst D L) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p j : ℕ) (s : HH D) : ℝ :=
  ∑' e : ℕ, if Odd e then max 0 ((a s : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ e - fillG X b j (partner D p s e)) else 0

open Classical in

def askP (X : Inst D L) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p j : ℕ) (s : HH D) : ℝ :=
  ∑' e : ℕ, if Odd e then max 0 ((a s : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ e - fill X b j (partner D p s e)) else 0

open Classical in

def lo (K : Consts) (L : SizeData₂ D) (C bo : ℝ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  let s := L.sc
  let cp : ℝ := C * (cG L + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * s.a)
  let pr : ℝ := if IsResidueClassZero D p then
      (if (p : ℝ) < s.p0 D.R.k then C * (rG s : ℝ) * s.b' * Real.log s.x
       else if (p : ℝ) < s.X' then
         s.b' / s.x + L.etaSz + C * s.w'' D.R.n * Real.log s.x / s.delta1 + bo
       else 0) else 0
  let orr : ℝ := if IsResidueClassZero D p ∧ (p : ℝ) < s.X' then
      C * Real.log s.x * (La K s * s.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) +
        (if (p : ℝ) < s.p0 D.R.k then s.a * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(L1 D s p : ℝ)) else s.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2))
      else 0
  cp + pr + orr

def fl (D : D7) (s : Scale) (C : ℝ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  C * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Nm D ^ 2 * Real.logb p s.a + C * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.logb p s.a ^ 2

def aL (L : SizeData₂ D) (t : HH D) : ℤ := if h : t ∈ D.host.TT then L.aT ⟨t, h⟩ else 0

def bL (L : SizeData₂ D) (t : HH D) : ℤ := if h : t ∈ D.host.TT then L.bT ⟨t, h⟩ else 0

def plGG (D : D7) (z : Bool × HH D) : D.R.GG := if z.1 then D.R.tau2 z.2 else D.R.tau1 z.2



[GLG_star_contract_01] InWin, exact askP/askG gain predicates, RequiresAll, StarAt, StarAll and AdmFam
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/LogarithmicSums/Conclusion1OfGainBoundAll.lean#L99-L160
Range SHA256: baf7ff373ca5ec36c91457d5c52b67d8490e3bf22a8e8ac33dc832f601e06e28
def InWin (L : SizeData₂ D) (a b : HH D → ℤ) : Prop :=
  ∀ t ∈ D.host.TT, L.sc.a ≤ (a t : ℝ) ∧
    (a t : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.a + L.sc.a / ((D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ∧ L.sc.b' ≤ (b t : ℝ)

def GainBoundAtPairUniform (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (C bo : ℝ) (a b : HH D → ℤ) : Prop :=
  ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → (IsResidueClassZero D p ∨ IsSg D p) → (p : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n →
    (∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, 0 ≤ gainP X K a b p j ∧
      gainP X K a b p j ≤ 4 * (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * L.sc.a / (p : ℝ)) ∧
    (∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∑ s ∈ TTP X K a b p,
        (askP X a b p j s - lo K L C bo p)) - (D.R.m : ℝ) * fl D L.sc C p ≤
      (∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, gainP X K a b p j) - tauP X K a b p

def GainBoundAllUniform (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (C bo : ℝ) : Prop := ∀ a b, InWin L a b → GainBoundAtPairUniform X K C bo a b

def GainBoundAt (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (C bo : ℝ) (a b : HH D → ℤ) : Prop :=
  ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → (IsResidueClassZero D p ∨ IsSg D p) → (p : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n →
    (∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, 0 ≤ gainP X K a b p j ∧
      gainP X K a b p j ≤ 4 * (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * L.sc.a / (p : ℝ)) ∧
    (∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∑ s ∈ TTP X K a b p,
        (askG X a b p j s - lo K L C bo p)) - (D.R.m : ℝ) * fl D L.sc C p ≤
      (∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, gainP X K a b p j) - tauP X K a b p

def GainBoundAll (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (C bo : ℝ) : Prop := ∀ a b, InWin L a b → GainBoundAt X K C bo a b

def RequiresAll (X : Inst D L) : Prop :=
  ∀ a b : HH D → ℤ, (∀ s ∈ D.host.TT, (a s : ℝ) ≤ 2 * L.sc.a) → (∀ s ∈ D.host.TT, (0 : ℝ) ≤ (b s : ℝ)) →
    ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∀ s ∈ D.host.TT,
    askP X a b p j s ≤ askG X a b p j s ∧
    askG X a b p j s ≤ askP X a b p j s + Nm D * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a)

def StarAt (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (C bo : ℝ) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p j : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : Prop :=
  ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, (askP X a b p j s - lo K L C bo p) ≤
    gU X K a b p j t0 + dl X K p t0 - Pr X K b p j t0 - (⌈tv X K a b p t0⌉₊ : ℝ) - (Dp K L p : ℝ) - 1

def StarAll (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (C bo : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∀ a b, InWin L a b → ∀ p, Cand D L K p → ∀ t0 ∈ goodReps X p, ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, StarAt X K C bo a b p j t0

open Classical in

def candF (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) : Finset ℕ :=
  (Finset.range ⌈L.sc.X'⌉₊).filter (fun p => Cand D L K p)

open Classical in

def ZZ (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) : ℝ :=
  ∑ p ∈ candF D L K, Real.log p / p *
    ((if L.sc.x ^ (-L.sc.eps) < Sig X K a b p then 1 else 0) + Sig X K a b p)

def MeanEx (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  (∀ a b p, Cand D L K p → 0 ≤ Sig X K a b p) ∧
  ∃ a ∈ box D (winA D L.sc), ∃ b ∈ box D (winB D K L.sc), ZZ X K a b ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-9 : ℝ) / 4

theorem inWin_sizeData (L : SizeData₂ D) : InWin L (aL L) (bL L) := by
  intro t ht
  simp only [aL, bL, dif_pos ht]
  exact ⟨(L.aT_win _).1, (L.aT_win _).2, L.bT_ge _⟩

def AdmFam {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (K : Consts) (X : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), Inst (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i))
    (Y : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), LatInst (Fm.D x i)) (C31 Ce Cm : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∀ δ > 0, ∃ x0 : ℝ, 1 < x0 ∧ ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x,
    admitted (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (X x i) (Y x i) K ⟨C31, Ce, Cm, δ⟩

structure NumStar (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) (C31 C bo : ℝ) : Prop where

  hL2 : L2 D L.sc < goodU L.sc.delta1

  h3G : ((3 * Gp L.sc : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4)
  hlx : 1 ≤ Real.log L.sc.x

  hGu : C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 * (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D) ≤ L.sc.b'

  hc0 : c0num L ≤ 3 * L.sc.b'

  hkQ : ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k → (D.R.k : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p ≤ L.sc.b'

  hDII : ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime →
    K.DII * ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 3 + (rG L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2) * Real.logb p L.sc.a ≤ L.sc.b'
  hC3 : 3 ≤ C

  hCt : K.tII * (rG L.sc : ℝ) ≤ C
  hCD : 2 * (3 : ℝ) + 4 * K.tII + 160 * C31 + 7 ≤ C
  hCP : K.Pr1 * C31 ≤ C

  hN1 : K.kappaThresholdOne * (N1 L.sc : ℝ) ≤ C * Real.log L.sc.x

  hbo : ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ) → (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' → ∀ n' : ℕ, n' ≤ D.R.n →
    K.Pr1 * (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) * (L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D) +
      K.kappaThresholdTwo * (Qp L.sc p * c6f K L p n' +
        (D.R.k : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p + (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.logb p L.sc.a + cG L) +
      2 * (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) * cG L +
      40 * C31 * Real.log L.sc.x * (K.DI * (L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ^ 2 *
        ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3) * Real.logb p L.sc.a) + 3 ≤ bo

def RestStar {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  ∀ K : Consts, K.Nonneg → ∀ C31 Ce Cm : ℝ, 0 < C31 → ∃ C0 : ℝ, 0 ≤ C0 ∧ ∀ C ≥ C0,
    ∀ (X : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), Inst (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i)) (Y : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), LatInst (Fm.D x i)),

[GLG_orbit_and_shift_01] finite odd-exponent shift inequalities and their exact log loss
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/LogarithmicSums/TsumTruncatedShiftLe.lean#L1-L168
Range SHA256: 83a116e0903673a0389c94d89b0425a9c520640a357f75d916acff055492d381
import Formalism.SharpRestriction.GrowthBounds.LogarithmicSums.Conclusion1OfGainBoundAll

noncomputable section

open scoped BigOperators

namespace Formalism.MainNotions.AuxBlock8.Pf

theorem summable_geo (p y : ℝ) (hp : 2 ≤ p) : Summable (fun e : ℕ => y / p ^ e) := by
  have h : Summable (fun e : ℕ => y * (1 / p) ^ e) :=
    (summable_geometric_of_lt_one (by positivity) (by rw [div_lt_one (by linarith)]; linarith)).mul_left y
  refine h.congr (fun e => ?_)
  rw [one_div, inv_pow, div_eq_mul_inv]

theorem summable_if_of_le (p y : ℝ) (hp : 2 ≤ p) (f : ℕ → ℝ) (Q : ℕ → Prop) [DecidablePred Q]
    (h0 : ∀ e, 0 ≤ f e) (h1 : ∀ e, f e ≤ y / p ^ e) : Summable (fun e => if Q e then f e else 0) := by
  refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun e => ?_) (fun e => ?_) (summable_geo p y hp)
  · split_ifs
    · exact h0 e
    · exact le_rfl
  · split_ifs
    · exact h1 e
    · exact le_trans (h0 e) (h1 e)

theorem summable_odd (p y : ℝ) (hp : 2 ≤ p) (Q : ℕ → Prop) [DecidablePred Q] :
    Summable (fun e : ℕ => if Q e then y / p ^ e else 0) := by
  have h := (summable_geo p |y| hp)
  refine Summable.of_norm_bounded h (fun e => ?_)
  have hpe : 0 < p ^ e := by positivity
  split_ifs
  · rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_div, abs_of_pos hpe]
  · rw [norm_zero]; positivity

theorem tsum_odd (p y : ℝ) (hp : 2 ≤ p) (Q : ℕ → Prop) [DecidablePred Q] (hQ : ∀ e, Q e ↔ Odd e) :
    ∑' e : ℕ, (if Q e then y / p ^ e else 0) = p / (p ^ 2 - 1) * y := by
  set f : ℕ → ℝ := fun e => if Q e then y / p ^ e else 0 with hf
  have he : ∀ k : ℕ, f (2 * k) = 0 := by
    intro k
    rw [hf]
    exact if_neg (fun h => by have := (hQ _).1 h; exact (Nat.not_odd_iff_even.2 (even_two_mul k)) this)
  have ho : ∀ k : ℕ, f (2 * k + 1) = y / p * (1 / p ^ 2) ^ k := by
    intro k
    rw [hf]
    have : Q (2 * k + 1) := (hQ _).2 (odd_two_mul_add_one k)
    simp only [if_pos this]
    rw [pow_succ, pow_mul, one_div, inv_pow]
    field_simp
  have hp2 : (1 : ℝ) / p ^ 2 < 1 := by
    rw [div_lt_one (by positivity)]
    nlinarith
  have hs0 : Summable (fun k : ℕ => f (2 * k)) := by
    simp only [he]; exact summable_zero
  have hs1 : Summable (fun k : ℕ => f (2 * k + 1)) := by
    simp only [ho]
    exact (summable_geometric_of_lt_one (by positivity) hp2).mul_left _
  rw [← tsum_even_add_odd hs0 hs1]
  simp only [he, ho, tsum_zero, zero_add]
  rw [tsum_mul_left, tsum_geometric_of_lt_one (by positivity) hp2]
  have h1 : p ^ 2 - 1 ≠ 0 := by nlinarith
  have h2 : p ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have h3 : (1 : ℝ) - 1 / p ^ 2 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp

theorem max_add_min_eq (u w : ℝ) : max 0 (u - w) + min u w = u := by
  rcases le_total u w with h | h
  · rw [max_eq_left (by linarith), min_eq_left h]; ring
  · rw [max_eq_right (by linarith), min_eq_right h]; ring

theorem tsum_pos_add_min (p y : ℝ) (hp : 2 ≤ p) (hy : 0 ≤ y) (w : ℕ → ℝ) (hw : ∀ e, 0 ≤ w e)
    (Q : ℕ → Prop) [DecidablePred Q] (hQ : ∀ e, Q e ↔ Odd e) :
    ∑' e : ℕ, (if Q e then max 0 (y / p ^ e - w e) else 0) +
      ∑' e : ℕ, (if Q e then min (y / p ^ e) (w e) else 0) = p / (p ^ 2 - 1) * y := by
  have hpe : ∀ e : ℕ, 0 ≤ y / p ^ e := fun e => div_nonneg hy (by positivity)
  have s1 : Summable (fun e : ℕ => if Q e then max 0 (y / p ^ e - w e) else 0) :=
    summable_if_of_le p y hp _ Q (fun e => le_max_left _ _)
      (fun e => max_le (hpe e) (by have := hw e; linarith))
  have s2 : Summable (fun e : ℕ => if Q e then min (y / p ^ e) (w e) else 0) :=
    summable_if_of_le p y hp _ Q (fun e => le_min (hpe e) (hw e)) (fun e => min_le_left _ _)
  rw [← s1.tsum_add s2, ← tsum_odd p y hp Q hQ]
  congr 1
  funext e
  split_ifs
  · exact max_add_min_eq _ _
  · simp

theorem tsum_pos_le (p y : ℝ) (hp : 2 ≤ p) (hy : 0 ≤ y) (w : ℕ → ℝ) (hw : ∀ e, 0 ≤ w e)
    (Q : ℕ → Prop) [DecidablePred Q] (hQ : ∀ e, Q e → Odd e) :
    ∑' e : ℕ, (if Q e then max 0 (y / p ^ e - w e) else 0) ≤ p / (p ^ 2 - 1) * y := by
  classical
  have hpe : ∀ e : ℕ, 0 ≤ y / p ^ e := fun e => div_nonneg hy (by positivity)
  rw [← tsum_odd p y hp (fun e => Odd e) (fun e => Iff.rfl)]
  refine Summable.tsum_le_tsum (fun e => ?_)
    (summable_if_of_le p y hp _ Q (fun e => le_max_left _ _)
      (fun e => max_le (hpe e) (by have := hw e; linarith))) (summable_odd p y hp _)
  by_cases h : Q e
  · rw [if_pos h, if_pos (hQ e h)]
    exact max_le (hpe e) (by have := hw e; linarith)
  · rw [if_neg h]
    split_ifs
    · exact hpe e
    · exact le_rfl

theorem tsum_truncated_shift_le (p y c A : ℝ) (hp : 2 ≤ p) (hc : 0 ≤ c) (hy : 2 * c ≤ y) (hyA : y ≤ A) (hA : 1 ≤ A)
    (w w' : ℕ → ℝ) (hw : ∀ e, 1 ≤ w e) (hw0 : ∀ e, 0 ≤ w' e) (hw' : ∀ e, w' e ≤ w e + c)
    (Q : ℕ → Prop) [DecidablePred Q] (hQ : ∀ e, Q e → Odd e) :
    ∑' e : ℕ, (if Q e then max 0 (y / p ^ e - w e) else 0) - 3 * c * Real.logb p A ≤
      ∑' e : ℕ, (if Q e then max 0 ((y - 2 * c) / p ^ e - w' e) else 0) := by
  classical
  have hy0 : 0 ≤ y := by linarith
  set M : ℕ := ⌊Real.logb p A⌋₊ with hM
  have hlog : 0 ≤ Real.logb p A := Real.logb_nonneg (by linarith) hA
  have hpe : ∀ e : ℕ, 0 ≤ y / p ^ e := fun e => div_nonneg hy0 (by positivity)
  have hpe' : ∀ e : ℕ, 0 ≤ (y - 2 * c) / p ^ e := fun e => div_nonneg (by linarith) (by positivity)
  have sF : Summable (fun e : ℕ => if Q e then max 0 (y / p ^ e - w e) else 0) :=
    summable_if_of_le p y hp _ Q (fun e => le_max_left _ _)
      (fun e => max_le (hpe e) (by have := hw e; linarith))
  have sG : Summable (fun e : ℕ => if Q e then max 0 ((y - 2 * c) / p ^ e - w' e) else 0) :=
    summable_if_of_le p (y - 2 * c) hp _ Q (fun e => le_max_left _ _)
      (fun e => max_le (hpe' e) (by have := hw0 e; linarith))
  have sH : Summable (fun e : ℕ => if e ∈ Finset.Icc 1 M then 3 * c else 0) :=
    summable_of_ne_finset_zero (s := Finset.Icc 1 M) (fun e he => if_neg he)
  have hH : ∑' e : ℕ, (if e ∈ Finset.Icc 1 M then 3 * c else 0) ≤ 3 * c * Real.logb p A := by
    rw [tsum_eq_sum (s := Finset.Icc 1 M) (fun e he => if_neg he)]
    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun e he => if_pos he), Finset.sum_const, Nat.card_Icc, nsmul_eq_mul]
    have h1 : ((M + 1 - 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ Real.logb p A := by
      rw [Nat.add_sub_cancel]
      exact Nat.floor_le hlog
    nlinarith
  have hterm : ∀ e : ℕ, (if Q e then max 0 (y / p ^ e - w e) else 0) ≤
      (if Q e then max 0 ((y - 2 * c) / p ^ e - w' e) else 0) +
        (if e ∈ Finset.Icc 1 M then 3 * c else 0) := by
    intro e
    have h3c : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (if e ∈ Finset.Icc 1 M then 3 * c else 0) := by
      split_ifs
      · linarith
      · exact le_rfl
    by_cases h : Q e
    · rw [if_pos h, if_pos h]
      by_cases hpos : y / p ^ e ≤ w e
      · rw [max_eq_left (by linarith)]
        exact add_nonneg (le_max_left _ _) h3c
      · push_neg at hpos
        have hpp : (0 : ℝ) < p ^ e := by positivity
        have h1 : 1 < y / p ^ e := lt_of_le_of_lt (hw e) hpos
        have h2 : p ^ e < y := by rwa [lt_div_iff₀ hpp, one_mul] at h1
        have h3 : (e : ℝ) ≤ Real.logb p A := by
          rw [Real.le_logb_iff_rpow_le (by linarith) (by linarith), Real.rpow_natCast]
          linarith
        have h4 : e ∈ Finset.Icc 1 M := by
          rw [Finset.mem_Icc]
          refine ⟨?_, Nat.le_floor h3⟩
          obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := hQ e h
          omega
        rw [if_pos h4, max_eq_right (by linarith)]
        have h5 : 2 * c / p ^ e ≤ 2 * c := div_le_self (by linarith) (one_le_pow₀ (by linarith))
        have h6 : (y - 2 * c) / p ^ e = y / p ^ e - 2 * c / p ^ e := sub_div _ _ _
        have h7 := hw' e
        have h8 : (y - 2 * c) / p ^ e - w' e ≤ max 0 ((y - 2 * c) / p ^ e - w' e) := le_max_right _ _
        linarith
    · rw [if_neg h, if_neg h]
      linarith
  have := Summable.tsum_le_tsum hterm sF (sG.add sH)
  rw [sG.tsum_add sH] at this
  linarith

end Formalism.MainNotions.AuxBlock8.Pf

end


[GLG_orbit_and_shift_02] actual cyclic orbit dictionary and injective predecessor kink inequality

def phi (D : D7) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) (i : Fin (addOrderOf (hP D p))) : HH D := t0 + (i : ℕ) • hP D p

theorem phi_inj (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : Function.Injective (phi D p t0) := by
  intro i j hij
  have h1 : (i : ℕ) • hP D p = (j : ℕ) • hP D p := add_left_cancel hij
  exact Fin.ext (nsmul_injOn_Iio_addOrderOf (Set.mem_Iio.2 i.2) (Set.mem_Iio.2 j.2) h1)

theorem phi_posAdd (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) (i : Fin (addOrderOf (hP D p))) (e : ℕ) :
    phi D p t0 (posAdd i e) = phi D p t0 i + e • hP D p := by
  unfold phi posAdd
  simp only
  rw [mod_addOrderOf_nsmul, add_nsmul, add_assoc]

theorem bs_phi (X : Inst D L) {p : ℕ} (hc : IsResidueClassZero D p) {t0 : HH D} (h0 : X.bs (stepP D p) t0 = t0)
    (i : Fin (addOrderOf (hP D p))) : X.bs (stepP D p) (phi D p t0 i) = t0 := by
  rw [frobenius_step_large_order hc] at h0 ⊢
  unfold phi
  rw [bs_add, h0]

theorem reps_fix (X : Inst D L) {p : ℕ} {t0 : HH D} (h : t0 ∈ reps X p) : X.bs (stepP D p) t0 = t0 := by
  unfold reps at h
  obtain ⟨t, -, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 h
  exact bs_idem X _ t

theorem topsOn_eq_image (X : Inst D L) {p : ℕ} (hc : IsResidueClassZero D p) {t0 : HH D} (h0 : X.bs (stepP D p) t0 = t0) :
    topsOn X p t0 = (topsPos D p t0).image (phi D p t0) := by
  classical
  ext t
  unfold topsOn topsPos
  simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_image, Finset.mem_univ, true_and]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨ht, hb⟩
    obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := X.bs_orb (stepP D p) t
    rw [hb, frobenius_step_large_order hc] at hi
    have hpos : 0 < addOrderOf (hP D p) := addOrderOf_pos _
    refine ⟨⟨i % addOrderOf (hP D p), Nat.mod_lt _ hpos⟩, ?_, ?_⟩
    · simp only
      rw [mod_addOrderOf_nsmul, hi]
      exact ht
    · unfold phi
      simp only
      rw [mod_addOrderOf_nsmul, hi]
  · rintro ⟨i, hi, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨hi, bs_phi X hc h0 i⟩

theorem sum_topsOn (X : Inst D L) {p : ℕ} (hc : IsResidueClassZero D p) {t0 : HH D} (h0 : X.bs (stepP D p) t0 = t0)
    (F : HH D → ℝ) : ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, F s = ∑ i ∈ topsPos D p t0, F (phi D p t0 i) := by
  classical
  rw [topsOn_eq_image X hc h0, Finset.sum_image (fun x _ y _ h => phi_inj p t0 h)]

theorem Nv_eq_card (X : Inst D L) {p : ℕ} (hc : IsResidueClassZero D p) {t0 : HH D} (h0 : X.bs (stepP D p) t0 = t0) :
    Nv X p t0 = (topsPos D p t0).card := by
  classical
  unfold Nv
  rw [topsOn_eq_image X hc h0, Finset.card_image_of_injective _ (phi_inj p t0)]

theorem isV_phi (X : Inst D L) {p : ℕ} (hc : IsResidueClassZero D p) {t0 : HH D} (h0 : X.bs (stepP D p) t0 = t0)
    (i : Fin (addOrderOf (hP D p))) : IsV X p (phi D p t0 i) ↔ IsTypeV i := by
  unfold IsV IsTypeV
  rw [bs_phi X hc h0, partner_count_of_central hc]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨i', hi', hev, hh⟩
    have : (⟨i', hi'⟩ : Fin (addOrderOf (hP D p))) = i := phi_inj p t0 hh
    rw [← this]
    exact hev
  · intro hev
    exact ⟨i, i.2, hev, rfl⟩

theorem dMinus_spec {n' : ℕ} (TT : Finset (Fin n')) (y : Fin n') (hd : dMinus TT y ≠ 0) :
    0 < dMinus TT y ∧ dMinus TT y ≤ n' ∧ posAdd y (n' - dMinus TT y) ∈ TT ∧
      IsTypeV (posAdd y (n' - dMinus TT y)) := by
  classical
  unfold dMinus at hd ⊢
  split_ifs at hd ⊢ with h
  · exact Nat.find_spec h
  · exact absurd rfl hd

theorem posAdd_back {n' : ℕ} (y : Fin n') (d : ℕ) (hd : d ≤ n') : posAdd (posAdd y (n' - d)) d = y := by
  apply Fin.ext
  unfold posAdd
  simp only
  rw [Nat.mod_add_mod, show y.val + (n' - d) + d = y.val + n' by omega, Nat.add_mod_right, Nat.mod_eq_of_lt y.2]

theorem dMinus_odd {n' : ℕ} (hn : Even n') (TT : Finset (Fin n')) (y : Fin n') (hy : ¬ IsTypeV y)
    (hd : dMinus TT y ≠ 0) : Odd (dMinus TT y) := by
  obtain ⟨h1, h2, -, h4⟩ := dMinus_spec TT y hd
  unfold IsTypeV at h4 hy
  unfold posAdd at h4
  simp only at h4
  obtain ⟨q, hq⟩ := hn
  have h2n : 2 ∣ n' := ⟨q, by omega⟩
  rw [Nat.even_iff, Nat.mod_mod_of_dvd _ h2n] at h4
  rw [Nat.even_iff] at hy
  rw [Nat.odd_iff]
  omega

open Classical in
theorem kink {n' : ℕ} (hn : Even n') (TT : Finset (Fin n')) (q : ℝ) (hq : 2 ≤ q) (a W : Fin n' → ℝ)
    (ha : ∀ x ∈ TT, 0 ≤ a x) (hW : ∀ x, 0 ≤ W x) :
    ∑ y ∈ TT.filter (fun y => ¬ IsTypeV y),
      (if dMinus TT y = 0 then 0 else min (W y) (a (posAdd y (n' - dMinus TT y)) / q ^ dMinus TT y)) ≤
    ∑ x ∈ TT.filter (fun x => IsTypeV x),
      ∑' e : ℕ, (if Odd e then min (a x / q ^ e) (W (posAdd x e)) else 0) := by
  set S := (TT.filter (fun y => ¬ IsTypeV y)).filter (fun y => dMinus TT y ≠ 0) with hS
  set xm : Fin n' → Fin n' := fun y => posAdd y (n' - dMinus TT y) with hxm
  set g : Fin n' → ℕ → ℝ := fun x e => min (a x / q ^ e) (W (posAdd x e)) with hg
  have h1 : ∑ y ∈ TT.filter (fun y => ¬ IsTypeV y),
      (if dMinus TT y = 0 then 0 else min (W y) (a (xm y) / q ^ dMinus TT y)) =
      ∑ y ∈ S, g (xm y) (dMinus TT y) := by
    symm
    rw [hS, Finset.sum_filter]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro y _
    by_cases hd : dMinus TT y = 0
    · rw [if_pos hd, if_neg (not_not.2 hd)]
    · rw [if_neg hd, if_pos hd]
      simp only [hg, hxm]
      rw [posAdd_back y _ (dMinus_spec TT y hd).2.1, min_comm]
  rw [h1]
  have hmaps : ∀ y ∈ S, xm y ∈ TT.filter (fun x => IsTypeV x) := by
    intro y hy
    have hd := (Finset.mem_filter.1 hy).2
    obtain ⟨_, _, h3, h4⟩ := dMinus_spec TT y hd
    exact Finset.mem_filter.2 ⟨h3, h4⟩
  rw [← Finset.sum_fiberwise_of_maps_to hmaps]
  apply Finset.sum_le_sum
  intro x hx
  have hxT := (Finset.mem_filter.1 hx).1
  set Fb := S.filter (fun y => xm y = x) with hFb
  have hinj : ∀ y ∈ Fb, ∀ y' ∈ Fb, dMinus TT y = dMinus TT y' → y = y' := by
    intro y hy y' hy' hdd
    have e1 : xm y = x := (Finset.mem_filter.1 hy).2
    have e2 : xm y' = x := (Finset.mem_filter.1 hy').2
    have d1 := (Finset.mem_filter.1 (Finset.mem_filter.1 hy).1).2
    have d2 := (Finset.mem_filter.1 (Finset.mem_filter.1 hy').1).2
    have b1 : posAdd (xm y) (dMinus TT y) = y := posAdd_back y _ (dMinus_spec TT y d1).2.1
    have b2 : posAdd (xm y') (dMinus TT y') = y' := posAdd_back y' _ (dMinus_spec TT y' d2).2.1
    calc y = posAdd (xm y) (dMinus TT y) := b1.symm
      _ = posAdd (xm y') (dMinus TT y') := by rw [e1, e2, hdd]
      _ = y' := b2
  have h2 : ∑ y ∈ Fb, g (xm y) (dMinus TT y) =
      ∑ e ∈ Fb.image (dMinus TT), (if Odd e then g x e else 0) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_image hinj]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro y hy
    have e1 : xm y = x := (Finset.mem_filter.1 hy).2
    have hyS := (Finset.mem_filter.1 hy).1
    have d1 := (Finset.mem_filter.1 hyS).2
    have o1 := (Finset.mem_filter.1 (Finset.mem_filter.1 hyS).1).2
    rw [if_pos (dMinus_odd hn TT y o1 d1), e1]
  rw [h2]
  have hg0 : ∀ e, 0 ≤ g x e := fun e => le_min (div_nonneg (ha x hxT) (by positivity)) (hW _)
  apply Summable.sum_le_tsum
  · intro e _
    split_ifs
    · exact hg0 e
    · exact le_rfl
  · exact summable_if_of_le q (a x) hq _ _ hg0 (fun e => min_le_left _ _)

theorem base (P : ParameterChoice' D L) : 500 ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n ∧ cG L ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n ∧ L.sc.w'' D.R.n ≤ L.sc.b' ∧
    0 ≤ cG L ∧ L.Sc ≤ cG L := by
  obtain ⟨hk, hN, hSc⟩ := Nm_le_Sc P
  have hkS := P.kS_le
  obtain ⟨hw1, hw2⟩ := w''_le L.sc D.R.n D.R.ord_pos
  have hkSc : 5 * L.Sc ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.Sc := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hk (by linarith)
  unfold cG
  refine ⟨by linarith, by linarith, by linarith, by linarith, by linarith⟩

theorem fill_ge_one (X : Inst D L) (P : ParameterChoice' D L) {a b : HH D → ℤ} (hW : InWin L a b) (j : ℕ)
    (z : Bool × HH D) : 1 ≤ fill X b j z := by
  obtain ⟨h500, _, hwb, _, _⟩ := base P
  have hx := sc_x_pos L.sc
  unfold fill
  by_cases ht : z.1 = false ∧ z.2 ∈ D.host.TT
  · rw [if_pos ht]
    have h1 := (X.off_top j z ht).1
    have h2 := (hW z.2 ht.2).2.2
    have h3 : 0 ≤ L.sc.b' / L.sc.x := div_nonneg (by linarith) hx.le
    linarith
  · rw [if_neg ht]
    by_cases hth : IsThickPl D z
    · have := (X.off_thick j z hth).1
      have := sc_one_le_b L.sc
      linarith
    · have := (X.off_thin j z ⟨ht, hth⟩).1
      linarith

  have hj1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (j : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast (Finset.mem_Icc.1 hj).1
  have h : 0 ≤ (2 * (j : ℝ) - 1) * ((D.R.t : ℝ) + 1) := mul_nonneg (by linarith) (by positivity)
  unfold kaJ
  have hk : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  linarith

theorem a_bounds (P : ParameterChoice' D L) {a b : HH D → ℤ} (hW : InWin L a b) {s : HH D} (hs : s ∈ D.host.TT) :
    2 * cG L ≤ (a s : ℝ) ∧ (a s : ℝ) ≤ 2 * L.sc.a ∧ L.sc.a ≤ (a s : ℝ) := by
  obtain ⟨h500, hc, hwb, _, _⟩ := base P
  obtain ⟨hk, _, _⟩ := Nm_le_Sc P
  obtain ⟨h1, h2, _⟩ := hW s hs
  have hab := two_b'_le_a L.sc
  have ha := sc_one_lt_a L.sc
  have h4 : L.sc.a / ((D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ≤ L.sc.a := by
    apply div_le_self (by linarith)
    have : 1 ≤ L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) := Real.one_le_rpow L.sc.one_lt_alt.le (by unfold Scale.A; norm_num)
    nlinarith
  refine ⟨by linarith, by linarith, h1⟩

def Mn (X : Inst D L) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p j : ℕ) (s : HH D) : ℝ :=
  ∑' e : ℕ, if Odd e then min ((a s : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ e) (fill X b j (partner D p s e)) else 0

theorem ask_add_Mn (X : Inst D L) (P : ParameterChoice' D L) {a b : HH D → ℤ} (hW : InWin L a b) {p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    (j : ℕ) {s : HH D} (hs : s ∈ D.host.TT) : askP X a b p j s + Mn X a b p j s = gam p * (a s : ℝ) := by
  have hp2 : (2 : ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast hp.two_le
  obtain ⟨h1, _, _⟩ := a_bounds P hW hs
  obtain ⟨_, _, _, hc, _⟩ := base P
  unfold askP Mn gam
  exact tsum_pos_add_min (p : ℝ) (a s) hp2 (by linarith) (fun e => fill X b j (partner D p s e))
    (fun e => by have := fill_ge_one X P hW j (partner D p s e); linarith) (fun e => Odd e) (fun e => Iff.rfl)

theorem Mn_nonneg (X : Inst D L) (P : ParameterChoice' D L) {a b : HH D → ℤ} (hW : InWin L a b) {p : ℕ}
    (j : ℕ) {s : HH D} (hs : s ∈ D.host.TT) : 0 ≤ Mn X a b p j s := by
  obtain ⟨h1, _, _⟩ := a_bounds P hW hs
  obtain ⟨_, _, _, hc, _⟩ := base P
  unfold Mn
  apply tsum_nonneg
  intro e
  split_ifs
  · exact le_min (div_nonneg (by linarith) (by positivity))
      (by have := fill_ge_one X P hW j (partner D p s e); linarith)
  · exact le_rfl

theorem odd_top (X : Inst D L) (P : ParameterChoice' D L) {a b : HH D → ℤ} (hW : InWin L a b) {p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    {j : ℕ} (hj : j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D) {s : HH D} (hs : s ∈ D.host.TT) :
    askP X a b p j s - 3 * cG L * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) ≤ gam p * al L a s -
      ∑' e : ℕ, (if Odd e then min (al L a s / (p : ℝ) ^ e) (capU X b p j s e) else 0) := by
  have hp2 : (2 : ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast hp.two_le
  obtain ⟨h1, h2, _⟩ := a_bounds P hW hs
  obtain ⟨_, _, _, hc, hSc⟩ := base P
  have ha := sc_one_lt_a L.sc
  have hk0 := kaJ_nonneg D j hj
  have hk1 := kaJ_le P j hj
  have hcap0 : ∀ e, 0 ≤ capU X b p j s e := by
    intro e
    have := fill_ge_one X P hW j (partner D p s e)
    unfold capU fillG
    linarith
  have hcap1 : ∀ e, capU X b p j s e ≤ fill X b j (partner D p s e) + cG L := by
    intro e
    unfold capU fillG
    linarith
  have e1 := tsum_pos_add_min (p : ℝ) (al L a s) hp2 (by unfold al; linarith) (fun e => capU X b p j s e) hcap0
    (fun e => Odd e) (fun e => Iff.rfl)
  have e2 := tsum_truncated_shift_le (p : ℝ) (a s) (cG L) (2 * L.sc.a) hp2 hc h1 h2 (by linarith)
    (fun e => fill X b j (partner D p s e)) (fun e => capU X b p j s e)
    (fun e => fill_ge_one X P hW j _) hcap0 hcap1 (fun e => Odd e) (fun e h => h)
  unfold askP gam
  unfold al at e1 ⊢
  linarith

theorem gam_bounds {p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime) : 0 ≤ gam p ∧ gam p ≤ 1 := by
  have hp2 : (2 : ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast hp.two_le
  have hd : (0 : ℝ) < (p : ℝ) ^ 2 - 1 := by nlinarith
  unfold gam
  refine ⟨div_nonneg (by linarith) hd.le, ?_⟩
  rw [div_le_one hd]
  nlinarith

open Classical in

theorem gU_lower (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (P : ParameterChoice' D L) {a b : HH D → ℤ} (hW : InWin L a b) {p : ℕ}
    (hp : p.Prime) (hlg : 1 ≤ Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a)) {j : ℕ} (hj : j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D) (t0 : HH D) :
    (∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, askP X a b p j s) +
      (∑ s ∈ (topsOn X p t0).filter (fun s => IsV X p s), Mn X a b p j s) -
      (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (3 * cG L * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a)) ≤ gU X K a b p j t0 + dl X K p t0 := by
  unfold gU Nv
  set T := topsOn X p t0 with hT
  set lgc := 3 * cG L * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) with hlgc
  have hTT : ∀ s ∈ T, s ∈ D.host.TT := fun s hs => (Finset.mem_filter.1 hs).1
  obtain ⟨_, _, _, hc, _⟩ := base P
  obtain ⟨hg0, hg1⟩ := gam_bounds hp
  have hcl : cG L ≤ cG L * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) := le_mul_of_one_le_right hc hlg
  have hgc : gam p * (2 * cG L) ≤ lgc := by
    have : gam p * (2 * cG L) ≤ 1 * (2 * cG L) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hg1 (by linarith)
    rw [hlgc]
    linarith
  have hE : ∀ s ∈ T.filter (fun s => IsV X p s),
      askP X a b p j s + Mn X a b p j s - lgc ≤ gam p * al L a s := by
    intro s hs
    have h1 := ask_add_Mn X P hW hp j (hTT s (Finset.mem_filter.1 hs).1)
    unfold al
    rw [mul_sub]
    linarith
  have hO : ∀ s ∈ T.filter (fun s => ¬ IsV X p s), askP X a b p j s - lgc ≤ gam p * al L a s -
      ∑' e : ℕ, (if Odd e then min (al L a s / (p : ℝ) ^ e) (capU X b p j s e) else 0) := by
    intro s hs
    exact odd_top X P hW hp hj (hTT s (Finset.mem_filter.1 hs).1)
  have sE := Finset.sum_le_sum hE
  have sO := Finset.sum_le_sum hO
  simp only [Finset.sum_sub_distrib, Finset.sum_add_distrib] at sE sO
  have a1 := Finset.sum_filter_add_sum_filter_not T (fun s => IsV X p s) (fun s => askP X a b p j s)
  have a2 := Finset.sum_filter_add_sum_filter_not T (fun s => IsV X p s) (fun s => gam p * al L a s)
  have a4 := Finset.sum_filter_add_sum_filter_not T (fun s => IsV X p s) (fun _ => lgc)
  have a5 : ∑ _s ∈ T, lgc = (T.card : ℝ) * lgc := by rw [Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul]
  rw [Finset.mul_sum]
  linarith

theorem partner_of_central {p : ℕ} (hc : IsResidueClassZero D p) (s : HH D) (e : ℕ) : partner D p s e = (false, s + e • hP D p) := by
  unfold partner
  rw [decide_eq_false (fun h => not_singular_of_central hc h.1)]

open Classical in
theorem sum_topsOn_V (X : Inst D L) {p : ℕ} (hc : IsResidueClassZero D p) {t0 : HH D} (h0 : X.bs (stepP D p) t0 = t0)
    (F : HH D → ℝ) : ∑ s ∈ (topsOn X p t0).filter (fun s => IsV X p s), F s =
      ∑ i ∈ (topsPos D p t0).filter (fun i => IsTypeV i), F (phi D p t0 i) := by
  rw [Finset.sum_filter, Finset.sum_filter, sum_topsOn X hc h0]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro i _
  exact if_congr (isV_phi X hc h0 i) rfl rfl

open Classical in
theorem sum_topsOn_nV (X : Inst D L) {p : ℕ} (hc : IsResidueClassZero D p) {t0 : HH D} (h0 : X.bs (stepP D p) t0 = t0)
    (F : HH D → ℝ) : ∑ s ∈ (topsOn X p t0).filter (fun s => ¬ IsV X p s), F s =
      ∑ i ∈ (topsPos D p t0).filter (fun i => ¬ IsTypeV i), F (phi D p t0 i) := by
  rw [Finset.sum_filter, Finset.sum_filter, sum_topsOn X hc h0]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro i _
  exact if_congr (not_congr (isV_phi X hc h0 i)) rfl rfl

open Classical in

theorem kinkHH (X : Inst D L) (P : ParameterChoice' D L) {a b : HH D → ℤ} (hW : InWin L a b) {p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    (hc : IsResidueClassZero D p) (h1 : Even (addOrderOf (hP D p))) {t0 : HH D} (h0 : X.bs (stepP D p) t0 = t0) {j : ℕ}
    (hj : j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D) :
    (∑ s ∈ (topsOn X p t0).filter (fun s => ¬ IsV X p s), (fillG X b j (false, s) - (b s : ℝ))) +
      (∑ y ∈ (topsPos D p t0).filter (fun y => ¬ IsTypeV y),
        (if dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y = 0 then 0 else
          min (wPos D p t0 b y : ℝ) ((wPos D p t0 a (posAdd y (addOrderOf (hP D p) - dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y)) : ℝ) /
            (p : ℝ) ^ dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y))) ≤
    (∑ s ∈ (topsOn X p t0).filter (fun s => IsV X p s), Mn X a b p j s) +
      (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (L.sc.b' / L.sc.x + L.etaSz) := by
  have hp2 : (2 : ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast hp.two_le
  obtain ⟨h500, _, hwb, hcG, _⟩ := base P
  have hx := sc_x_pos L.sc
  have hbx : 0 ≤ L.sc.b' / L.sc.x := div_nonneg (by linarith) hx.le
  have hη := L.etaSz_pos
  have hk0 := kaJ_nonneg D j hj
  have hmemT : ∀ y ∈ topsPos D p t0, phi D p t0 y ∈ D.host.TT := fun y hy => (Finset.mem_filter.1 hy).2
  rw [sum_topsOn_nV X hc h0, sum_topsOn_V X hc h0, ← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
  have hk := kink h1 (topsPos D p t0) (p : ℝ) hp2 (fun i => (a (phi D p t0 i) : ℝ))
    (fun i => fill X b j (false, phi D p t0 i))
    (fun x hx => by have := (a_bounds P hW (hmemT x hx)).1; linarith)
    (fun x => by have := fill_ge_one X P hW j (false, phi D p t0 x); linarith)
  have hR : ∑ x ∈ (topsPos D p t0).filter (fun x => IsTypeV x), ∑' e : ℕ, (if Odd e then
      min ((a (phi D p t0 x) : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ e) (fill X b j (false, phi D p t0 (posAdd x e))) else 0) =
      ∑ i ∈ (topsPos D p t0).filter (fun i => IsTypeV i), Mn X a b p j (phi D p t0 i) := by
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro x _
    unfold Mn
    apply tsum_congr
    intro e
    rw [partner_of_central hc, phi_posAdd]
  rw [hR] at hk
  have hterm : ∀ y ∈ (topsPos D p t0).filter (fun y => ¬ IsTypeV y),
      (fillG X b j (false, phi D p t0 y) - (b (phi D p t0 y) : ℝ)) +
        (if dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y = 0 then 0 else
          min (wPos D p t0 b y : ℝ) ((wPos D p t0 a (posAdd y (addOrderOf (hP D p) - dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y)) : ℝ) /
            (p : ℝ) ^ dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y)) ≤
      (if dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y = 0 then 0 else
          min (fill X b j (false, phi D p t0 y))
            ((a (phi D p t0 (posAdd y (addOrderOf (hP D p) - dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y))) : ℝ) /
            (p : ℝ) ^ dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y)) + (L.sc.b' / L.sc.x + L.etaSz) := by
    intro y hy
    have hyT := hmemT y (Finset.mem_filter.1 hy).1
    have hf : fill X b j (false, phi D p t0 y) = (b (phi D p t0 y) : ℝ) + X.off j (false, phi D p t0 y) := by
      unfold fill
      rw [if_pos ⟨rfl, hyT⟩]
    obtain ⟨o1, o2⟩ := X.off_top j (false, phi D p t0 y) ⟨rfl, hyT⟩
    unfold fillG
    rw [hf]
    split_ifs with hd
    · linarith
    · have hm : min (wPos D p t0 b y : ℝ)
          ((wPos D p t0 a (posAdd y (addOrderOf (hP D p) - dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y)) : ℝ) /
            (p : ℝ) ^ dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y) ≤
          min ((b (phi D p t0 y) : ℝ) + X.off j (false, phi D p t0 y))
            ((a (phi D p t0 (posAdd y (addOrderOf (hP D p) - dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y))) : ℝ) /
            (p : ℝ) ^ dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y) := by
        apply min_le_min
        · show (b (phi D p t0 y) : ℝ) ≤ _
          linarith
        · exact le_rfl
      linarith
  have hs := Finset.sum_le_sum hterm
  have hcard : (((topsPos D p t0).filter (fun y => ¬ IsTypeV y)).card : ℝ) ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) := by
    rw [Nv_eq_card X hc h0]
    exact_mod_cast Finset.card_filter_le _ _
  have hmul := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hcard (by linarith : 0 ≤ L.sc.b' / L.sc.x + L.etaSz)
  refine le_trans hs ?_
  rw [Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul]
  linarith


[GLG_star_cases_01] adm1 good-orbit count and central orbit guards
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/SmallOrderPrimes.lean#L70-L83
Range SHA256: 6f34edceeb4844dd7a28888ec156c27d18432fb6e55efc8518e13dd2c736ece3
def adm1 (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (Ce : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → IsResidueClassZero D p → L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) ≤ (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) →
    L2 D L.sc < goodU L.sc.delta1 → 3 * Gp L.sc ≤ addOrderOf (hP D p) →
    ∀ t0 ∈ reps X p, Good X p t0 →
      ((addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1 / (Ce * Real.log L.sc.x) ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) ∧
        (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) ≤ 40 * (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1) ∧
      (∀ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, IsV X p s → ∃ d : ℕ, 1 ≤ d ∧ d ≤ Gp L.sc ∧
        s + d • hP D p ∈ D.host.TT ∧ IsV X p (s + d • hP D p) ∧
        ((Finset.Ioo 0 d).filter (fun e => s + e • hP D p ∈ D.host.TT)).card ≤ N1 L.sc) ∧
      (∀ i ∈ topsOn X p t0,
        ((Finset.Icc 1 (L1 D L.sc p)).filter (fun d =>
          i - d • hP D p ∈ D.host.TT ∧ IsV X p (i - d • hP D p))).card < rG L.sc)

open Classical in


[GLG_star_cases_02] orbit count and finite arithmetic budgets
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/LogarithmicSums/PartnerCountOfCentral.lean#L471-L561
Range SHA256: 0c4af4a6f66136791d0a0b3a294112b8d2fa0f0827fb5b1e9fbadbf000020f04
theorem orbit_facts (X : Inst D L) {K : Consts} {C31 C bo : ℝ} (A1 : adm1 D L X C31) (hC31 : 0 < C31)
    (Nn : NumStar D L K C31 C bo) {p : ℕ} (hcand : Cand D L K p) {t0 : HH D} (ht0 : t0 ∈ goodReps X p) :
    (1 : ℝ) ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) ∧
    (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1 ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x) ∧
    (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨hp, hX, hc, hord, _⟩ := hcand
  have h3 : 3 * Gp L.sc ≤ addOrderOf (hP D p) := by exact_mod_cast le_trans Nn.h3G hord
  obtain ⟨hr, hg⟩ := Finset.mem_filter.1 ht0
  have h := (A1 p hp hc hord Nn.hL2 h3 t0 hr hg).1.1
  have hpos : 0 < C31 * Real.log L.sc.x := mul_pos hC31 (by have := Nn.hlx; linarith)
  have hδ := sc_delta1_pos L.sc
  rw [div_le_iff₀ hpos] at h
  have hn1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast addOrderOf_pos (hP D p)
  have hN0 : 0 < (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) := by
    by_contra hcon
    have h0 : (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) ≤ 0 := not_lt.1 hcon
    have h1 : (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x) ≤ 0 := mul_nonpos_of_nonpos_of_nonneg h0 hpos.le
    have h2 : 0 < (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1 := mul_pos (by linarith) hδ
    linarith
  refine ⟨?_, h, ?_⟩
  · have : 0 < Nv X p t0 := by exact_mod_cast hN0
    exact_mod_cast this
  · rw [← mul_div_assoc, le_div_iff₀ hδ]
    exact h

theorem caseD_arith (N n nd r lx b' G u c c0 kQ S1 Dw C31 Pr2 tc C : ℝ)
    (hN : 1 ≤ N) (hn0 : 0 ≤ n) (_hnd0 : 0 ≤ nd) (hr : 1 ≤ r) (hlx : 1 ≤ lx) (hb : 1 ≤ b') (hnG : n ≤ N * G)
    (hnd : nd ≤ N * (C31 * lx)) (hGu : G * u ≤ b') (hc0' : 0 ≤ c) (hcu : c ≤ u) (hc03 : c0 ≤ 3 * b')
    (hc00 : 0 ≤ c0) (hkQ : kQ ≤ b') (hS1 : 0 ≤ S1) (hDw0 : 0 ≤ Dw) (hDw : Dw ≤ b') (hPr2 : 0 ≤ Pr2)
    (htc : 0 ≤ tc) (hC31 : 0 ≤ C31) (hC1 : tc * r ≤ C) (hC2 : 2 * Pr2 + 4 * tc + 160 * C31 + 7 ≤ C) :
    Pr2 * r * (N * b' + n * u) + (tc * r * N * (S1 + c0 + kQ) + 2 * r * n * c + 1) +
      (40 * nd * Dw + 2 * r * n * c + r * (40 * nd) * c0 + 1) + 1 ≤ N * (C * r * b' * lx + C * lx * S1) := by
  have hu0 : 0 ≤ u := le_trans hc0' hcu
  have hN0 : 0 ≤ N := by linarith
  have hr0 : 0 ≤ r := by linarith
  have hb0 : 0 ≤ b' := by linarith
  have hlx0 : 0 ≤ lx := by linarith
  have hC0 : 0 ≤ C := by linarith
  have t1 : n * u ≤ N * b' := by
    calc n * u ≤ N * G * u := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hnG hu0
      _ = N * (G * u) := by ring
      _ ≤ N * b' := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hGu hN0
  have t2 : n * c ≤ N * b' := le_trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hcu hn0) t1
  have hZ : 1 ≤ N * b' := by nlinarith
  have hZ0 : 0 ≤ N * b' := by linarith
  have hCl0 : 0 ≤ C31 * lx := mul_nonneg hC31 hlx0
  have t3 : nd * Dw ≤ C31 * (N * b' * lx) := by
    calc nd * Dw ≤ N * (C31 * lx) * Dw := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hnd hDw0
      _ ≤ N * (C31 * lx) * b' := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hDw (mul_nonneg hN0 hCl0)
      _ = C31 * (N * b' * lx) := by ring
  have t4 : nd * c0 ≤ 3 * (C31 * (N * b' * lx)) := by
    calc nd * c0 ≤ N * (C31 * lx) * c0 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hnd hc00
      _ ≤ N * (C31 * lx) * (3 * b') := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hc03 (mul_nonneg hN0 hCl0)
      _ = 3 * (C31 * (N * b' * lx)) := by ring
  have y0 : N * b' ≤ N * b' * r := le_mul_of_one_le_right hZ0 hr
  have y1 : N * b' * r ≤ N * b' * r * lx := le_mul_of_one_le_right (by linarith) hlx
  have y2 : N * b' * lx ≤ N * b' * r * lx := by
    have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right y0 hlx0
    linarith
  have B1 : Pr2 * r * (n * u) ≤ Pr2 * r * (N * b') := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left t1 (mul_nonneg hPr2 hr0)
  have B2 : Pr2 * (N * b' * r) ≤ Pr2 * (N * b' * r * lx) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left y1 hPr2
  have B3 : tc * r * N * (c0 + kQ) ≤ tc * r * N * (4 * b') :=
    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg htc hr0) hN0)
  have B4 : tc * (N * b' * r) ≤ tc * (N * b' * r * lx) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left y1 htc
  have B5 : tc * r * (N * S1) ≤ C * (N * S1) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hC1 (mul_nonneg hN0 hS1)
  have B6 : C * (N * S1) ≤ C * (N * S1) * lx := le_mul_of_one_le_right (mul_nonneg hC0 (mul_nonneg hN0 hS1)) hlx
  have B7 : r * (n * c) ≤ r * (N * b') := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left t2 hr0
  have BetaSesquilinearity : C31 * (N * b' * lx) ≤ C31 * (N * b' * r * lx) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left y2 hC31
  have B9 : r * (nd * c0) ≤ r * (3 * (C31 * (N * b' * lx))) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left t4 hr0
  have lowerOffDiagonalCoeff : (2 * Pr2 + 4 * tc + 160 * C31 + 7) * (N * b' * r * lx) ≤ C * (N * b' * r * lx) :=
    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hC2 (by linarith)
  nlinarith [B1, B2, B3, B4, B5, B6, B7, BetaSesquilinearity, B9, lowerOffDiagonalCoeff, t3, y0, y1, y2, hZ]

theorem caseE_arith (N n nd lx G w η c Nm S2 B1 E tN1 Pr1 kappaThresholdTwo C31 C bo Cw : ℝ)
    (hN : 1 ≤ N) (hnG : n ≤ N * G) (hnd : nd ≤ N * (C31 * lx)) (hw : 0 ≤ w) (hη : 0 ≤ η) (hc : 0 ≤ c)
    (hNm : 0 ≤ Nm) (hS2 : 0 ≤ S2) (hE : 0 ≤ E) (hPr1 : 0 ≤ Pr1) (hGw : Pr1 * G * w ≤ Cw)
    (htN1 : tN1 ≤ C * lx)
    (hbo : Pr1 * G * (η + c + Nm) + kappaThresholdTwo * B1 + 2 * G * c + 40 * C31 * lx * E + 3 ≤ bo) :
    Pr1 * n * (w + η + c + Nm) + (tN1 * N * S2 + kappaThresholdTwo * N * B1 + 2 * n * c + 1) + (40 * nd * E + 1) + 1 ≤
      N * (Cw + bo + C * lx * S2) := by
  have hN0 : 0 ≤ N := by linarith
  have A1 : Pr1 * n * (w + η + c + Nm) ≤ Pr1 * (N * G) * (w + η + c + Nm) :=
    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hnG hPr1) (by linarith)
  have A2 : n * c ≤ N * G * c := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hnG hc
  have A3 : nd * E ≤ N * (C31 * lx) * E := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hnd hE
  have A4 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hbo hN0
  have A5 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hGw hN0
  have A6 : tN1 * (N * S2) ≤ C * lx * (N * S2) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right htN1 (mul_nonneg hN0 hS2)
  nlinarith [A1, A2, A3, A4, A5, A6]



[GLG_star_cases_03] exact p<p0 StarAt branch
