theorem lg_ge_one (P : ParameterChoice' D L) {p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime) (hX : (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X') :
    1 ≤ Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) := by
  obtain ⟨h500, _, hwb, _, _⟩ := base P
  have hp1 : (1 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp.one_lt
  have hXpos : 0 < L.sc.X' := by unfold Scale.X'; exact Real.exp_pos _
  have ha := sc_one_lt_a L.sc
  have hb : 1 ≤ L.sc.a / L.sc.X' := by
    have : L.sc.b' = L.sc.a / L.sc.X' := rfl
    linarith
  rw [one_le_div hXpos] at hb
  rw [Real.le_logb_iff_rpow_le hp1 (by linarith), Real.rpow_one]
  linarith

theorem lo_D (K : Consts) (L : SizeData₂ D) (C bo : ℝ) {p : ℕ} (hc : IsResidueClassZero D p) (h0 : (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k)
    (hX : (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X') : lo K L C bo p = C * (cG L + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) +
      C * (rG L.sc : ℝ) * L.sc.b' * Real.log L.sc.x +
      C * Real.log L.sc.x * (La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) + L.sc.a * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(L1 D L.sc p : ℝ))) := by
  simp only [lo, if_pos hc, if_pos h0, if_pos (And.intro hc hX)]

theorem lo_E (K : Consts) (L : SizeData₂ D) (C bo : ℝ) {p : ℕ} (hc : IsResidueClassZero D p) (h0 : L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ))
    (hX : (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X') : lo K L C bo p = C * (cG L + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) +
      (L.sc.b' / L.sc.x + L.etaSz + C * L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 + bo) +
      C * Real.log L.sc.x * (La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) + L.sc.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2) := by
  simp only [lo, if_pos hc, if_neg (not_lt.2 h0), if_pos hX, if_pos (And.intro hc hX)]

theorem La_nonneg (K : Consts) (hLa : 0 ≤ K.La) (s : Scale) : 0 ≤ La K s := by
  unfold La
  exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hLa (by positivity)) (Real.log_nonneg (sc_one_lt_a s).le)

theorem rG_ge_one (s : Scale) : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (rG s : ℝ) := by
  unfold rG
  push_cast
  have : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (KG s : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  linarith

theorem starAt_D (X : Inst D L) {K : Consts} (hK : K.Nonneg) (P : ParameterChoice' D L) {C31 C bo : ℝ}
    (A1 : adm1 D L X C31) (hC31 : 0 < C31) (Nn : NumStar D L K C31 C bo) {a b : HH D → ℤ} (hW : InWin L a b)
    {p : ℕ} (hcand : Cand D L K p) (h0 : (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k) {t0 : HH D} (ht0 : t0 ∈ goodReps X p) {j : ℕ}
    (hj : j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D) : StarAt X K C bo a b p j t0 := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨hN1, hnd, hnG⟩ := orbit_facts X A1 hC31 Nn hcand ht0
  obtain ⟨hp, hX, hc, hord, _⟩ := hcand
  obtain ⟨hLa, _, _, htII, hDII, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _⟩ := hK
  obtain ⟨h500, hcw, hwb, hc0, _⟩ := base P
  have hlg := lg_ge_one P hp hX
  have hgl := gU_lower X K P hW hp hlg hj t0
  have hMn : 0 ≤ ∑ s ∈ (topsOn X p t0).filter (fun s => IsV X p s), Mn X a b p j s :=
    Finset.sum_nonneg (fun s hs => Mn_nonneg X P hW j (Finset.mem_filter.1 (Finset.mem_filter.1 hs).1).1)
  have hp1 : (1 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp.one_lt
  have hp0 : (0 : ℝ) < p := by linarith
  have ha := sc_one_lt_a L.sc
  have hη := L.etaSz_pos
  have hδ := sc_delta1_pos L.sc
  have hr := rG_ge_one L.sc
  have hNm0 : 0 ≤ Nm D := by unfold Nm; exact Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hn0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hk0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hlp : 0 ≤ Real.logb p L.sc.a := Real.logb_nonneg hp1 ha.le
  have hQ0 : 0 ≤ Qp L.sc p := by unfold Qp; positivity
  have hc00 : 0 ≤ c0num L := by unfold c0num; linarith
  have hS1 : 0 ≤ La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) + L.sc.a * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(L1 D L.sc p : ℝ)) :=
    add_nonneg (div_nonneg (mul_nonneg (La_nonneg K hLa _) (by linarith)) (mul_nonneg hp0.le hk0))
      (mul_nonneg (by linarith) (Real.rpow_nonneg hp0.le _))
  have hDw0 : 0 ≤ K.DII * ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 3 + (rG L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2) * Real.logb p L.sc.a :=
    mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hDII (by positivity)) hlp
  have har := caseD_arith (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) ((addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1)
    (rG L.sc : ℝ) (Real.log L.sc.x) L.sc.b' (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1)
    (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D) (cG L) (c0num L) ((D.R.k : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p)
    (La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) + L.sc.a * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(L1 D L.sc p : ℝ)))
    (K.DII * ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 3 + (rG L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2) * Real.logb p L.sc.a) C31 3 K.tII C
    hN1 hn0 (mul_nonneg hn0 hδ.le) hr Nn.hlx (by linarith) hnG hnd Nn.hGu hc0 (by linarith) Nn.hc0 hc00
    (Nn.hkQ p hp h0) hS1 hDw0 (Nn.hDII p hp) (by norm_num) htII hC31.le Nn.hCt Nn.hCD
  have hPr : Pr X K b p j t0 ≤ 3 * (rG L.sc : ℝ) * ((Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.b' +
      (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D)) := by
    unfold Pr
    rw [if_neg (not_le.2 h0), partner_count_of_central hc]
    have e1 : (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (2 * L.sc.b' + cG L) ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (3 * L.sc.b') :=
      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) (by linarith)
    have e2 : 0 ≤ (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D) :=
      mul_nonneg hn0 (by linarith)
    have e3 : (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (2 * L.sc.b' + cG L) +
        (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D) ≤
        3 * ((Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.b' +
          (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D)) := by linarith
    have e4 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left e3 (by linarith : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (rG L.sc : ℝ))
    linarith
  have htv : tv X K a b p t0 = tIIf K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (Nv X p t0) := by
    unfold tv
    rw [if_pos h0, partner_count_of_central hc]
  have hDp : Dp K L p = DIIf K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) := by
    unfold Dp
    rw [if_pos h0, partner_count_of_central hc]
  have hN0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) := by linarith
  have hr0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (rG L.sc : ℝ) := by linarith
  have ht0' : 0 ≤ tIIf K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (Nv X p t0) := by
    unfold tIIf
    exact add_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg htII hr0) hN0)
      (by have := mul_nonneg hk0 hQ0; linarith)) (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (by norm_num) hr0) hn0) hc0)
  have hceil := (Nat.ceil_lt_add_one ht0').le
  have hNp0 : 0 ≤ NplusF L.sc (addOrderOf (hP D p)) := by
    unfold NplusF
    exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (by norm_num) hn0) hδ.le
  have hD := (Nat.ceil_lt_add_one (a := K.DII * NplusF L.sc (addOrderOf (hP D p)) *
      ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 3 + (rG L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2) *
      Real.logb p L.sc.a + 2 * (rG L.sc : ℝ) * (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * cG L +
      (rG L.sc : ℝ) * NplusF L.sc (addOrderOf (hP D p)) * c0num L)
    (add_nonneg (add_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hDII hNp0) (by positivity)) hlp)
      (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (by norm_num) hr0) hn0) hc0)) (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hr0 hNp0) hc00))).le
  have hs : (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (3 * cG L * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a)) ≤
      (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (C * (cG L + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a)) := by
    apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left _ hN0
    apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right _ (by linarith)
    have h1 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right Nn.hC3 hc0
    have h2 : 0 ≤ C * L.etaSz := mul_nonneg (by linarith [Nn.hC3]) hη.le
    linarith
  unfold StarAt
  rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib, Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul, lo_D K L C bo hc h0 hX, htv, hDp]
  have hcard : ((topsOn X p t0).card : ℝ) = (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) := rfl
  rw [hcard]
  unfold tIIf at hceil
  unfold NplusF at hD
  have hDD : (DIIf K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) : ℝ) ≤ K.DII * (40 * (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1) *
      ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 3 + (rG L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2) *
      Real.logb p L.sc.a + 2 * (rG L.sc : ℝ) * (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * cG L +
      (rG L.sc : ℝ) * (40 * (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1) * c0num L + 1 := hD
  unfold tIIf
  linarith [hgl, hMn, har, hceil, hDD, hs, hPr]

theorem order_le_n (D : D7) (h : HH D) : addOrderOf h ≤ D.R.n := by
  have hd : addOrderOf h ∣ D.R.ord := by
    have := addOrderOf_dvd_card (x := h)
    rwa [D.R.card_A] at this
  exact Nat.le_of_dvd D.R.ord_pos hd

theorem starAt_E (X : Inst D L) {K : Consts} (hK : K.Nonneg) (P : ParameterChoice' D L) {C31 C bo : ℝ}
    (A1 : adm1 D L X C31) (hC31 : 0 < C31) (Nn : NumStar D L K C31 C bo) {a b : HH D → ℤ} (hW : InWin L a b)
    {p : ℕ} (hcand : Cand D L K p) (h0 : L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ)) {t0 : HH D} (ht0 : t0 ∈ goodReps X p) {j : ℕ}
    (hj : j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D) : StarAt X K C bo a b p j t0 := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨hN1, hnd, hnG⟩ := orbit_facts X A1 hC31 Nn hcand ht0
  obtain ⟨hp, hX, hc, hord, _⟩ := hcand
  obtain ⟨hLa, _, _, _, _, _, _, hDI, htI1, htI2, _, _, _, hPr1, _⟩ := hK
  obtain ⟨h500, hcw, hwb, hc0, _⟩ := base P
  have hfix : X.bs (stepP D p) t0 = t0 := reps_fix X (Finset.mem_filter.1 ht0).1
  have hord1 : 1 < addOrderOf (hP D p) := by
    have h1 : (1 : ℝ) < L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) :=
      Real.one_lt_rpow L.sc.one_lt_alt (by have := L.sc.eps_pos; positivity)
    exact_mod_cast lt_of_lt_of_le h1 hord
  have hlg := lg_ge_one P hp hX
  have hgl := gU_lower X K P hW hp hlg hj t0
  have hkk := kinkHH X P hW hp hc (UpperTheory.even_addOrderOf_hP_of_pow_le.2 D L p hord) hfix hj
  have hp1 : (1 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp.one_lt
  have hp0 : (0 : ℝ) < p := by linarith
  have ha := sc_one_lt_a L.sc
  have hη := L.etaSz_pos
  have hδ := sc_delta1_pos L.sc
  have hlx := Nn.hlx
  have hNm0 : 0 ≤ Nm D := by unfold Nm; exact Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hn0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hk0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hlp : 0 ≤ Real.logb p L.sc.a := Real.logb_nonneg hp1 ha.le
  have hQ0 : 0 ≤ Qp L.sc p := by unfold Qp; positivity
  have hN0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) := by linarith
  have hw0 : 0 ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n := by linarith
  have hS2 : 0 ≤ L.sc.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2 + La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) :=
    add_nonneg (div_nonneg (by linarith) (by positivity))
      (div_nonneg (mul_nonneg (La_nonneg K hLa _) (by linarith)) (mul_nonneg hp0.le hk0))
  have hE0 : 0 ≤ K.DI * (L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ^ 2 * ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3) * Real.logb p L.sc.a :=
    mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hDI (by positivity)) (by positivity)) hlp
  have hnle : addOrderOf (hP D p) ≤ D.R.n := order_le_n D _
  have hNle : Nv X p t0 ≤ D.R.n := by
    rw [Nv_eq_card X hc hfix]
    refine le_trans (Finset.card_le_univ _) ?_
    rw [Fintype.card_fin]
    exact hnle
  have hGw : K.Pr1 * (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) * L.sc.w'' D.R.n ≤
      C * L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 := by
    have h1 : 0 ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 :=
      div_nonneg (mul_nonneg hw0 (by linarith)) hδ.le
    have h2 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right Nn.hCP h1
    calc K.Pr1 * (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) * L.sc.w'' D.R.n
        = K.Pr1 * C31 * (L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) := by ring
      _ ≤ C * (L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) := h2
      _ = C * L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 := by ring
  have hNp0 : 0 ≤ NplusF L.sc (addOrderOf (hP D p)) := by
    unfold NplusF
    exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (by norm_num) hn0) hδ.le
  have hB0 : 0 ≤ Qp L.sc p * c6f K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) +
        (D.R.k : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p + (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.logb p L.sc.a + cG L := by
    have e1 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (DIf K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
    have e2 : 0 ≤ K.kappaThresholdTwo * NplusF L.sc (addOrderOf (hP D p)) * (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.logb p L.sc.a :=
      mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg htI2 hNp0) hk0) hlp
    have e3 : 0 ≤ 4 * NplusF L.sc (addOrderOf (hP D p)) * cG L := mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (by norm_num) hNp0) hc0
    have e4 : 0 ≤ 2 * (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * cG L := mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (by norm_num) hn0) hc0
    have e5 := mul_nonneg hk0 hQ0
    have e6 := mul_nonneg hk0 hlp
    have e7 : 0 ≤ Qp L.sc p * c6f K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) := mul_nonneg hQ0 (by unfold c6f; linarith)
    linarith
  have har := caseE_arith (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) ((addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1)
    (Real.log L.sc.x) (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) (L.sc.w'' D.R.n) L.etaSz (cG L) (Nm D)
    (L.sc.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2 + La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k))
    (Qp L.sc p * c6f K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) +
        (D.R.k : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p + (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.logb p L.sc.a + cG L)
    (K.DI * (L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ^ 2 * ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3) * Real.logb p L.sc.a)
    (K.kappaThresholdOne * (N1 L.sc : ℝ)) K.Pr1 K.kappaThresholdTwo C31 C bo (C * L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1)
    hN1 hnG hnd hw0 hη.le hc0 hNm0 hS2 hE0 hPr1 hGw Nn.hN1
    (Nn.hbo p hp h0 hX (addOrderOf (hP D p)) hnle)
  have hPr : Pr X K b p j t0 =
      (∑ s ∈ (topsOn X p t0).filter (fun s => ¬ IsV X p s), (fillG X b j (false, s) - (b s : ℝ))) +
      K.Pr1 * (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D) := by
    unfold Pr
    rw [if_pos h0, partner_count_of_central hc]
  have htv : tv X K a b p t0 =
      tIf K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0) (wPos D p t0 a) (wPos D p t0 b) := by
    unfold tv
    rw [if_neg (not_lt.2 h0)]
  have hDp : Dp K L p = DIf K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) := by
    unfold Dp
    rw [if_neg (not_lt.2 h0), partner_count_of_central hc]

  have hKk : 0 ≤ ∑ y ∈ (topsPos D p t0).filter (fun y => ¬ IsTypeV y),
        (if dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y = 0 then 0 else
          min (wPos D p t0 b y : ℝ) ((wPos D p t0 a (posAdd y (addOrderOf (hP D p) - dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y)) : ℝ) /
            (p : ℝ) ^ dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y)) := by
    apply Finset.sum_nonneg
    intro y hy
    split_ifs with hd
    · exact le_rfl
    · have hyT : phi D p t0 y ∈ D.host.TT := (Finset.mem_filter.1 (Finset.mem_filter.1 hy).1).2
      have hxT : phi D p t0 (posAdd y (addOrderOf (hP D p) - dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y)) ∈ D.host.TT :=
        (Finset.mem_filter.1 (dMinus_spec _ y hd).2.2.1).2
      apply le_min
      · have := (hW _ hyT).2.2
        show (0 : ℝ) ≤ (b (phi D p t0 y) : ℝ)
        linarith
      · apply div_nonneg _ (by positivity)
        have := (a_bounds P hW hxT).1
        show (0 : ℝ) ≤ (a (phi D p t0 (posAdd y (addOrderOf (hP D p) - dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y))) : ℝ)
        linarith
  have hcardN : ((topsPos D p t0).card : ℝ) = (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) := by rw [Nv_eq_card X hc hfix]
  have htIf : tIf K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0) (wPos D p t0 a) (wPos D p t0 b) =
      (∑ y ∈ (topsPos D p t0).filter (fun y => ¬ IsTypeV y),
        (if dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y = 0 then 0 else
          min (wPos D p t0 b y : ℝ) ((wPos D p t0 a (posAdd y (addOrderOf (hP D p) - dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y)) : ℝ) /
            (p : ℝ) ^ dMinus (topsPos D p t0) y))) +
      K.kappaThresholdOne * (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (N1 L.sc : ℝ) *
        (L.sc.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2 + La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k)) +
      K.kappaThresholdTwo * (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (Qp L.sc p * c6f K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) +
        (D.R.k : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p + (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.logb p L.sc.a + cG L) +
      2 * (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * cG L := by
    unfold tIf
    rw [hcardN]
  have ht0' : 0 ≤ tIf K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0) (wPos D p t0 a) (wPos D p t0 b) := by
    rw [htIf]
    have e1 : 0 ≤ K.kappaThresholdOne * (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (N1 L.sc : ℝ) *
        (L.sc.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2 + La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k)) :=
      mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg htI1 hN0) (Nat.cast_nonneg _)) hS2
    have e2 := mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg htI2 hN0) hB0
    have e3 : 0 ≤ 2 * (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * cG L := mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (by norm_num) hn0) hc0
    linarith
  have hceil := (Nat.ceil_lt_add_one ht0').le
  have hD := (Nat.ceil_lt_add_one (a := K.DI * NplusF L.sc (addOrderOf (hP D p)) * (L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ^ 2 *
      ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3) * Real.logb p L.sc.a)
    (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hDI hNp0) (by positivity)) (by positivity)) hlp)).le
  unfold NplusF at hD
  have hDD : (DIf K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) : ℝ) ≤ K.DI * (40 * (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1) *
      (L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ^ 2 * ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3) * Real.logb p L.sc.a + 1 := hD
  have hs : (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (3 * cG L * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a)) ≤
      (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (C * (cG L + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a)) := by
    apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left _ hN0
    apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right _ (by linarith)
    have h1 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right Nn.hC3 hc0
    have h2 : 0 ≤ C * L.etaSz := mul_nonneg (by linarith [Nn.hC3]) hη.le
    linarith
  unfold StarAt
  rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib, Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul, lo_E K L C bo hc h0 hX, hPr, htv, hDp]
  have hcard : ((topsOn X p t0).card : ℝ) = (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) := rfl
  rw [hcard]
  linarith [hgl, hkk, har, hceil, hDD, hs, htIf]

theorem starAll_of (X : Inst D L) {K : Consts} (hK : K.Nonneg) (P : ParameterChoice' D L) {C31 C bo : ℝ}
    (A1 : adm1 D L X C31) (hC31 : 0 < C31) (Nn : NumStar D L K C31 C bo) : StarAll X K C bo := by
  intro a b hW p hcand t0 ht0 j hj
  rcases lt_or_ge (p : ℝ) (L.sc.p0 D.R.k) with h0 | h0
  · exact starAt_D X hK P A1 hC31 Nn hW hcand h0 ht0 hj
  · exact starAt_E X hK P A1 hC31 Nn hW hcand h0 ht0 hj

end Formalism.MainNotions.AuxBlock8.Pf.St

end


[GLG_uniform_star_01] both original adm5 exponents

Range SHA256: 75fa383ec38ecb08ba75ea39961c9fc0124ee6c219461c2f8afaa47c765fec49
def adm5 (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) (δ : ℝ) : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps - δ) ≤ La K L.sc ∧ La K L.sc ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps + δ) ∧
  ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime →
    ((p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k → Real.log (Qp L.sc p) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8 + δ)) ∧
    ((p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' → Real.log (Qp L.sc p) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (11 * L.sc.eps / 24 + δ))



[GLG_uniform_star_02] explicit CStar, small budgets and eventual NumStar
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/LogarithmicSums/StarNum.lean#L15-L327
Range SHA256: 8a07335aab65bdecca6c71908e584a0ad7ed5b88273d8c198408b6d1f4003d5d
  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos hxa] at hx
  have hc : |c| * (1 / (|c| + 1)) ≤ 1 := by
    rw [mul_one_div, div_le_one (by positivity)]
    linarith
  calc c * Real.log x ≤ |c| * |Real.log x| := by rw [← abs_mul]; exact le_abs_self _
    _ ≤ |c| * (1 / (|c| + 1) * x ^ α) := by gcongr
    _ = (|c| * (1 / (|c| + 1))) * x ^ α := by ring
    _ ≤ 1 * x ^ α := by gcongr
    _ = x ^ α := one_mul _

theorem ev_pow (c α β : ℝ) (h : α < β) : ∀ᶠ x : ℝ in atTop, c * x ^ α ≤ x ^ β := by
  have ht := tendsto_rpow_atTop (sub_pos.2 h)
  filter_upwards [ht.eventually_ge_atTop c, eventually_gt_atTop 0] with x hx hx0
  have hsplit : x ^ β = x ^ α * x ^ (β - α) := by
    rw [← Real.rpow_add hx0]
    ring_nf
  rw [hsplit, mul_comm c]
  exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hx (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hx0 α).le

def rE (eps : ℝ) : ℕ := ⌈8 * (2 + 8 * eps + 2) / eps⌉₊ + 1

theorem rG_rE (s : Scale) : rG s = rE s.eps := rfl

def cGE (eps : ℝ) : ℝ := (((2 + 8 * eps + 3) * 10 ^ (6 * rE eps + 1) + 1) + 5) * (6 * (rE eps : ℝ) + 1) * 2

def cNE (eps : ℝ) : ℝ := 18 * cGE eps + 2 * (2 + 8 * eps + 3) + 2

theorem cGE_nonneg (eps : ℝ) (he : 0 < eps) : 0 ≤ cGE eps := by
  unfold cGE
  have h1 : 0 ≤ 2 + 8 * eps + 3 := by linarith
  have h2 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 10 ^ (6 * rE eps + 1) := by positivity
  have h3 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 6 * (rE eps : ℝ) + 1 := by positivity
  have h4 := mul_nonneg h1 h2
  exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (by linarith) h3) (by norm_num)

theorem inv_delta1 (s : Scale) : 1 / s.delta1 = s.x ^ (s.eps / 8) := by
  unfold Scale.delta1
  rw [Real.rpow_neg (le_of_lt (zero_lt_one.trans s.one_lt_alt)), one_div, inv_inv]

theorem one_le_xpow (s : Scale) (y : ℝ) (hy : 0 ≤ y) : 1 ≤ s.x ^ y :=
  Real.one_le_rpow s.one_lt_alt.le hy

theorem Gp_le (s : Scale) (hx : Real.exp 1 ≤ s.x) :
    (Gp s : ℝ) ≤ cGE s.eps * Real.log s.x * s.x ^ (s.eps / 8) := by
  have he := s.eps_pos
  have hL : 1 ≤ Real.log s.x := by
    rw [← Real.log_exp 1]
    exact Real.log_le_log (Real.exp_pos 1) hx
  have hy : 1 ≤ s.x ^ (s.eps / 8) := one_le_xpow s _ (by linarith)
  set r := rG s with hr
  set A : ℝ := (2 + 8 * s.eps + 3) * 10 ^ (6 * r + 1) with hA
  have hA0 : 0 ≤ A := mul_nonneg (by linarith) (by positivity)
  have hM : (orbitSmallMultiplicity r (hG s) s.x : ℝ) ≤ A * Real.log s.x + 1 := by
    unfold orbitSmallMultiplicity
    have harg : (hG s + 3) * Real.log s.x / (10 : ℝ) ^ (-((6 * r + 1 : ℕ) : ℤ)) =
        A * Real.log s.x := by
      rw [zpow_neg, zpow_natCast, div_inv_eq_mul, hA, hG]
      ring
    rw [harg]
    exact (Nat.ceil_lt_add_one (mul_nonneg hA0 (by linarith))).le
  have hE : (goodEll r s.delta1 : ℝ) ≤ (6 * (r : ℝ) + 1) * (2 * s.x ^ (s.eps / 8)) := by
    unfold goodEll goodU
    push_cast
    rw [inv_delta1]
    have hc := (Nat.ceil_lt_add_one (by linarith : (0 : ℝ) ≤ s.x ^ (s.eps / 8))).le
    have h6 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 6 * (r : ℝ) + 1 := by positivity
    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) h6
  have hc : cGE s.eps = (A + 1 + 5) * (6 * (r : ℝ) + 1) * 2 := rfl
  unfold Gp goodG'
  push_cast
  have h1 : A * Real.log s.x + 1 + 5 ≤ (A + 1 + 5) * Real.log s.x := by nlinarith
  have h2 : 0 ≤ (6 * (r : ℝ) + 1) * (2 * s.x ^ (s.eps / 8)) := by positivity
  calc ((orbitSmallMultiplicity r (hG s) s.x : ℝ) + 5) * (goodEll r s.delta1 : ℝ)
      ≤ (A * Real.log s.x + 1 + 5) * ((6 * (r : ℝ) + 1) * (2 * s.x ^ (s.eps / 8))) := by
        gcongr
    _ ≤ ((A + 1 + 5) * Real.log s.x) * ((6 * (r : ℝ) + 1) * (2 * s.x ^ (s.eps / 8))) :=
        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h1 h2
    _ = cGE s.eps * Real.log s.x * s.x ^ (s.eps / 8) := by rw [hc]; ring

theorem first_count_le_log (s : Scale) (hx : Real.exp 1 ≤ s.x) : (N1 s : ℝ) ≤ cNE s.eps * Real.log s.x := by
  have he := s.eps_pos
  have hx0 : 0 < s.x := lt_of_lt_of_le (Real.exp_pos 1) hx
  have hL : 1 ≤ Real.log s.x := by
    rw [← Real.log_exp 1]
    exact Real.log_le_log (Real.exp_pos 1) hx
  have hcg := cGE_nonneg s.eps he
  have hd0 : 0 < s.delta1 := by unfold Scale.delta1; exact Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hx0 _
  have hyd : s.x ^ (s.eps / 8) * s.delta1 = 1 := by
    rw [← inv_delta1, one_div, inv_mul_cancel₀ hd0.ne']
  have hGp := Gp_le s hx
  unfold Gp at hGp
  have hG1 : 6 * Real.exp 1 * (goodG' (rG s) s.delta1 (hG s) s.x : ℝ) * s.delta1 ≤
      18 * cGE s.eps * Real.log s.x := by
    have he3 : Real.exp 1 < 3 := lt_trans Real.exp_one_lt_d9 (by norm_num)
    have hstep : (goodG' (rG s) s.delta1 (hG s) s.x : ℝ) * s.delta1 ≤ cGE s.eps * Real.log s.x := by
      calc (goodG' (rG s) s.delta1 (hG s) s.x : ℝ) * s.delta1
          ≤ cGE s.eps * Real.log s.x * s.x ^ (s.eps / 8) * s.delta1 :=
            mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hGp hd0.le
        _ = cGE s.eps * Real.log s.x := by rw [mul_assoc _ (s.x ^ _), hyd, mul_one]
    have hcl : 0 ≤ cGE s.eps * Real.log s.x := mul_nonneg hcg (by linarith)
    nlinarith [Real.exp_pos 1]
  have hlog2 : 1 / 2 < Real.log 2 := lt_trans (by norm_num) Real.log_two_gt_d9
  have hG2 : (hG s + 3) * Real.logb 2 s.x ≤ 2 * (2 + 8 * s.eps + 3) * Real.log s.x := by
    unfold Real.logb hG
    have hl2 : 0 < Real.log 2 := by linarith
    rw [mul_div_assoc', div_le_iff₀ hl2]
    have hX : 0 < (2 + 8 * s.eps + 3) * Real.log s.x := mul_pos (by linarith) (by linarith)
    nlinarith [mul_pos hX (sub_pos.2 hlog2)]
  have hmax : max (6 * Real.exp 1 * (goodG' (rG s) s.delta1 (hG s) s.x : ℝ) * s.delta1)
      ((hG s + 3) * Real.logb 2 s.x) ≤ (18 * cGE s.eps + 2 * (2 + 8 * s.eps + 3)) * Real.log s.x := by
    refine max_le ?_ ?_
    · nlinarith
    · nlinarith
  have hnn : 0 ≤ max (6 * Real.exp 1 * (goodG' (rG s) s.delta1 (hG s) s.x : ℝ) * s.delta1)
      ((hG s + 3) * Real.logb 2 s.x) := le_max_of_le_left (by positivity)
  unfold N1 orbitWindowCount
  push_cast
  have hceil := (Nat.ceil_lt_add_one hnn).le
  unfold cNE
  nlinarith

theorem k_le_x_pow_six {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (hS : Formalism.MainNotions.SizesAt L) : (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (6 : ℝ) := by
  have hk := hS.2.2.1.2
  have he := L.sc.eps_lt
  have he0 := L.sc.eps_pos
  refine hk.trans (Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le L.sc.one_lt_alt.le ?_)
  unfold Formalism.MainNotions.szTh
  linarith

theorem one_le_k (D : D7) : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) := by
  have h := D.R.m_add_three_le_k
  exact_mod_cast (by omega : 1 ≤ D.R.k)

theorem xpow_le_exp (x N y : ℝ) (hx : 0 < x) (h : N * Real.log x ≤ y) : x ^ N ≤ Real.exp y := by
  rw [Real.rpow_def_of_pos hx]
  exact Real.exp_le_exp.2 (by rw [mul_comm]; exact h)

theorem second_level_lt_good_of_log {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (hS : Formalism.MainNotions.SizesAt L) (hx : Real.exp 1 ≤ L.sc.x)
    (h : 514 * Real.log L.sc.x ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8)) : L2 D L.sc < goodU L.sc.delta1 := by
  have hx0 : 0 < L.sc.x := lt_trans zero_lt_one L.sc.one_lt_alt
  have hL : 1 ≤ Real.log L.sc.x := by
    rw [← Real.log_exp 1]
    exact Real.log_le_log (Real.exp_pos 1) hx
  have hk1 := one_le_k D
  have hk6 := k_le_x_pow_six L hS
  have hlogk : Real.log (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ 6 * Real.log L.sc.x := by
    calc Real.log (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ Real.log (L.sc.x ^ (6 : ℝ)) := Real.log_le_log (by linarith) hk6
      _ = 6 * Real.log L.sc.x := by rw [Real.log_rpow hx0]
  have hlog2 := Real.log_two_gt_d9
  have hL1 : ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → L1 D L.sc p ≤ ⌈186 * Real.log L.sc.x⌉₊ := by
    intro p hp
    unfold L1
    apply Nat.ceil_mono
    have hp2 : (2 : ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast hp.two_le
    have hlp : Real.log 2 ≤ Real.log p := Real.log_le_log (by norm_num) hp2
    have hlp0 : 0 < Real.log p := by linarith
    have hk0 : (D.R.k : ℝ) ≠ 0 := ne_of_gt (by linarith)
    have hxA : L.sc.x ^ Scale.A ≠ 0 := (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hx0 _).ne'
    have hy : Real.log (((D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ Scale.A) ^ 4) =
        4 * (Real.log D.R.k + Scale.A * Real.log L.sc.x) := by
      rw [Real.log_pow, Real.log_mul hk0 hxA, Real.log_rpow hx0]
      push_cast
      ring
    have hy0 : 0 ≤ Real.log (D.R.k : ℝ) := Real.log_nonneg hk1
    have hA : Scale.A = 26 := rfl
    unfold Real.logb
    rw [hy, div_le_iff₀ hlp0, hA]
    have hLn : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 186 * Real.log L.sc.x := by linarith
    nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hlp hLn, mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hlog2.le hLn]
  have hSup : sSup {l | ∃ p : ℕ, p.Prime ∧ D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass ∧ l = L1 D L.sc p} ≤
      ⌈186 * Real.log L.sc.x⌉₊ := by
    apply csSup_le'
    rintro l ⟨p, hp, -, rfl⟩
    exact hL1 p hp
  have hL2 : L2 D L.sc ≤ 2 * ⌈186 * Real.log L.sc.x⌉₊ := by
    unfold L2
    exact Nat.mul_le_mul_left 2 hSup
  have hL2r : (L2 D L.sc : ℝ) ≤ 2 * (⌈186 * Real.log L.sc.x⌉₊ : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast hL2
  have hceil := Nat.ceil_lt_add_one (by linarith : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 186 * Real.log L.sc.x)
  have hU : L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8) ≤ (goodU L.sc.delta1 : ℝ) := by
    unfold goodU
    rw [inv_delta1]
    exact Nat.le_ceil _
  have hlt : (L2 D L.sc : ℝ) < goodU L.sc.delta1 := by linarith
  exact_mod_cast hlt

def CStar (eps : ℝ) (K : Consts) (C31 : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  3 + K.tII * (rE eps : ℝ) + (2 * (3 : ℝ) + 4 * K.tII + 160 * C31 + 7) + K.Pr1 * C31 + K.kappaThresholdOne * |cNE eps|

theorem CStar_nonneg (eps : ℝ) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (C31 : ℝ) (hC31 : 0 < C31) : 0 ≤ CStar eps K C31 := by
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, htII, -, -, -, -, htI1, -, -, -, -, hPr1, -⟩ := hK
  unfold CStar
  have a := mul_nonneg htII (Nat.cast_nonneg (α := ℝ) (rE eps))
  have b := mul_nonneg hPr1 hC31.le
  have c := mul_nonneg htI1 (abs_nonneg (cNE eps))
  linarith

def SmallB (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) (C31 bo : ℝ) : Prop :=
  (∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k → (D.R.k : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p ≤ L.sc.b') ∧
  (∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime →
    K.DII * ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 3 + (rG L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2) * Real.logb p L.sc.a ≤ L.sc.b') ∧
  (∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ) → (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' → ∀ n' : ℕ, n' ≤ D.R.n →
    K.Pr1 * (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) * (L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D) +
      K.kappaThresholdTwo * (Qp L.sc p * c6f K L p n' +
        (D.R.k : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p + (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.logb p L.sc.a + cG L) +
      2 * (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) * cG L +
      40 * C31 * Real.log L.sc.x * (K.DI * (L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ^ 2 *
        ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3) * Real.logb p L.sc.a) + 3 ≤ bo)

theorem numStar_ev (eps : ℝ) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (C31 : ℝ) (hC31 : 0 < C31) :
    ∀ᶠ x : ℝ in atTop, ∀ (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D), L.sc.x = x → L.sc.eps = eps → Formalism.MainNotions.SizesAt L →
      ParameterChoice' D L → ∀ bo : ℝ, SmallB D L K C31 bo → ∀ C : ℝ, CStar eps K C31 ≤ C → NumStar D L K C31 C bo := by
  by_cases heps : 0 < eps ∧ eps < 1 / 52
  swap
  · exact Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x D L _ hLe =>
      absurd ⟨hLe ▸ L.sc.eps_pos, hLe ▸ L.sc.eps_lt⟩ heps
  obtain ⟨he0, he1⟩ := heps
  have E1 : ∀ᶠ x : ℝ in atTop, Real.exp 1 ≤ x := eventually_ge_atTop _
  have E2 := ev_log 514 (eps / 8) (by linarith)
  have E3 := ev_log (3 * cGE eps) (eps / 8) (by linarith)
  have E4 := ev_log (4 * C31) (1 - eps / 8) (by linarith)
  have E5 : ∀ᶠ x : ℝ in atTop, (2 : ℝ) ≤ x := eventually_ge_atTop _
  filter_upwards [E1, E2, E3, E4, E5] with x h1 h2 h3 h4 h5
  intro D L hx hLe hS P bo hSB C hC
  subst hx
  subst hLe
  have hx0 : 0 < L.sc.x := sc_x_pos L.sc
  have hL : 1 ≤ Real.log L.sc.x := by
    rw [← Real.log_exp 1]
    exact Real.log_le_log (Real.exp_pos 1) h1
  have hy0 : 0 ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8) := (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hx0 _).le
  have hGp := Gp_le L.sc h1
  have hN1 := first_count_le_log L.sc h1
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, htII, -, -, -, -, htI1, -, -, -, -, hPr1, -⟩ := hK
  have hCS : CStar L.sc.eps K C31 ≤ C := hC
  unfold CStar at hCS
  have ha := mul_nonneg htII (Nat.cast_nonneg (α := ℝ) (rE L.sc.eps))
  have hb := mul_nonneg hPr1 hC31.le
  have hc := mul_nonneg htI1 (abs_nonneg (cNE L.sc.eps))
  obtain ⟨hw500, hcGw, hwb, hcG0, hScG⟩ := base P
  obtain ⟨-, hNmSc, -⟩ := Nm_le_Sc P
  have hn1 : 1 ≤ D.R.n := by
    have hexp : 0 ≤ 2 + 8 * L.sc.eps - Formalism.MainNotions.szTh L := by
      unfold Formalism.MainNotions.szTh
      linarith
    have h1n : (1 : ℝ) ≤ D.R.n := le_trans (Real.one_le_rpow L.sc.one_lt_alt.le hexp) hS.1.1
    exact_mod_cast h1n
  obtain ⟨hwx, -⟩ := w''_le L.sc D.R.n hn1
  have hb0 : 0 ≤ L.sc.b' := by linarith
  have hbx : L.sc.b' / L.sc.x ≤ L.sc.b' / 2 := div_le_div_of_nonneg_left hb0 (by norm_num) h5
  have hEta := hS.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.1
  have ht1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ D.R.t := by exact_mod_cast D.R.t_odd.pos
  have heta0 : 0 ≤ L.etaSz := by linarith [hEta.1]
  have heta : L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n := by
    have h4e := hEta.2.2.2.1
    have hwx' : L.sc.w'' D.R.n / L.sc.x ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n :=
      div_le_self (by linarith) L.sc.one_lt_alt.le
    nlinarith
  have hNm : Nm D ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n := by
    unfold Nm
    linarith
  have hS4 : L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D ≤ 4 * (L.sc.b' / L.sc.x) := by linarith
  have hkey : 4 * C31 * Real.log L.sc.x * L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8) ≤ L.sc.x := by
    calc 4 * C31 * Real.log L.sc.x * L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8)
        ≤ L.sc.x ^ (1 - L.sc.eps / 8) * L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8) :=
          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h4 hy0
      _ = L.sc.x := by rw [← Real.rpow_add hx0]; simp
  have hinv := inv_delta1 L.sc
  have hrG : (rG L.sc : ℝ) = (rE L.sc.eps : ℝ) := rfl
  exact
    { hL2 := second_level_lt_good_of_log L hS h1 h2
      h3G := by
        push_cast
        calc (3 : ℝ) * (Gp L.sc : ℝ) ≤ 3 * (cGE L.sc.eps * Real.log L.sc.x * L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8)) := by
              linarith
          _ = (3 * cGE L.sc.eps * Real.log L.sc.x) * L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8) := by ring
          _ ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8) * L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h3 hy0
          _ = L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) := by rw [← Real.rpow_add hx0]; ring_nf
      hlx := hL
      hGu := by
        have hpos : 0 ≤ C31 * Real.log L.sc.x * L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8) :=
          mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hC31.le (by linarith)) hy0
        calc C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 * (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D)
            = (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x * (1 / L.sc.delta1)) *
                (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D) := by ring
          _ = (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x * L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8)) *
                (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D) := by rw [hinv]
          _ ≤ (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x * L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8)) * (4 * (L.sc.b' / L.sc.x)) :=
              mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hS4 hpos
          _ = (4 * C31 * Real.log L.sc.x * L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8)) * L.sc.b' / L.sc.x := by ring
          _ ≤ L.sc.x * L.sc.b' / L.sc.x := by gcongr
          _ = L.sc.b' := by field_simp
      hc0 := by
        unfold c0num
        linarith
      hkQ := hSB.1
      hDII := hSB.2.1
      hC3 := by linarith
      hCt := by rw [hrG]; linarith
      hCD := by linarith
      hCP := by linarith
      hN1 := by
        have hN1' : (N1 L.sc : ℝ) ≤ |cNE L.sc.eps| * Real.log L.sc.x :=
          hN1.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (le_abs_self _) (by linarith))
        calc K.kappaThresholdOne * (N1 L.sc : ℝ) ≤ K.kappaThresholdOne * (|cNE L.sc.eps| * Real.log L.sc.x) :=
              mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hN1' htI1
          _ = (K.kappaThresholdOne * |cNE L.sc.eps|) * Real.log L.sc.x := by ring
          _ ≤ C * Real.log L.sc.x := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) (by linarith)
      hbo := hSB.2.2 }

end Formalism.MainNotions.AuxBlock8.Pf.St

[GLG_uniform_star_03] N1/Gp ceilings, full polynomial budget and uniform facts
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/LogarithmicSums/StarNumB.lean#L12-L359
Range SHA256: 6b05d75930bba5aa4be6fb0b427d25ecd461679509f8a18b491590a7ecb551da

def cNE (eps : ℝ) : ℝ := 18 * cGE eps + 2 * (2 + 8 * eps + 3) + 2

theorem cGE_nonneg (eps : ℝ) (he : 0 < eps) : 0 ≤ cGE eps := by unfold cGE; positivity

theorem inv_delta1 (s : Scale) : 1 / s.delta1 = s.x ^ (s.eps / 8) := by
  unfold Scale.delta1
  rw [Real.rpow_neg (sc_x_pos s).le, one_div, inv_inv]

theorem one_le_xpow (s : Scale) (y : ℝ) (hy : 0 ≤ y) : 1 ≤ s.x ^ y := Real.one_le_rpow s.one_lt_alt.le hy

theorem Gp_le (s : Scale) (hx : Real.exp 1 ≤ s.x) :
    (Gp s : ℝ) ≤ cGE s.eps * Real.log s.x * s.x ^ (s.eps / 8) := by
  have he := s.eps_pos
  have hx0 := sc_x_pos s
  have lx1 : 1 ≤ Real.log s.x := (Real.le_log_iff_exp_le hx0).2 hx
  have hy1 : 1 ≤ s.x ^ (s.eps / 8) := one_le_xpow s _ (by positivity)
  have hU : ((goodU s.delta1 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ 2 * s.x ^ (s.eps / 8) := by
    unfold goodU
    rw [inv_delta1]
    have := Nat.ceil_lt_add_one (le_trans zero_le_one hy1)
    linarith
  have hM : ((orbitSmallMultiplicity (rG s) (hG s) s.x : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ ((2 + 8 * s.eps + 3) * 10 ^ (6 * rE s.eps + 1) + 1) * Real.log s.x := by
    unfold orbitSmallMultiplicity hG
    rw [rG_rE, zpow_neg, div_inv_eq_mul, zpow_natCast]
    have h0 : 0 ≤ (2 + 8 * s.eps + 3) * Real.log s.x * 10 ^ (6 * rE s.eps + 1) := by positivity
    have := Nat.ceil_lt_add_one h0
    nlinarith
  unfold Gp goodG' goodEll
  push_cast
  rw [rG_rE] at hM ⊢
  unfold cGE
  have h5 : ((orbitSmallMultiplicity (rE s.eps) (hG s) s.x : ℕ) : ℝ) + 5 ≤
      (((2 + 8 * s.eps + 3) * 10 ^ (6 * rE s.eps + 1) + 1) + 5) * Real.log s.x := by nlinarith
  have h6 : (6 * (rE s.eps : ℝ) + 1) * ((goodU s.delta1 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ (6 * (rE s.eps : ℝ) + 1) * (2 * s.x ^ (s.eps / 8)) :=
    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hU (by positivity)
  calc (((orbitSmallMultiplicity (rE s.eps) (hG s) s.x : ℕ) : ℝ) + 5) * ((6 * (rE s.eps : ℝ) + 1) * ((goodU s.delta1 : ℕ) : ℝ))
      ≤ ((((2 + 8 * s.eps + 3) * 10 ^ (6 * rE s.eps + 1) + 1) + 5) * Real.log s.x) *
          ((6 * (rE s.eps : ℝ) + 1) * (2 * s.x ^ (s.eps / 8))) :=
        mul_le_mul h5 h6 (by positivity) (by positivity)
    _ = _ := by ring

theorem first_count_le_log (s : Scale) (hx : Real.exp 1 ≤ s.x) : (N1 s : ℝ) ≤ cNE s.eps * Real.log s.x := by
  have he := s.eps_pos
  have hx0 := sc_x_pos s
  have lx1 : 1 ≤ Real.log s.x := (Real.le_log_iff_exp_le hx0).2 hx
  have hGp := Gp_le s hx
  have hc := cGE_nonneg s.eps he
  have hd : s.x ^ (s.eps / 8) * s.delta1 = 1 := by
    unfold Scale.delta1
    rw [← Real.rpow_add hx0]; simp
  have hd0 := sc_delta1_pos s
  have h1 : (Gp s : ℝ) * s.delta1 ≤ cGE s.eps * Real.log s.x := by
    calc (Gp s : ℝ) * s.delta1 ≤ cGE s.eps * Real.log s.x * s.x ^ (s.eps / 8) * s.delta1 :=
          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hGp hd0.le
      _ = cGE s.eps * Real.log s.x := by rw [mul_assoc, hd, mul_one]
  have he3 : Real.exp 1 ≤ 3 := by have := Real.exp_one_lt_d9; linarith
  have h0 : 0 ≤ (Gp s : ℝ) * s.delta1 := by positivity
  have h2 : 6 * Real.exp 1 * (Gp s : ℝ) * s.delta1 ≤ 18 * cGE s.eps * Real.log s.x := by
    have : 6 * Real.exp 1 * ((Gp s : ℝ) * s.delta1) ≤ 18 * ((Gp s : ℝ) * s.delta1) :=
      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) h0
    nlinarith
  have h3 : (hG s + 3) * Real.logb 2 s.x ≤ 2 * (2 + 8 * s.eps + 3) * Real.log s.x := by
    unfold Formalism.MainNotions.G.hG Real.logb
    have hl2 : (1 / 2 : ℝ) ≤ Real.log 2 := by have := Real.log_two_gt_d9; linarith
    have : Real.log s.x / Real.log 2 ≤ 2 * Real.log s.x := by
      rw [div_le_iff₀ (by linarith)]
      nlinarith
    have h4 : 0 ≤ 2 + 8 * s.eps + 3 := by positivity
    nlinarith
  have hmax : max (6 * Real.exp 1 * (Gp s : ℝ) * s.delta1) ((hG s + 3) * Real.logb 2 s.x) ≤
      (18 * cGE s.eps + 2 * (2 + 8 * s.eps + 3)) * Real.log s.x := by
    have a1 : 0 ≤ 18 * cGE s.eps * Real.log s.x := by positivity
    have a2 : 0 ≤ 2 * (2 + 8 * s.eps + 3) * Real.log s.x := by positivity
    apply max_le <;> nlinarith
  have hpos : 0 ≤ max (6 * Real.exp 1 * (Gp s : ℝ) * s.delta1) ((hG s + 3) * Real.logb 2 s.x) :=
    le_max_of_le_left (by positivity)
  have hceil := Nat.ceil_lt_add_one hpos
  have hN : (N1 s : ℝ) = (⌈max (6 * Real.exp 1 * (Gp s : ℝ) * s.delta1) ((hG s + 3) * Real.logb 2 s.x)⌉₊ : ℝ) + 1 := by
    unfold N1 orbitWindowCount Gp
    push_cast
    rfl
  rw [hN]
  unfold cNE
  nlinarith

theorem hboB_arith (Y Pr1 G η c Nm kappaThresholdTwo Q DIf N k lb n' C31 lx DI v N1 Np : ℝ) (hY : 130 ≤ Y)
    (a1 : 0 ≤ Pr1) (b1 : Pr1 ≤ Y) (a2 : 0 ≤ G) (b2 : G ≤ Y) (a3 : 0 ≤ η) (b3 : η ≤ Y) (a4 : 0 ≤ c) (b4 : c ≤ Y)
    (a5 : 0 ≤ Nm) (b5 : Nm ≤ Y) (a6 : 0 ≤ kappaThresholdTwo) (b6 : kappaThresholdTwo ≤ Y) (a7 : 0 ≤ Q) (b7 : Q ≤ Y) (a8 : 0 ≤ DI) (b8 : DI ≤ Y)
    (a9 : 0 ≤ N) (b9 : N ≤ Y) (a10 : 0 ≤ k) (b10 : k ≤ Y) (a11 : 0 ≤ lb) (b11 : lb ≤ Y) (a12 : 0 ≤ n') (b12 : n' ≤ Y)
    (a13 : 0 ≤ C31) (b13 : C31 ≤ Y) (a14 : 0 ≤ lx) (b14 : lx ≤ Y) (a15 : 0 ≤ v) (b15 : v ≤ Y) (a16 : 0 ≤ N1)
    (b16 : N1 ≤ Y) (a17 : 0 ≤ Np) (b17 : Np ≤ Y) (a18 : 0 ≤ DIf)
    (hD : DIf ≤ DI * Np * v ^ 2 * (N1 ^ 5 + k ^ 3) * lb + 1) :
    Pr1 * G * (η + c + Nm) + kappaThresholdTwo * (Q * (8 * (DIf + 1) + kappaThresholdTwo * N * k * lb + 4 * N * c + 2 * n' * c) + k * Q +
      k * lb + c) + 2 * G * c + 40 * C31 * lx * (DI * v ^ 2 * (N1 ^ 5 + k ^ 3) * lb) + 3 ≤ Y ^ 13 := by
  have hY0 : 0 ≤ Y := by linarith
  have hY1 : 1 ≤ Y := by linarith
  have hD' : DIf ≤ Y * Y * Y ^ 2 * (Y ^ 5 + Y ^ 3) * Y + 1 := le_trans hD (by gcongr)
  have h1 : Pr1 * G * (η + c + Nm) + kappaThresholdTwo * (Q * (8 * (DIf + 1) + kappaThresholdTwo * N * k * lb + 4 * N * c + 2 * n' * c) + k * Q +
      k * lb + c) + 2 * G * c + 40 * C31 * lx * (DI * v ^ 2 * (N1 ^ 5 + k ^ 3) * lb) + 3 ≤
      Y * Y * (Y + Y + Y) + Y * (Y * (8 * ((Y * Y * Y ^ 2 * (Y ^ 5 + Y ^ 3) * Y + 1) + 1) + Y * Y * Y * Y + 4 * Y * Y +
        2 * Y * Y) + Y * Y + Y * Y + Y) + 2 * Y * Y + 40 * Y * Y * (Y * Y ^ 2 * (Y ^ 5 + Y ^ 3) * Y) + 3 := by gcongr
  have h2 : Y * Y * (Y + Y + Y) + Y * (Y * (8 * ((Y * Y * Y ^ 2 * (Y ^ 5 + Y ^ 3) * Y + 1) + 1) + Y * Y * Y * Y + 4 * Y * Y +
        2 * Y * Y) + Y * Y + Y * Y + Y) + 2 * Y * Y + 40 * Y * Y * (Y * Y ^ 2 * (Y ^ 5 + Y ^ 3) * Y) + 3 =
      8 * Y ^ 12 + 40 * Y ^ 11 + 8 * Y ^ 10 + 40 * Y ^ 9 + Y ^ 6 + 6 * Y ^ 4 + 5 * Y ^ 3 + 19 * Y ^ 2 + 3 := by ring
  have p : ∀ i : ℕ, i ≤ 12 → Y ^ i ≤ Y ^ 12 := fun i hi => pow_le_pow_right₀ hY1 hi
  have p11 := p 11 (by norm_num)
  have p10 := p 10 (by norm_num)
  have p9 := p 9 (by norm_num)
  have p6 := p 6 (by norm_num)
  have p4 := p 4 (by norm_num)
  have p3 := p 3 (by norm_num)
  have p2 := p 2 (by norm_num)
  have p0 : 1 ≤ Y ^ 12 := one_le_pow₀ hY1
  have p13 : 130 * Y ^ 12 ≤ Y ^ 13 := by
    calc 130 * Y ^ 12 ≤ Y * Y ^ 12 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hY (by positivity)
      _ = Y ^ 13 := by ring
  linarith

theorem hDII_arith (Y DII N1 r k lb : ℝ) (hY : 130 ≤ Y) (a1 : 0 ≤ DII) (b1 : DII ≤ Y) (a2 : 0 ≤ N1) (b2 : N1 ≤ Y)
    (a3 : 0 ≤ r) (b3 : r ≤ Y) (a4 : 0 ≤ k) (b4 : k ≤ Y) (a5 : 0 ≤ lb) (b5 : lb ≤ Y) :
    DII * (N1 ^ 3 + r ^ 5 + k ^ 2) * lb ≤ Y ^ 8 := by
  have hY0 : 0 ≤ Y := by linarith
  have hY1 : 1 ≤ Y := by linarith
  have h1 : DII * (N1 ^ 3 + r ^ 5 + k ^ 2) * lb ≤ Y * (Y ^ 3 + Y ^ 5 + Y ^ 2) * Y := by gcongr
  have h2 : Y * (Y ^ 3 + Y ^ 5 + Y ^ 2) * Y = Y ^ 5 + Y ^ 7 + Y ^ 4 := by ring
  have p5 : Y ^ 5 ≤ Y ^ 7 := pow_le_pow_right₀ hY1 (by norm_num)
  have p4 : Y ^ 4 ≤ Y ^ 7 := pow_le_pow_right₀ hY1 (by norm_num)
  have p8 : 130 * Y ^ 7 ≤ Y ^ 8 := by
    calc 130 * Y ^ 7 ≤ Y * Y ^ 7 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hY (by positivity)
      _ = Y ^ 8 := by ring
  have : 0 ≤ Y ^ 7 := by positivity
  linarith

structure Facts (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) (C31 bo : ℝ) : Prop where
  hkQ : ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k → (D.R.k : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p ≤ L.sc.b'
  hDII : ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime →
    K.DII * ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 3 + (rG L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2) * Real.logb p L.sc.a ≤ L.sc.b'
  hbo : ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ) → (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' → ∀ n' : ℕ, n' ≤ D.R.n →
    K.Pr1 * (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) * (L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D) +
      K.kappaThresholdTwo * (Qp L.sc p * c6f K L p n' +
        (D.R.k : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p + (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.logb p L.sc.a + cG L) +
      2 * (C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) * cG L +
      40 * C31 * Real.log L.sc.x * (K.DI * (L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ^ 2 *
        ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3) * Real.logb p L.sc.a) + 3 ≤ bo

theorem facts_ev (eps : ℝ) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (C31 : ℝ) (hC31 : 0 < C31) :
    ∀ᶠ x : ℝ in atTop, ∀ (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D), L.sc.x = x → L.sc.eps = eps → Formalism.MainNotions.SizesAt L →
      ParameterChoice' D L → adm5 D L K (eps / 48) → Facts D L K C31 (L.sc.b ^ (x ^ (-(eps / 2)))) := by
  by_cases he : 0 < eps ∧ eps < 1 / 52
  swap
  · refine Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x D L _ h2 => ?_
    exact absurd ⟨h2 ▸ L.sc.eps_pos, h2 ▸ L.sc.eps_lt⟩ he
  obtain ⟨he0, he1⟩ := he
  have hτ : 0 < 23 * eps / 48 := by positivity
  have F1 := ev_log 14 (23 * eps / 48) hτ
  have F2 := ev_const 130 (23 * eps / 48) hτ
  have G1 := ev_const K.Pr1 (23 * eps / 48) hτ
  have G2 := ev_const K.kappaThresholdTwo (23 * eps / 48) hτ
  have G3 := ev_const K.DI (23 * eps / 48) hτ
  have G4 := ev_const K.DII (23 * eps / 48) hτ
  have G5 := ev_const C31 (23 * eps / 48) hτ
  have G6 := ev_const (rE eps : ℝ) (23 * eps / 48) hτ
  have F4 := ev_log 1 eps he0
  have F5 := ev_pow 3 eps 1 (by linarith)
  have F6 := ev_pow 14 (23 * eps / 48) eps (by linarith)
  have F7 := ev_pow 4 (eps / 3) eps (by linarith)
  have F8 := ev_pow 13 (23 * eps / 48) (eps / 2) (by linarith)
  have F9 := ev_log C31 1 one_pos
  have WorkedExample := ev_log (cNE eps) 1 one_pos
  filter_upwards [F1, F2, G1, G2, G3, G4, G5, G6, F4, F5, F6, F7, F8, F9, WorkedExample, eventually_ge_atTop 40,
    eventually_ge_atTop (Real.exp 1)] with x f1 f2 g1 g2 g3 g4 g5 g6 f4 f5 f6 f7 f8 f9 f10 hx40 hxe D L hx heps hS P h5
  subst hx
  subst heps
  obtain ⟨_, _, _, _, hDII0, _, _, hDI0, _, htI20, _, _, _, hPr10, _⟩ := hK
  have hx0 := sc_x_pos L.sc
  have hx1 := L.sc.one_lt_alt
  have hlx0 : 0 ≤ Real.log L.sc.x := Real.log_nonneg hx1.le
  have hk1 := one_le_k D
  have hk6 := k_le_x_pow_six L hS
  have hk0 : (0 : ℝ) < (D.R.k : ℝ) := by linarith
  obtain ⟨_, hNmSc, hSc100⟩ := Nm_le_Sc P
  obtain ⟨hSN, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, hSEta, -, -, hSDel, -⟩ := hS
  unfold Formalism.MainNotions.SizeN Formalism.MainNotions.szTh at hSN
  unfold Formalism.MainNotions.SizeEta at hSEta
  unfold Formalism.MainNotions.SizeDel at hSDel
  rw [Real.rpow_one] at f5 f9 f10
  have mono : ∀ u v : ℝ, u ≤ v → L.sc.x ^ u ≤ L.sc.x ^ v := fun u v h => Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le hx1.le h
  have nat3 : L.sc.x ^ (3 : ℝ) = L.sc.x ^ 3 := by rw [show (3 : ℝ) = ((3 : ℕ) : ℝ) by norm_num, Real.rpow_natCast]
  have nat6 : L.sc.x ^ (6 : ℝ) = L.sc.x ^ 6 := by rw [show (6 : ℝ) = ((6 : ℕ) : ℝ) by norm_num, Real.rpow_natCast]
  have natA2 : L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) = L.sc.x ^ 13 := by
    unfold Scale.A; rw [show (26 / 2 : ℝ) = ((13 : ℕ) : ℝ) by norm_num, Real.rpow_natCast]
  have hn3 : (D.R.n : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ 3 := by rw [← nat3]; exact le_trans hSN.2 (mono _ _ (by linarith))
  rw [nat6] at hk6
  have hxe1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps := Real.one_le_rpow hx1.le he0.le
  have hb : L.sc.b = Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps) := rfl
  have ha : L.sc.a = L.sc.x * L.sc.b := rfl
  have hY130 : 130 ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := f2
  have hY0 : 0 < (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := Real.exp_pos _
  have pY : ∀ q : ℝ, ∀ M : ℕ, q ≤ L.sc.x ^ M → M ≤ 14 → q ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := by
    intro q M hq hM
    refine le_trans hq ?_
    rw [← Real.rpow_natCast]
    apply xpow_le_exp _ _ _ hx0
    have : (M : ℝ) ≤ 14 := by exact_mod_cast hM
    exact le_trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right this hlx0) f1
  have e_eta : L.etaSz ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) :=
    calc L.etaSz ≤ Real.exp (Real.log L.etaSz) := Real.le_exp_log _
      _ ≤ Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4)) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 hSEta.2.2.1
      _ ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 (mono _ _ (by linarith))
  have hT4 : L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) ≤ (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48)) := mono _ _ (by linarith)
  have e_Del : (D.s.Del : ℝ) ≤ Real.exp ((L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48)) / 2) :=
    calc (D.s.Del : ℝ) ≤ Real.exp (Real.log (D.s.Del : ℝ)) := Real.le_exp_log _
      _ ≤ Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) / 2) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 hSDel.2.2.1
      _ ≤ _ := Real.exp_le_exp.2 (by linarith)
  have hDel0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ) := by
    have h := L.Sc_eq
    by_contra hneg
    have : (D.R.k : ℝ) * (4 * (D.s.Del : ℝ)) ≤ 0 := mul_nonpos_of_nonneg_of_nonpos hk0.le (by linarith)
    linarith
  have h7 : L.sc.x * L.sc.x ^ 6 ≤ Real.exp ((L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48)) / 2) := by
    have : L.sc.x * L.sc.x ^ 6 = L.sc.x ^ ((7 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by rw [Real.rpow_natCast]; ring
    rw [this]
    apply xpow_le_exp _ _ _ hx0
    push_cast
    linarith

          mul_le_mul (mul_le_mul (by linarith) hk6 hk0.le hx0.le) e_Del hDel0 (by positivity)

      _ = (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := by rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
  have e_Nm : Nm D ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := by
    have : Nm D ≤ cG L := by unfold Nm cG; linarith
    linarith
  have hNm0 : 0 ≤ Nm D := by unfold Nm; exact Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have e_n : (D.R.n : ℝ) ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := pY _ 3 hn3 (by norm_num)
  have e_k : (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := pY _ 6 hk6 (by norm_num)
  have e_v : L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := pY _ 13 (le_of_eq natA2) (by norm_num)
  have hxY : L.sc.x ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := pY _ 1 (by rw [pow_one]) (by norm_num)
  have e_lx : Real.log L.sc.x ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := by linarith
  have hla : Real.log L.sc.a = Real.log L.sc.x + L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps := by
    rw [ha, hb, Real.log_mul hx0.ne' (Real.exp_pos _).ne', Real.log_exp]
  have hla0 : 0 ≤ Real.log L.sc.a := by rw [hla]; positivity
  have hlaX : 2 * Real.log L.sc.a ≤ L.sc.x ^ 2 := by
    rw [hla]
    have : 2 * L.sc.x ≤ L.sc.x ^ 2 := by rw [pow_two]; exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) hx0.le
    linarith
  have e_lb : ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → 0 ≤ Real.logb p L.sc.a ∧ Real.logb p L.sc.a ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := by

    refine pY _ 2 (le_trans ?_ hlaX) (by norm_num)
    rw [div_le_iff₀ (by linarith)]
    calc Real.log L.sc.a = 2 * Real.log L.sc.a * (1 / 2) := by ring
      _ ≤ 2 * Real.log L.sc.a * Real.log p := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hlp (by linarith)
  have hd : 1 / L.sc.delta1 = L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8) := inv_delta1 L.sc
  have h8 : L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8) ≤ L.sc.x := by
    have := mono (L.sc.eps / 8) 1 (by linarith)
    rwa [Real.rpow_one] at this
  have h80 : 0 ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8) := Real.rpow_nonneg hx0.le _
  have hG0 : 0 ≤ C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 := div_nonneg (mul_nonneg hC31.le hlx0) (sc_delta1_pos L.sc).le
  have e_G : C31 * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := by

      _ = C31 * Real.log L.sc.x * L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8) := by rw [hd]
      _ ≤ L.sc.x * L.sc.x := mul_le_mul f9 h8 h80 hx0.le
      _ = L.sc.x ^ 2 := by ring
  have e_N1 : (N1 L.sc : ℝ) ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := le_trans (le_trans (first_count_le_log L.sc hxe) f10) hxY
  have hd1 : L.sc.delta1 ≤ 1 := by
    unfold Scale.delta1
    exact Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos hx1.le (by linarith)
  have e_Q : ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' → Qp L.sc p ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := by
    intro p hp hpX
    calc Qp L.sc p ≤ Real.exp (Real.log (Qp L.sc p)) := Real.le_exp_log _
      _ ≤ Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (11 * L.sc.eps / 24 + L.sc.eps / 48)) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 ((h5.2.2 p hp).2 hpX)
      _ ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 (mono _ _ (by linarith))
  have e_Q0 : ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k → Qp L.sc p ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := by
    intro p hp hpX
    calc Qp L.sc p ≤ Real.exp (Real.log (Qp L.sc p)) := Real.le_exp_log _
      _ ≤ Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8 + L.sc.eps / 48)) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 ((h5.2.2 p hp).1 hpX)
      _ ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 (mono _ _ (by linarith))
  have hQ0 : ∀ p : ℕ, 0 ≤ Qp L.sc p := fun p => by unfold Qp; positivity
  have e8 : (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) ^ 8 ≤ L.sc.b' := by
    have h2 : L.sc.b' = L.sc.x * Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps - L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 3)) := by
      unfold Scale.b' Scale.a Scale.b Scale.X' Scale.L
      rw [Real.exp_sub]; ring
    rw [h2, ← Real.exp_nat_mul]
    calc Real.exp (((8 : ℕ) : ℝ) * (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) ≤ Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps - L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 3)) := by
          apply Real.exp_le_exp.2
          push_cast
          linarith
      _ = 1 * Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps - L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 3)) := by ring
      _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hx1.le (Real.exp_pos _).le
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro p hp hp0
    have h1 : (D.R.k : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) * (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := mul_le_mul e_k (e_Q0 p hp hp0) (hQ0 p) hY0.le
    have h2 : (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) * (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) ^ 8 := by
      rw [← pow_two]; exact pow_le_pow_right₀ (by linarith) (by norm_num)
    linarith
  · intro p hp
    rw [rG_rE]
    exact le_trans (hDII_arith (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) K.DII _ _ _ _ hY130 hDII0 g4 (Nat.cast_nonneg _) e_N1 (Nat.cast_nonneg _) g6 hk0.le e_k
      (e_lb p hp).1 (e_lb p hp).2) e8
  · intro p hp _ hpX n' hn'
    have hbo : L.sc.b ^ (L.sc.x ^ (-(L.sc.eps / 2))) = Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 2)) := by
      rw [hb, ← Real.exp_mul, ← Real.rpow_add hx0]
      congr 2
      ring
    rw [hbo]
    have hn'R : (n' : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.n : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast hn'
    have hNp0 : 0 ≤ NplusF L.sc n' := by
      unfold NplusF; exact mul_nonneg (by positivity) (sc_delta1_pos L.sc).le
    have hNp : NplusF L.sc n' ≤ (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) := by
      refine pY _ 4 ?_ (by norm_num)
      unfold NplusF
      calc 40 * (n' : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1 ≤ L.sc.x * L.sc.x ^ 3 * 1 :=
            mul_le_mul (mul_le_mul hx40 (le_trans hn'R hn3) (Nat.cast_nonneg _) hx0.le) hd1
              (sc_delta1_pos L.sc).le (by positivity)
        _ = L.sc.x ^ 4 := by ring
    have hval0 : 0 ≤ K.DI * NplusF L.sc n' * (L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ^ 2 * ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3) *
        Real.logb p L.sc.a :=
      mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hDI0 hNp0) (by positivity)) (by positivity)) (e_lb p hp).1
    have hDIf : (DIf K L p n' : ℝ) ≤ K.DI * NplusF L.sc n' * (L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ^ 2 *
        ((N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 5 + (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3) * Real.logb p L.sc.a + 1 := by
      unfold DIf
      exact (Nat.ceil_lt_add_one hval0).le
    have h13 : (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) ^ 13 ≤ Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 2)) := by
      rw [← Real.exp_nat_mul]
      apply Real.exp_le_exp.2
      push_cast
      exact f8
    refine le_trans ?_ h13
    unfold c6f
    exact hboB_arith (Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (23 * L.sc.eps / 48))) K.Pr1 _ L.etaSz (cG L) (Nm D) K.kappaThresholdTwo (Qp L.sc p) _ (NplusF L.sc n') (D.R.k : ℝ) _ (n' : ℝ) C31 _ K.DI _
      (N1 L.sc : ℝ) (NplusF L.sc n') hY130 hPr10 g1 hG0 e_G L.etaSz_pos.le e_eta hcG0 e_c hNm0 e_Nm htI20 g2 (hQ0 p)
      (e_Q p hp hpX) hDI0 g3 hNp0 hNp hk0.le e_k (e_lb p hp).1 (e_lb p hp).2
      (Nat.cast_nonneg _) (le_trans hn'R e_n) hC31.le g5 hlx0 e_lx (Real.rpow_nonneg hx0.le _) e_v (Nat.cast_nonneg _) e_N1
      hNp0 hNp (Nat.cast_nonneg _) hDIf

end Formalism.MainNotions.AuxBlock8.Pf.St.B

[GLG_uniform_star_04] whole original star_fam quantifier order
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/LogarithmicSums/StarFam.lean#L10-L40
Range SHA256: 3e5bd30ad5026ad6ed62115d454e13c33a51a234cc8854457f4393f5454caaba

theorem star_fam {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (C31 Ce Cm : ℝ) (hC31 : 0 < C31) :
    ∃ C0 : ℝ, 0 ≤ C0 ∧ ∀ C ≥ C0, ∀ (X : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), Inst (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i))
      (Y : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), LatInst (Fm.D x i)), AdmFam Fm K X Y C31 Ce Cm →
      ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, StarAll (X x i) K C ((Fm.L x i).sc.b ^ (x ^ (-(eps / 2)))) := by
  have heps : 0 < eps := by
    obtain ⟨i0⟩ := Fm.Ix_zero
    rw [← Fm.L_eps 0 i0]
    exact (Fm.L 0 i0).sc.eps_pos
  refine ⟨max (CStar eps K C31) 0, le_max_right _ _, fun C hC X Y hAdm => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨x2, -, hx2⟩ := hAdm (eps / 48) (by linarith)
  obtain ⟨x3, hx3⟩ := Filter.eventually_atTop.1
    ((numStar_ev eps K hK C31 hC31).and (B.facts_ev eps K hK C31 hC31))
  refine ⟨max (max x2 x3) (Fm.x1 + 1), fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  have h1 := le_max_left (max x2 x3) (Fm.x1 + 1)
  have h2 := le_max_right (max x2 x3) (Fm.x1 + 1)
  have h3 := le_max_left x2 x3
  have h4 := le_max_right x2 x3
  have hxx1 : Fm.x1 < x := by linarith
  have hP := Fm.params x hxx1 i
  have hS := Fm.sizes x hxx1 i
  obtain ⟨A1, -, -, -, A5, -⟩ := hx2 x (by linarith) i
  have hx3' := hx3 x (by linarith)
  have hF := hx3'.2 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.L_x x i hxx1) (Fm.L_eps x i) hS hP A5
  have hN := hx3'.1 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.L_x x i hxx1) (Fm.L_eps x i) hS hP _
    ⟨hF.hkQ, hF.hDII, hF.hbo⟩ C (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hC)
  exact starAll_of (X x i) hK hP A1 hC31 hN

end Formalism.MainNotions.AuxBlock8.Pf.St

end


[GLG_gain_and_count_01] GainBoundConstants
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/LogarithmicSums/NotSingularOfCentral.lean#L13-L18
Range SHA256: f9ce08e4406c79e04e331802904db833dfa661268a717c9cd8fc741dec947110
structure GainBoundConstants (K : Consts) (Ce C bo : ℝ) : Prop where
  C3 : 3 ≤ C
  Cdl1 : 2 * K.dl1 + Ce ≤ C
  Cdl2 : 4 * K.dl2 ≤ C
  bo0 : 0 ≤ bo



[GLG_gain_and_count_02] representative partition and exact sum of losses
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/LogarithmicSums/NotSingularOfCentral.lean#L209-L350
Range SHA256: 4a1879866ce3a27c48549d4c06833d08c4de9ca03407c7a132615d9fd5cee421
theorem sum_reps (X : Inst D L) (p : ℕ) (f : HH D → ℝ) :
    ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, f s = ∑ s ∈ D.host.TT, f s := by
  unfold topsOn reps
  rw [Finset.sum_fiberwise_of_maps_to]
  intro x _
  exact Finset.mem_image_of_mem _ (Finset.mem_univ x)

theorem VV_subset_reps (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) : VV X K a b p ⊆ reps X p := by
  unfold VV
  split_ifs
  · unfold goodReps
    exact Finset.filter_subset _ _
  · exact Finset.empty_subset _

theorem bs_nsmul (X : Inst D L) (h t : HH D) (i : ℕ) : X.bs h (t + i • h) = X.bs h t := by
  induction i with
  | zero => simp
  | succ n ih => rw [succ_nsmul, ← add_assoc, X.bs_step, ih]

theorem bs_bs (X : Inst D L) (h t : HH D) : X.bs h (X.bs h t) = X.bs h t := by
  obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := X.bs_orb h t
  have e := bs_nsmul X h (X.bs h t) i
  rw [hi] at e
  exact e.symm

theorem card_reps_mul_le (X : Inst D L) (p : ℕ) : (reps X p).card * addOrderOf (stepP D p) ≤ D.R.n := by
  classical
  have hcard : Fintype.card (HH D) = D.R.n := D.R.card_A
  have h1 : (Finset.univ : Finset (HH D)).card =
      ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, (Finset.univ.filter (fun t => X.bs (stepP D p) t = t0)).card :=
    Finset.card_eq_sum_card_fiberwise (fun s _ => Finset.mem_image_of_mem _ (Finset.mem_univ s))
  have h2 : ∀ t0 ∈ reps X p, addOrderOf (stepP D p) ≤
      (Finset.univ.filter (fun t => X.bs (stepP D p) t = t0)).card := by
    intro t0 ht0
    obtain ⟨t, _, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 ht0
    have := Finset.card_le_card_of_injOn (s := Finset.range (addOrderOf (stepP D p)))
      (t := Finset.univ.filter (fun t' => X.bs (stepP D p) t' = X.bs (stepP D p) t))
      (fun i => X.bs (stepP D p) t + i • stepP D p)
      (fun i _ => by
        simp only [Finset.coe_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and, Set.mem_ofPred_eq]
        rw [bs_nsmul, bs_bs])
      (fun i hi i' hi' hii => by
        have hi1 : i ∈ Set.Iio (addOrderOf (stepP D p)) := by simpa using hi
        have hi2 : i' ∈ Set.Iio (addOrderOf (stepP D p)) := by simpa using hi'
        exact nsmul_injOn_Iio_addOrderOf hi1 hi2 (add_left_cancel hii))
    simpa using this
  have h3 := Finset.sum_le_sum h2
  rw [Finset.sum_const, smul_eq_mul, ← h1, Finset.card_univ, hcard] at h3
  exact h3

theorem card_reps_mul_nP_le (X : Inst D L) (p : ℕ) : ((reps X p).card : ℝ) * (nP D p : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) := by
  have h := card_reps_mul_le X p
  have hf : D.R.f = 2 * D.R.n := rfl
  by_cases hs : IsSg D p
  · have h1 : stepP D p = 2 • hP D p := by unfold stepP; rw [if_pos hs]
    have h2 : nP D p = 2 * addOrderOf (2 • hP D p) := by unfold nP; rw [if_pos hs]
    rw [h1] at h
    rw [h2, hf]
    have : (reps X p).card * (2 * addOrderOf (2 • hP D p)) ≤ 2 * D.R.n := by nlinarith
    exact_mod_cast this
  · have h1 : stepP D p = hP D p := by unfold stepP; rw [if_neg hs]
    have h2 : nP D p = addOrderOf (hP D p) := by unfold nP; rw [if_neg hs]
    rw [h1] at h
    rw [h2, hf]
    have : (reps X p).card * addOrderOf (hP D p) ≤ 2 * D.R.n := by omega
    exact_mod_cast this

theorem sum_Nv (X : Inst D L) (p : ℕ) : ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) = (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) := by
  have h := sum_reps X p (fun _ => (1 : ℝ))
  simp only [Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul, mul_one] at h
  unfold Nv
  exact h

theorem card_TT_le_f (D : D7) : (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) := by
  have h1 : D.host.TT.card ≤ Fintype.card (HH D) := Finset.card_le_univ _
  have h2 : Fintype.card (HH D) = D.R.n := D.R.card_A
  have hf : D.R.f = 2 * D.R.n := rfl
  have : D.host.TT.card ≤ D.R.f := by omega
  exact_mod_cast this

theorem sum_dl_le (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) :
    ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, dl X K p t0 ≤ 2 * K.dl1 * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Nm D ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a +
      4 * K.dl2 * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.logb p L.sc.a ^ 2 := by
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, h1, h2, -, -⟩ := hK
  obtain ⟨hl0, hl20, hl2⟩ := logb_facts L.sc p hp
  have hR := card_reps_mul_nP_le X p
  have hN := sum_Nv X p
  have hT := card_TT_le_f D
  have hsum : ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, dl X K p t0 =
      K.dl1 * Nm D ^ 2 * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) * (((reps X p).card : ℝ) * (nP D p : ℝ)) +
      K.dl2 * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) ^ 2 * ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) := by
    unfold dl
    rw [Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul, Finset.mul_sum]
    congr 1
    · ring
    · exact Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun _ _ => by ring)
  have c0 : 0 ≤ K.dl1 * Nm D ^ 2 := mul_nonneg h1 (sq_nonneg _)
  have t1 : K.dl1 * Nm D ^ 2 * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) * (((reps X p).card : ℝ) * (nP D p : ℝ)) ≤
      K.dl1 * Nm D ^ 2 * (2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a) * (D.R.f : ℝ) :=
    mul_le_mul (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hl2 c0) hR (by positivity) (mul_nonneg c0 (by linarith))
  have hB2 : Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) ^ 2 ≤ (2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a) ^ 2 := by nlinarith
  have t2 : K.dl2 * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) ^ 2 * ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) ≤
      K.dl2 * (2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a) ^ 2 * (D.R.f : ℝ) := by
    rw [hN]
    exact mul_le_mul (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hB2 h2) hT (by positivity) (mul_nonneg h2 (sq_nonneg _))
  rw [hsum]
  nlinarith [t1, t2]

theorem fl_nonneg (s : Scale) (C : ℝ) (hC : 0 ≤ C) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) : 0 ≤ fl D s C p := by
  have h := (logb_facts s p hp).1
  have hf : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) := by positivity
  unfold fl
  have h1 : 0 ≤ C * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Nm D ^ 2 * Real.logb p s.a :=
    mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hC hf) (sq_nonneg _)) h
  have h2 : 0 ≤ C * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.logb p s.a ^ 2 := mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hC hf) (sq_nonneg _)
  linarith

theorem sum_dl_add_ep_le (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (Ce C bo : ℝ) (hCe : 0 ≤ Ce)
    (hN : GainBoundConstants K Ce C bo) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (h9 : (X.ep p : ℝ) ≤ Ce * (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a) :
    ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, dl X K p t0 + (X.ep p : ℝ) ≤ fl D L.sc C p := by
  have h1 := sum_dl_le X K hK p hp
  obtain ⟨hlg, _, _⟩ := logb_facts L.sc p hp
  have hf0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hk0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hkN : (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ Nm D := by
    have : D.R.k ≤ D.R.N D.R.m := by unfold Ranks.N; omega
    unfold Nm
    exact_mod_cast this
  have hk2 : (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 ≤ Nm D ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hk0 hkN 2
  have h2 : Ce * (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a ≤
      Ce * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Nm D ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a :=
    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hk2 (mul_nonneg hCe hf0)) hlg
  have h3 : (2 * K.dl1 + Ce) * ((D.R.f : ℝ) * Nm D ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a) ≤
      C * ((D.R.f : ℝ) * Nm D ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a) :=
    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hN.Cdl1 (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hf0 (sq_nonneg _)) hlg)
  have h4 : (4 * K.dl2) * ((D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.logb p L.sc.a ^ 2) ≤
      C * ((D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.logb p L.sc.a ^ 2) :=
    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hN.Cdl2 (mul_nonneg hf0 (sq_nonneg _))
  unfold fl
  nlinarith
