theorem lo_ge_cp (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (Ce C bo : ℝ) (hN : GainBoundConstants K Ce C bo) (p : ℕ)
    (hp : p.Prime) : 3 * cG L * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) ≤ lo K L C bo p := by
  obtain ⟨_, hlg2, _⟩ := logb_facts L.sc p hp
  obtain ⟨_, _, hSc, hw500, _⟩ := basics P
  have hC0 : 0 ≤ C := le_trans (by norm_num) hN.C3
  have hη := L.etaSz_pos
  have hcG : 0 ≤ cG L := by unfold cG; linarith
  have hb' := sc_b'_nonneg L.sc
  have hx := sc_x_pos L.sc
  have ha : 0 ≤ L.sc.a := le_trans zero_le_one (sc_one_lt_a L.sc).le
  have hlx : 0 ≤ Real.log L.sc.x := Real.log_nonneg L.sc.one_lt_alt.le
  have hd := sc_delta1_pos L.sc
  have hLa := La_nonneg K hK L.sc
  have hp0 : (0 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp.pos
  have hk0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hcp : 3 * cG L * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) ≤ C * (cG L + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) := by
    apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right _ hlg2
    nlinarith [hN.C3]
  dsimp only [lo]
  apply le_add_of_le_of_nonneg
  · apply le_add_of_le_of_nonneg hcp
    split_ifs
    · exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hC0 (Nat.cast_nonneg _)) hb') hlx
    · have h1 : 0 ≤ L.sc.b' / L.sc.x := div_nonneg hb' hx.le
      have h2 : 0 ≤ C * L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 :=
        div_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hC0 (by linarith)) hlx) hd.le
      have := hN.bo0
      linarith
    · exact le_rfl
    · exact le_rfl
  · have h0 : 0 ≤ La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) := div_nonneg (mul_nonneg hLa ha) (mul_nonneg hp0.le hk0)
    split_ifs
    · exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hC0 hlx) (add_nonneg h0 (mul_nonneg ha (Real.rpow_nonneg hp0.le _)))
    · exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hC0 hlx) (add_nonneg h0 (div_nonneg ha (by positivity)))
    · exact le_rfl

theorem lo_nonneg (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (Ce C bo : ℝ) (hN : GainBoundConstants K Ce C bo) (p : ℕ)
    (hp : p.Prime) : 0 ≤ lo K L C bo p := by
  obtain ⟨_, hlg2, _⟩ := logb_facts L.sc p hp
  obtain ⟨_, _, hSc, _⟩ := basics P
  have hcG : 0 ≤ cG L := by unfold cG; linarith
  exact le_trans (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (by norm_num) hcG) hlg2) (lo_ge_cp P K hK Ce C bo hN p hp)

theorem g_zero_ge_ask_sum (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (hW : InWin L a b)
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (j : ℕ) (hj : j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D) (t0 : HH D)
    (hEx : ∀ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, ∀ e, Excl D p s e → (a s : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ e ≤ fill X b j (partner D p s e)) :
    ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, (askP X a b p j s - 3 * cG L * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a)) - dl X K p t0 ≤
      g0 X K a b p j t0 := by
  have hp2 : (2 : ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast hp.two_le
  obtain ⟨_, _, hSc, _⟩ := basics P
  have hcG : 0 ≤ cG L := by unfold cG; linarith
  have hb : ∀ t ∈ D.host.TT, L.sc.b' ≤ (b t : ℝ) := fun t ht => (hW t ht).2.2
  unfold g0
  apply sub_le_sub_right
  apply Finset.sum_le_sum
  intro s hs
  obtain ⟨ha1, ha2, ha3, _, _⟩ := a_bounds P a b hW s (mem_TT_of_topsOn X p t0 s hs)
  have ha := sc_one_lt_a L.sc
  have hask : askP X a b p j s = ∑' e : ℕ, if Odd e ∧ ¬ Excl D p s e then
      max 0 ((a s : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ e - fill X b j (partner D p s e)) else 0 := by
    unfold askP
    congr 1
    funext e
    by_cases ho : Odd e
    · by_cases hx : Excl D p s e
      · rw [if_pos ho, if_neg (fun h => h.2 hx), max_eq_left (by linarith [hEx s hs e hx])]
      · rw [if_pos ho, if_pos ⟨ho, hx⟩]
    · rw [if_neg ho, if_neg (fun h => ho h.1)]
  rw [hask]
  unfold al
  exact @tsum_truncated_shift_le (p : ℝ) (a s : ℝ) (cG L) (2 * L.sc.a) hp2 hcG ha3 (by linarith) (by linarith)
    (fun e => fill X b j (partner D p s e)) (fun e => capU X b p j s e)
    (fun e => fill_ge_one P X b hb j _) (fun e => (capU_bounds P X b hb p j hj s e).1)
    (fun e => (capU_bounds P X b hb p j hj s e).2) (fun e => Odd e ∧ ¬ Excl D p s e) _ (fun e h => h.1)

theorem card_lifts (D : D7) : ((SizeData₂.lifts D).card : ℝ) = (D.R.m : ℝ) := by
  simp [SizeData₂.lifts]

theorem noop (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (Ce C bo : ℝ) (hCe : 0 ≤ Ce)
    (hN : GainBoundConstants K Ce C bo) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (hW : InWin L a b) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (h9 : (X.ep p : ℝ) ≤ Ce * (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a)
    (hEx : ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∀ s ∈ D.host.TT, ∀ e, Excl D p s e →
      (a s : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ e ≤ fill X b j (partner D p s e)) :
    (∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∑ s ∈ D.host.TT, (askP X a b p j s - lo K L C bo p)) - (D.R.m : ℝ) * fl D L.sc C p ≤
      ∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0 := by
  have hfl : ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∑ s ∈ D.host.TT, (askP X a b p j s - lo K L C bo p) ≤
      (∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0) + fl D L.sc C p := by
    intro j hj
    have hlo := lo_ge_cp P K hK Ce C bo hN p hp
    have hdl := sum_dl_add_ep_le X K hK Ce C bo hCe hN p hp h9
    have hep : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (X.ep p : ℝ) := by positivity
    rw [← sum_reps X p]
    have hper : ∀ t0 ∈ reps X p, ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, (askP X a b p j s - lo K L C bo p) ≤
        max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0 + dl X K p t0 := by
      intro t0 _
      have hg := g_zero_ge_ask_sum P X K a b hW p hp j hj t0
        (fun s hs e hx => hEx j hj s (mem_TT_of_topsOn X p t0 s hs) e hx)
      have hmono : ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, (askP X a b p j s - lo K L C bo p) ≤
          ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, (askP X a b p j s - 3 * cG L * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a)) :=
        Finset.sum_le_sum (fun s _ => by linarith)
      have hmx := le_max_left (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0
      linarith
    calc ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, (askP X a b p j s - lo K L C bo p)
        ≤ ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, (max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0 + dl X K p t0) := Finset.sum_le_sum hper
      _ = (∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0) + ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, dl X K p t0 :=
          Finset.sum_add_distrib
      _ ≤ (∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0) + fl D L.sc C p := by linarith
  have htot := Finset.sum_le_sum hfl
  rw [Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul, card_lifts D] at htot
  linarith

theorem orbit_max_le (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (hW : InWin L a b)
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (j : ℕ) (hj : j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D) (t0 : HH D) :
    max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0 ≤ gam p * ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, al L a s ∧
    max (gU X K a b p j t0 - Pr X K b p j t0) (max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0) ≤
      gam p * ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, al L a s := by
  have hg := (gam_facts p hp).1
  have hsum : 0 ≤ ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, al L a s :=
    Finset.sum_nonneg (fun s hs => (a_bounds P a b hW s (mem_TT_of_topsOn X p t0 s hs)).2.2.2.1)
  have h0 : 0 ≤ gam p * ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, al L a s := mul_nonneg hg hsum
  have hg0 := g_zero_le_gamma_sum P X K hK a b hW p hp j hj t0
  have hgU := gU_le P X K hK a b hW p hp j hj t0
  have hPr := Pr_nonneg P X K hK b p j hj t0
  have hm : max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0 ≤ gam p * ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, al L a s := max_le hg0 h0
  exact ⟨hm, max_le (by linarith) hm⟩

theorem gain_bounds (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (hW : InWin L a b)
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (j : ℕ) (hj : j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D) :
    0 ≤ gainP X K a b p j ∧ gainP X K a b p j ≤ 4 * (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * L.sc.a / (p : ℝ) := by
  have hp0 : (0 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp.pos
  have ha : 0 ≤ L.sc.a := le_trans zero_le_one (sc_one_lt_a L.sc).le
  have hT : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hR : 0 ≤ 4 * (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * L.sc.a / (p : ℝ) := by positivity
  unfold gainP
  split_ifs with hc
  · exact ⟨le_rfl, hR⟩
  · constructor
    · exact add_nonneg (Finset.sum_nonneg (fun t0 _ => le_max_of_le_right (le_max_right _ _)))
        (Finset.sum_nonneg (fun t0 _ => le_max_right _ _))
    · have h1 : ∑ t0 ∈ VV X K a b p, max (gU X K a b p j t0 - Pr X K b p j t0) (max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0) ≤
          ∑ t0 ∈ VV X K a b p, gam p * ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, al L a s :=
        Finset.sum_le_sum (fun t0 _ => (orbit_max_le P X K hK a b hW p hp j hj t0).2)
      have h2 : ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p \ VV X K a b p, max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0 ≤
          ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p \ VV X K a b p, gam p * ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, al L a s :=
        Finset.sum_le_sum (fun t0 _ => (orbit_max_le P X K hK a b hW p hp j hj t0).1)
      have h3 := Finset.sum_sdiff (VV_subset_reps X K a b p) (f := fun t0 => gam p * ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, al L a s)
      have h3' : ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, gam p * ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, al L a s = gam p * ∑ s ∈ D.host.TT, al L a s := by
        rw [← Finset.mul_sum, sum_reps]
      rw [h3'] at h3
      have h4 : ∑ s ∈ D.host.TT, al L a s ≤ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * (6 / 5 * L.sc.a) := by
        rw [← nsmul_eq_mul]
        exact Finset.sum_le_card_nsmul _ _ _ (fun s hs => (a_bounds P a b hW s hs).2.2.2.2)
      obtain ⟨hg0, hg1⟩ := gam_facts p hp
      have h5 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h4 hg0
      have h6 : gam p * ((D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * (6 / 5 * L.sc.a)) ≤
          4 / 3 * (1 / p) * ((D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * (6 / 5 * L.sc.a)) :=
        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hg1 (by positivity)
      have h7 : 4 / 3 * (1 / (p : ℝ)) * ((D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * (6 / 5 * L.sc.a)) =
          2 / 5 * (4 * (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * L.sc.a / (p : ℝ)) := by
        field_simp
        ring
      linarith

theorem top_small (a b : HH D → ℤ) (hW : InWin L a b) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (hpX : L.sc.X' ≤ (p : ℝ))
    (s : HH D) (hs : s ∈ D.host.TT) (e : ℕ) (he : 1 ≤ e) :
    (a s : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ e ≤ L.sc.b' + L.sc.b' / L.sc.x := by
  obtain ⟨h1, h2, -⟩ := hW s hs
  have ha2 := two_le_a L.sc
  have hx1 : 1 < L.sc.x := L.sc.one_lt_alt
  have hX1 : 1 < L.sc.X' := by
    unfold Scale.X'
    have hpos : 0 < L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 3) := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by linarith) _
    have := Real.add_one_le_exp (L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 3))
    linarith
  have hX0 : 0 < L.sc.X' := by linarith
  have hpe : L.sc.X' ≤ (p : ℝ) ^ e := by
    calc L.sc.X' ≤ (p : ℝ) := hpX
      _ = (p : ℝ) ^ 1 := (pow_one _).symm
      _ ≤ (p : ℝ) ^ e := pow_le_pow_right₀ (by linarith) he
  have has : 0 ≤ (a s : ℝ) := by linarith
  have hb'0 : 0 ≤ L.sc.b' := by
    unfold Scale.b'
    exact div_nonneg (by linarith) hX0.le
  set Q : ℝ := (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) with hQ
  have hQx : L.sc.b' / Q ≤ L.sc.b' / L.sc.x := by
    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos D.R.k with hk | hk
    · have hQ0 : Q = 0 := by rw [hQ, hk]; simp
      rw [hQ0, div_zero]
      exact div_nonneg hb'0 (by linarith)
    · have hk1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast hk
      have hxA : L.sc.x ≤ L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) := by
        calc L.sc.x = L.sc.x ^ (1 : ℝ) := (Real.rpow_one _).symm
          _ ≤ L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) :=
            Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le hx1.le (by unfold Scale.A; norm_num)
      have hxQ : L.sc.x ≤ Q := by
        rw [hQ]
        nlinarith [mul_nonneg (sub_nonneg.mpr hk1) (show (0 : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) by linarith)]
      exact div_le_div_of_nonneg_left hb'0 (by linarith) hxQ
  calc (a s : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ e ≤ (a s : ℝ) / L.sc.X' := div_le_div_of_nonneg_left has hX0 hpe
    _ ≤ (L.sc.a + L.sc.a / Q) / L.sc.X' := div_le_div_of_nonneg_right h2 hX0.le
    _ = L.sc.b' + L.sc.b' / Q := by unfold Scale.b'; ring
    _ ≤ L.sc.b' + L.sc.b' / L.sc.x := by linarith

theorem excl_small_of_ge (X : Inst D L) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (hW : InWin L a b) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hpX : L.sc.X' ≤ (p : ℝ)) (j : ℕ) (s : HH D) (hs : s ∈ D.host.TT) (e : ℕ) (hx : Excl D p s e) :
    (a s : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ e ≤ fill X b j (partner D p s e) := by
  obtain ⟨he, s', hs', -, -, hpart, -⟩ := hx
  have hb : ∀ t ∈ D.host.TT, L.sc.b' ≤ (b t : ℝ) := fun t ht => (hW t ht).2.2
  rw [hpart]
  exact le_trans (top_small a b hW p hp hpX s hs e he.pos) (fill_top_ge X b hb j s' hs')

theorem not_excl_of_sg (p : ℕ) (hsg : IsSg D p) (s : HH D) (e : ℕ) : ¬ Excl D p s e := by
  rintro ⟨he, s', _, _, _, hpart, _⟩
  unfold partner at hpart
  have h1 := congrArg Prod.fst hpart
  simp [hsg, he] at h1

theorem not_singular_of_central (p : ℕ) (h : IsResidueClassZero D p) : ¬ IsSg D p := by
  intro hs
  have e : PrimeClass.inertClass = PrimeClass.signal :=
    (show D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass from h).symm.trans hs
  cases e

theorem hypothesis₂ (X : Inst D L) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (hW : InWin L a b) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hpX : L.sc.X' ≤ (p : ℝ)) (hc0 : IsResidueClassZero D p) (hh : hP D p = 0) (j : ℕ) (s : HH D) (hs : s ∈ D.host.TT) :
    askP X a b p j s = 0 := by
  unfold askP
  have hns : ¬ IsSg D p := not_singular_of_central p hc0
  have hb : ∀ t ∈ D.host.TT, L.sc.b' ≤ (b t : ℝ) := fun t ht => (hW t ht).2.2
  have hterm : ∀ e : ℕ, (if Odd e then max 0 ((a s : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ e - fill X b j (partner D p s e)) else 0)
      = 0 := by
    intro e
    split_ifs with he
    · have hpart : partner D p s e = (false, s) := by
        unfold partner
        simp [hns, hh]
      rw [hpart]
      have h1 := top_small a b hW p hp hpX s hs e he.pos
      have h2 := fill_top_ge X b hb j s hs
      exact max_eq_left (by linarith)
    · rfl
  simp only [hterm, tsum_zero]

theorem sum_TTP_served (X : Inst D L) (V : Finset (HH D)) (p : ℕ) (f : HH D → ℝ) :
    ∑ s ∈ D.host.TT.filter (fun t => X.bs (stepP D p) t ∈ V), f s = ∑ t0 ∈ V, ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, f s := by
  unfold topsOn
  simp only [Finset.sum_filter]
  rw [Finset.sum_comm]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro s _
  rw [Finset.sum_ite_eq]

theorem hP_ne_zero_of_cand (K : Consts) (p : ℕ) (hc : Cand D L K p) : hP D p ≠ 0 := by
  intro h0
  have h1 := hc.2.2.2.1
  rw [h0, addOrderOf_zero] at h1
  have he : 0 < L.sc.eps / 4 := div_pos L.sc.eps_pos (by norm_num)
  have h2 : 1 < L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) := Real.one_lt_rpow L.sc.one_lt_alt he
  push_cast at h1
  linarith

theorem gain_bound_at_of_star (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (Ce C bo : ℝ) (hCe : 0 ≤ Ce)
    (hN : GainBoundConstants K Ce C bo)
    (h9 : ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → (p : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n →
      (X.ep p : ℝ) ≤ Ce * (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a)
    (a b : HH D → ℤ) (hW : InWin L a b)
    (hStar : ∀ p, Cand D L K p → ∀ t0 ∈ goodReps X p, ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, StarAt X K C bo a b p j t0) :
    GainBoundAtPairUniform X K C bo a b := by
  intro p hp hcl hpX''
  refine ⟨fun j hj => gain_bounds P X K hK a b hW p hp j hj, ?_⟩
  have hC0 : 0 ≤ C := le_trans (by norm_num) hN.C3
  have hfl0 := fl_nonneg (D := D) L.sc C hC0 p hp
  have hm0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hm1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) := by
    have := D.R.two_le_m
    exact_mod_cast (by omega : 1 ≤ D.R.m)
  have hgain0 : 0 ≤ ∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, gainP X K a b p j :=
    Finset.sum_nonneg (fun j hj => (gain_bounds P X K hK a b hW p hp j hj).1)
  by_cases hVV : VV X K a b p = ∅
  · have htau : tauP X K a b p = 0 := by unfold tauP; rw [if_pos hVV]
    rw [htau, sub_zero]
    by_cases hfull : IsSg D p ∨ L.sc.X' ≤ (p : ℝ)
    · have hTTP : TTP X K a b p = D.host.TT := by unfold TTP; rw [if_pos hfull]
      rw [hTTP]
      by_cases hc : IsResidueClassZero D p ∧ hP D p = 0
      ·
        have hpX : L.sc.X' ≤ (p : ℝ) := hfull.resolve_left (not_singular_of_central p hc.1)
        have hlo := lo_nonneg P K hK Ce C bo hN p hp
        have h1 : ∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∑ s ∈ D.host.TT, (askP X a b p j s - lo K L C bo p) ≤ 0 := by
          apply Finset.sum_nonpos
          intro j _
          apply Finset.sum_nonpos
          intro s hs
          rw [hypothesis₂ X a b hW p hp hpX hc.1 hc.2 j s hs]
          linarith
        have h2 := mul_nonneg hm0 hfl0
        linarith
      ·
        have hg : ∀ j, gainP X K a b p j = ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0 := by
          intro j
          unfold gainP
          rw [if_neg (fun h => h.elim hc (fun h' => absurd hpX'' (not_le.2 h'))), hVV]
          simp
        simp only [hg]
        apply noop P X K hK Ce C bo hCe hN a b hW p hp (h9 p hp hpX'')
        intro j _ s hs e hx
        rcases hfull with hsg | hpX
        · exact absurd hx (not_excl_of_sg p hsg s e)
        · exact excl_small_of_ge X a b hW p hp hpX j s hs e hx
    ·
      have hTTP : TTP X K a b p = ∅ := by
        unfold TTP
        rw [if_neg hfull, hVV]
        simp
      rw [hTTP]
      simp only [Finset.sum_empty, Finset.sum_const_zero]
      have h2 := mul_nonneg hm0 hfl0
      linarith
  ·
    have hc := cand_of_VV_ne X K a b p hVV
    have hV : VV X K a b p = goodReps X p := by
      by_contra hne
      apply hVV
      unfold VV at hne ⊢
      split_ifs at hne ⊢ with h
      · exact absurd rfl hne
      · rfl
    have hnfull : ¬ (IsSg D p ∨ L.sc.X' ≤ (p : ℝ)) := by
      rintro (h | h)
      · exact not_singular_of_central p hc.2.2.1 h
      · exact absurd hc.2.1 (not_lt.2 h)
    have hTTP : TTP X K a b p = D.host.TT.filter (fun t => X.bs (stepP D p) t ∈ VV X K a b p) := by
      unfold TTP; rw [if_neg hnfull]
    set V := VV X K a b p with hVdef
    have htau : tauP X K a b p = (X.ep p : ℝ) + (D.R.m : ℝ) * ∑ t0 ∈ V,
        ((⌈tv X K a b p t0⌉₊ : ℝ) + (Dp K L p : ℝ) + 1) := by
      unfold tauP
      rw [if_neg hVV, Finset.mul_sum]
    have hgain : ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∑ t0 ∈ V, (gU X K a b p j t0 - Pr X K b p j t0) ≤ gainP X K a b p j := by
      intro j _
      unfold gainP
      rw [if_neg (fun h => h.elim (fun h' => hP_ne_zero_of_cand K p hc h'.2) (fun h' => absurd hpX'' (not_le.2 h')))]
      have h1 : ∑ t0 ∈ V, (gU X K a b p j t0 - Pr X K b p j t0) ≤
          ∑ t0 ∈ V, max (gU X K a b p j t0 - Pr X K b p j t0) (max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0) :=
        Finset.sum_le_sum (fun t0 _ => le_max_left _ _)
      have h2 : 0 ≤ ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p \ V, max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0 :=
        Finset.sum_nonneg (fun t0 _ => le_max_right _ _)
      linarith
    have hstar : ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∑ s ∈ TTP X K a b p, (askP X a b p j s - lo K L C bo p) ≤
        ∑ t0 ∈ V, (gU X K a b p j t0 - Pr X K b p j t0) + (∑ t0 ∈ V, dl X K p t0 -
          ∑ t0 ∈ V, ((⌈tv X K a b p t0⌉₊ : ℝ) + (Dp K L p : ℝ) + 1)) := by
      intro j hj
      rw [hTTP, sum_TTP_served, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
      apply Finset.sum_le_sum
      intro t0 ht0
      have := hStar p hc t0 (by rw [← hV]; exact ht0) j hj
      unfold StarAt at this
      linarith
    have h1 := Finset.sum_le_sum hstar
    rw [Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul, card_lifts] at h1
    have h2 := Finset.sum_le_sum hgain
    have h3 := sum_dl_add_ep_le X K hK Ce C bo hCe hN p hp (h9 p hp hpX'')
    have h4 : ∑ t0 ∈ V, dl X K p t0 ≤ ∑ t0 ∈ reps X p, dl X K p t0 :=
      Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg (VV_subset_reps X K a b p) (fun t0 _ _ => dl_nonneg X K hK p hp t0)
    have hep : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (X.ep p : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
    have h5 : (X.ep p : ℝ) + (D.R.m : ℝ) * ∑ t0 ∈ V, dl X K p t0 ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) * fl D L.sc C p := by
      have h6 : (D.R.m : ℝ) * (∑ t0 ∈ V, dl X K p t0 + (X.ep p : ℝ)) ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) * fl D L.sc C p :=
        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) hm0
      nlinarith
    rw [htau]
    nlinarith

theorem gain_bound_all_of_star (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (Ce C bo : ℝ) (hCe : 0 ≤ Ce)
    (hN : GainBoundConstants K (Ce + 1) C bo) (h9 : adm9 D L X Ce) (hStar : StarAll X K C bo) : GainBoundAllUniform X K C bo := by
  have hXa : L.sc.X'' D.R.n ≤ L.sc.a := by
    obtain ⟨_, _, _, hw500, _⟩ := basics P
    unfold Scale.X''
    exact div_le_self (le_trans zero_le_one (sc_one_lt_a L.sc).le) (by linarith)
  exact fun a b hW => gain_bound_at_of_star P X K hK (Ce + 1) C bo (by linarith) hN
    (fun p hp hpX => h9.1 p hp (le_trans hpX hXa)) a b hW (fun p hc t0 ht0 j hj => hStar a b hW p hc t0 ht0 j hj)

end Formalism.MainNotions.AuxBlock8.Pf

theorem fill_ge (X : Inst D L) (b : HH D → ℤ) (hb : ∀ t ∈ D.host.TT, (0 : ℝ) ≤ (b t : ℝ)) (j : ℕ)
    (z : Bool × HH D) : L.sc.w'' D.R.n ≤ fill X b j z := by
  have hx1 : 1 < L.sc.x := L.sc.one_lt_alt
  have hL0 : 0 ≤ L.sc.L := by
    show 0 ≤ L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps
    exact Real.rpow_nonneg (by linarith) _
  have hb1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.b := by
    show 1 ≤ Real.exp L.sc.L
    exact Real.one_le_exp hL0
  have ha1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.a := by
    show 1 ≤ L.sc.x * L.sc.b
    nlinarith
  have hX1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.X' := by
    show 1 ≤ Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 3))
    exact Real.one_le_exp (Real.rpow_nonneg (by linarith) _)
  have hb'0 : 0 ≤ L.sc.b' := by
    show 0 ≤ L.sc.a / L.sc.X'
    exact div_nonneg (by linarith) (by linarith)
  have hn : (1 : ℝ) ≤ D.R.n := by exact_mod_cast n_pos D
  have hw1 : L.sc.w'' D.R.n ≤ L.sc.b' / L.sc.x := by
    show L.sc.b' / (L.sc.x * D.R.n) ≤ L.sc.b' / L.sc.x
    exact div_le_div_of_nonneg_left hb'0 (by linarith) (by nlinarith)
  have hw2 : L.sc.w'' D.R.n ≤ L.sc.b := by
    calc L.sc.w'' D.R.n ≤ L.sc.b' / L.sc.x := hw1
      _ = L.sc.b / L.sc.X' := by
        show L.sc.x * L.sc.b / L.sc.X' / L.sc.x = L.sc.b / L.sc.X'
        field_simp
      _ ≤ L.sc.b := div_le_self (by linarith) hX1
  unfold fill
  by_cases htop : IsTopPl D z
  · rw [if_pos (show z.1 = false ∧ z.2 ∈ D.host.TT from htop)]
    linarith [hb z.2 htop.2, (X.off_top j z htop).1]
  · rw [if_neg (show ¬(z.1 = false ∧ z.2 ∈ D.host.TT) from htop), zero_add]
    by_cases hthick : IsThickPl D z
    · linarith [(X.off_thick j z hthick).1]
    · exact (X.off_thin j z ⟨htop, hthick⟩).1

theorem kaJ_bounds (D : D7) (j : ℕ) (hj : j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D) : 0 ≤ kaJ D j ∧ kaJ D j ≤ Nm D := by
  simp only [Formalism.BasicNotions.SizeData₂.lifts, Finset.mem_Icc] at hj
  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := hj
  have h1' : (1 : ℝ) ≤ j := by exact_mod_cast h1
  have h2' : (j : ℝ) ≤ D.R.m := by exact_mod_cast h2
  have ht : (0 : ℝ) ≤ D.R.t + 1 := by positivity
  have hk : (0 : ℝ) ≤ D.R.k := by positivity
  unfold kaJ Nm Formalism.BasicNotions.Ranks.N
  push_cast
  constructor
  · apply div_nonneg _ (by norm_num)
    nlinarith
  · rw [div_le_iff₀ (by norm_num)]
    nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (show 2 * (j : ℝ) - 1 ≤ 2 * D.R.m by linarith) ht]

theorem asksAll_of (X : Inst D L) (hSz : Nm D + 1 ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n) : RequiresAll X := by
  have hx1 : 1 < L.sc.x := L.sc.one_lt_alt
  have hL0 : 0 ≤ L.sc.L := by
    show 0 ≤ L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps
    exact Real.rpow_nonneg (by linarith) _
  have hb1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.b := by
    show 1 ≤ Real.exp L.sc.L
    exact Real.one_le_exp hL0
  have ha1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.a := by
    show 1 ≤ L.sc.x * L.sc.b
    nlinarith
  have hX1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.X' := by
    show 1 ≤ Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 3))
    exact Real.one_le_exp (Real.rpow_nonneg (by linarith) _)
  have hb'0 : 0 ≤ L.sc.b' := by
    show 0 ≤ L.sc.a / L.sc.X'
    exact div_nonneg (by linarith) (by linarith)
  intro a b ha hb p hp j hj s hs
  have hp2 : (2 : ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast hp.two_le
  obtain ⟨hk0, hkN⟩ := kaJ_bounds D j hj
  have hNm0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ Nm D := by unfold Nm; positivity
  have hwG : ∀ e, 1 ≤ fillG X b j (partner D p s e) := by
    intro e
    have := fill_ge X b hb j (partner D p s e)
    unfold fillG
    linarith
  have hw0 : ∀ e, 0 ≤ fillG X b j (partner D p s e) := fun e => by linarith [hwG e]
  have hwF : ∀ e, 0 ≤ fill X b j (partner D p s e) := fun e => by
    have := fill_ge X b hb j (partner D p s e)
    linarith
  have hA : 1 ≤ 2 * L.sc.a := by linarith
  have hlog : 0 ≤ Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) := Real.logb_nonneg (by linarith) hA
  by_cases hy : 0 ≤ (a s : ℝ)
  · have h1 := shift_ge (p : ℝ) (a s) (kaJ D j) hp2 hk0 hy
      (fun e => fillG X b j (partner D p s e)) hw0 Odd
    have h2 := shift_le (p : ℝ) (a s) (kaJ D j) (2 * L.sc.a) hp2 hk0 hy (ha s hs) hA
      (fun e => fillG X b j (partner D p s e)) hwG Odd (fun e h => h)
    simp only [fillG, sub_add_cancel] at h1 h2
    unfold askP askG fillG
    refine ⟨h1, ?_⟩
    nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hkN hlog]
  · have hz : ∀ c : ℕ → ℝ, (∀ e, 0 ≤ c e) →
        ∑' e : ℕ, (if Odd e then max 0 ((a s : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ e - c e) else 0) = 0 := by
      intro c hc
      refine (tsum_congr fun e => ?_).trans tsum_zero
      split_ifs
      · apply max_eq_left
        have : (a s : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ e ≤ 0 :=
          div_nonpos_of_nonpos_of_nonneg (by linarith) (by positivity)
        linarith [hc e]
      · rfl
    have e1 := hz (fun e => fill X b j (partner D p s e)) hwF
    have e2 := hz (fun e => fillG X b j (partner D p s e)) hw0
    beta_reduce at e1 e2
    unfold askP askG
    rw [e1, e2]
    exact ⟨le_rfl, by rw [zero_add]; exact mul_nonneg hNm0 hlog⟩

theorem lo_succ (K : Consts) (hLa : 0 ≤ K.La) (L : SizeData₂ D) (C bo : ℝ) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) :
    lo K L C bo p + (cG L + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) ≤ lo K L (C + 1) bo p := by
  have hx1 : 1 < L.sc.x := L.sc.one_lt_alt
  have hL0 : 0 ≤ L.sc.L := by
    show 0 ≤ L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps
    exact Real.rpow_nonneg (by linarith) _
  have hb1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.b := by
    show 1 ≤ Real.exp L.sc.L
    exact Real.one_le_exp hL0
  have ha1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.a := by
    show 1 ≤ L.sc.x * L.sc.b
    nlinarith
  have hX1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.X' := by
    show 1 ≤ Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 3))
    exact Real.one_le_exp (Real.rpow_nonneg (by linarith) _)
  have hb'0 : 0 ≤ L.sc.b' := by
    show 0 ≤ L.sc.a / L.sc.X'
    exact div_nonneg (by linarith) (by linarith)
  have hlogx : 0 ≤ Real.log L.sc.x := Real.log_nonneg hx1.le
  have hp0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ p := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hw : 0 ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n := by
    show 0 ≤ L.sc.b' / (L.sc.x * D.R.n)
    exact div_nonneg hb'0 (mul_nonneg (by linarith) (Nat.cast_nonneg _))
  have hd1 : 0 < L.sc.delta1 := by
    show 0 < L.sc.x ^ (-(L.sc.eps / 8))
    exact Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by linarith) _
  have hLaS : 0 ≤ La K L.sc := by
    unfold La
    exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hLa (by positivity)) (Real.log_nonneg ha1)
  have g1 : 0 ≤ (rG L.sc : ℝ) * L.sc.b' * Real.log L.sc.x :=
    mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (Nat.cast_nonneg _) hb'0) hlogx
  have g2 : 0 ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 :=
    div_nonneg (mul_nonneg hw hlogx) hd1.le
  have g3 : 0 ≤ La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) :=
    div_nonneg (mul_nonneg hLaS (by linarith)) (mul_nonneg hp0 (Nat.cast_nonneg _))
  have g4 : 0 ≤ L.sc.a * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(L1 D L.sc p : ℝ)) :=
    mul_nonneg (by linarith) (Real.rpow_nonneg hp0 _)
  have g5 : 0 ≤ L.sc.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2 := div_nonneg (by linarith) (by positivity)
  have g6 := mul_nonneg hlogx (add_nonneg g3 g4)
  have g7 := mul_nonneg hlogx (add_nonneg g3 g5)
  obtain ⟨α, β, hform, hα⟩ : ∃ α β : ℝ, (∀ C' : ℝ, lo K L C' bo p = C' * α + β) ∧
      (cG L + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) ≤ α :=
    ⟨(cG L + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) +
        (if IsResidueClassZero D p then (if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then (rG L.sc : ℝ) * L.sc.b' * Real.log L.sc.x
          else if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' then L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 else 0)
          else 0) +
        (if IsResidueClassZero D p ∧ (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' then Real.log L.sc.x * (La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) +
          (if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then L.sc.a * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(L1 D L.sc p : ℝ))
            else L.sc.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2)) else 0),
      (if IsResidueClassZero D p then (if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then 0
        else if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' then L.sc.b' / L.sc.x + L.etaSz + bo else 0) else 0),
      fun C' => by
        simp only [lo]
        split_ifs <;> ring,
      by split_ifs <;> linarith [g1, g2, g6, g7]⟩
  rw [hform, hform]
  have e : (C + 1) * α + β = C * α + β + α := by ring
  rw [e]
  linarith

theorem fl_succ (D : D7) (s : Scale) (C : ℝ) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) : fl D s C p ≤ fl D s (C + 1) p := by
  have hx1 : 1 < s.x := s.one_lt_alt
  have hL0 : 0 ≤ s.L := by
    show 0 ≤ s.x ^ s.eps
    exact Real.rpow_nonneg (by linarith) _
  have hb1 : 1 ≤ s.b := by
    show 1 ≤ Real.exp s.L
    exact Real.one_le_exp hL0
  have ha1 : 1 ≤ s.a := by
    show 1 ≤ s.x * s.b
    nlinarith
  have hX1 : 1 ≤ s.X' := by
    show 1 ≤ Real.exp (s.x ^ (s.eps / 3))
    exact Real.one_le_exp (Real.rpow_nonneg (by linarith) _)
  have hb'0 : 0 ≤ s.b' := by
    show 0 ≤ s.a / s.X'
    exact div_nonneg (by linarith) (by linarith)
  have hp1 : (1 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp.one_lt
  have hl : 0 ≤ Real.logb p s.a := Real.logb_nonneg hp1 ha1
  have h1 : 0 ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) * Nm D ^ 2 * Real.logb p s.a := mul_nonneg (by positivity) hl
  have h2 : 0 ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.logb p s.a ^ 2 := by positivity
  unfold fl
  nlinarith [h1, h2]

theorem TTP_subset₂ (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) : TTP X K a b p ⊆ D.host.TT := by
  unfold TTP
  split_ifs
  · exact Finset.Subset.refl _
  · exact Finset.filter_subset _ _

theorem gainBoundAll_add_one_of_gainBoundAllRequired (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (hLa : 0 ≤ K.La) (C bo : ℝ) (hA : RequiresAll X) (hNc : Nm D ≤ cG L)
    (h : GainBoundAllUniform X K C bo) : GainBoundAll X K (C + 1) bo := by
  have hx1 : 1 < L.sc.x := L.sc.one_lt_alt
  have hL0 : 0 ≤ L.sc.L := by
    show 0 ≤ L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps
    exact Real.rpow_nonneg (by linarith) _
  have hb1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.b := by
    show 1 ≤ Real.exp L.sc.L
    exact Real.one_le_exp hL0
  have ha1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.a := by
    show 1 ≤ L.sc.x * L.sc.b
    nlinarith
  have hX1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.X' := by
    show 1 ≤ Real.exp (L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 3))
    exact Real.one_le_exp (Real.rpow_nonneg (by linarith) _)
  have hb'0 : 0 ≤ L.sc.b' := by
    show 0 ≤ L.sc.a / L.sc.X'
    exact div_nonneg (by linarith) (by linarith)
  intro a b hwin p hp hcls hX
  obtain ⟨hgain, hsum⟩ := h a b hwin p hp hcls hX
  refine ⟨hgain, ?_⟩
  have hlo := lo_succ K hLa L C bo p hp
  have hfl := fl_succ D L.sc C p hp
  have hp1 : (1 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp.one_lt
  have hlog : 0 ≤ Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) := Real.logb_nonneg hp1 (by linarith)
  have heta := L.etaSz_pos
  have hk1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ D.R.k := by
    have := D.R.m_add_three_le_k
    exact_mod_cast (by omega : 1 ≤ D.R.k)
  have hxA : 1 ≤ L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) :=
    Real.one_le_rpow hx1.le (by unfold Scale.A; norm_num)
  have ha2 : ∀ t ∈ D.host.TT, (a t : ℝ) ≤ 2 * L.sc.a := by
    intro t ht
    have h1 := (hwin t ht).2.1
    have h2 : L.sc.a / ((D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ≤ L.sc.a :=
      div_le_self (by linarith) (one_le_mul_of_one_le_of_one_le hk1 hxA)
    linarith
  have hb0 : ∀ t ∈ D.host.TT, (0 : ℝ) ≤ (b t : ℝ) := fun t ht => by
    linarith [(hwin t ht).2.2]
  have hterm : ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∀ s ∈ TTP X K a b p,
      askG X a b p j s - lo K L (C + 1) bo p ≤ askP X a b p j s - lo K L C bo p := by
    intro j hj s hs
    have hA := (hA a b ha2 hb0 p hp j hj s (TTP_subset₂ X K a b p hs)).2
    have h3 : Nm D * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) ≤ (cG L + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) :=
      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) hlog
    linarith
  have hsum2 : (∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∑ s ∈ TTP X K a b p, (askG X a b p j s - lo K L (C + 1) bo p)) ≤
      ∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∑ s ∈ TTP X K a b p, (askP X a b p j s - lo K L C bo p) :=
    Finset.sum_le_sum fun j hj => Finset.sum_le_sum fun s hs => hterm j hj s hs
  have hm : 0 ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hfl hm]

end Formalism.MainNotions.AuxBlock8.Pf.As

end


[GLG_gain_and_count_06] literal FamilyAskBounds and RemainingCountingStatement
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/LogarithmicSums/RemainingCountingStatement.lean#L10-L20
Range SHA256: cbc2d6b8182e2c669b91e0f0b977893c71e77e0b9735062f9dfd2cd12775e59a
def FamilyAskBounds {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  ∀ K : Consts, K.Nonneg → ∀ C31 Ce Cm : ℝ, 0 < C31 → 0 ≤ Ce → 0 ≤ Cm →
    ∀ (X : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), Inst (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i)) (Y : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), LatInst (Fm.D x i)),
      AdmFam Fm K X Y C31 Ce Cm → ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, RequiresAll (X x i)

def RemainingCountingStatement {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  ∀ K : Consts, K.Nonneg → ∀ C31 Ce Cm : ℝ, 0 < C31 → 0 ≤ Ce → 0 ≤ Cm →
    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ (X : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), Inst (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i)) (Y : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), LatInst (Fm.D x i)),
      AdmFam Fm K X Y C31 Ce Cm → ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x,
        GainBoundAll (X x i) K C ((Fm.L x i).sc.b ^ (x ^ (-(eps / 2))))



[GLG_gain_and_count_07] original constant choice and complete remaining-count consumer
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/LogarithmicSums.lean#L43-L64
Range SHA256: d3f6f71abc3662f82dac43fc9580fa5032ed2e73b50ed49119d2f3fdfce9f870
theorem remainingCountingStatement_of_restStar {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (hS : RestStar Fm) : RemainingCountingStatement Fm := by
  intro K hK C31 Ce Cm hC31 hCe _
  obtain ⟨C0, hC0, hS'⟩ := hS K hK C31 Ce Cm hC31
  obtain ⟨C1, hC01, h3, hd1, hd2⟩ : ∃ C1 : ℝ, C0 ≤ C1 ∧ 3 ≤ C1 ∧ 2 * K.dl1 + (Ce + 1) ≤ C1 ∧
      4 * K.dl2 ≤ C1 :=
    ⟨max C0 (max 3 (max (2 * K.dl1 + (Ce + 1)) (4 * K.dl2))), le_max_left _ _,
      le_trans (le_max_left _ _) (le_max_right _ _),
      le_trans (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) (le_max_right _ _)) (le_max_right _ _),
      le_trans (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) (le_max_right _ _)) (le_max_right _ _)⟩
  refine ⟨C1 + 1, by linarith, fun X Y hA => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨x1, h1⟩ := hS' C1 hC01 X Y hA
  obtain ⟨x2, h2⟩ := eventually_adm_of_admFam Fm hA
  refine ⟨max x1 x2, fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨hxp, hadm⟩ := h2 x (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) hx)
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, a9, -, -, aSz⟩ := hadm i
  have hN : GainBoundConstants K (Ce + 1) C1 ((Fm.L x i).sc.b ^ (x ^ (-(eps / 2)))) :=
    ⟨h3, hd1, hd2, bo_nonneg _ _⟩
  have hP := gain_bound_all_of_star (Fm.params x hxp i) (X x i) K hK Ce C1 _ hCe hN a9
    (h1 x (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hx) i)
  have hNc : Nm (Fm.D x i) ≤ cG (Fm.L x i) :=
    (basics (Fm.params x hxp i)).2.1.trans (St.base (Fm.params x hxp i)).2.2.2.2
  exact As.gainBoundAll_add_one_of_gainBoundAllRequired (X x i) K hK.1 C1 _ (As.asksAll_of (X x i) aSz.2.2) hNc hP

def adm9 (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (Ce : ℝ) : Prop :=
  (∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → (p : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.a →
    (X.ep p : ℝ) ≤ (Ce + 1) * (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a) ∧
  ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)), Nonempty (GatTies D) →
    (D.gat u w hg).Mna.map ((((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^
        X.ep (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) • LinearMap.id) ≤ (D.gat u w hg).M ∧
      (D.gat u w hg).M ≤ (D.gat u w hg).Mna



[GLG_gain_and_count_09] all admG1 clauses, type-prime guards and full admitted conjunction
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/SmallOrderPrimes.lean#L220-L247
Range SHA256: 3e467728bae407ce706abba313ca973a48438f21fa6a6b21c24257325ea4a22b
def admG1 (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ hT : GatTies D, LatTied hT X Y → ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    let p := D.what₂.places.resChar w
    let G := D.gat u w hg
    let Mram := G.M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg
    NP X Y K u w hg ≤ Mram.map (((p : G.O) ^ ⌈tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) p⌉.toNat) • LinearMap.id) ∧
    (∃ l : ℕ, Module.length G.O (↥Mram ⧸ (NP X Y K u w hg).comap Mram.subtype) = l ∧
      (l : ℝ) ≤ vUnit G * (G.d : ℝ) * (tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) p + ioP X K (aL L) (bL L) p)) ∧
    ∀ F ∈ NP X Y K u w hg,
      Y.vKge u w hg F ((∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, gainP X K (aL L) (bL L) p j) + lowFE L u w)

def TypePrimeSizeConds (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  Formalism.MainNotions.SizesAt L ∧ 4 * L.Sc ≤ La K L.sc * L.sc.b' / (D.R.k : ℝ) ∧ Nm D + 1 ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n

structure AConsts where
  C31 : ℝ
  Ce : ℝ
  Cm : ℝ
  δ : ℝ

def admitted (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (A : AConsts) : Prop :=
  adm1 D L X A.C31 ∧ adm2 D L X ∧ adm3 D L ∧ ArchimedeanSignatureMatching D L K ∧ adm5 D L K A.δ ∧ adm6 D L K ∧
  adm7 D L X K ∧ adm8 D L X K A.Cm ∧ adm9 D L X A.Ce ∧ unitBoundAtTwistedPlaces D L X Y K ∧ admG1 D L X Y K ∧
  TypePrimeSizeConds D L K

def Conclusion₂ (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (C bo : ℝ) : Prop :=
  Conclusion1 D L X K C bo ∧ Conclusion2 D L X K ∧ PointsLemma D L X K ∧ WeightVectorExists D L X K ∧ Conclusion0 D L X ∧ WeightedPrimeSumBound D L X K C


[GLG_global_interface_01] loG/flG and ALL FIVE LinkG_at conjuncts, then full LinkG
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GlobalTheoremInputs.lean#L97-L140
Range SHA256: 92b096aa52bdef5669406c0e8ff34c2c77a66e617562a63c80c79821abb28825
def InertK (D : D7) (p : ℕ) : Prop :=
  D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.signal ∨ D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass

def flG {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (C : ℝ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  C * (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a +
    C * (D.R.f : ℝ) * (Real.logb p L.sc.a) ^ 2

def rG (eps : ℝ) : ℕ := ⌈8 * ((2 + 8 * eps) + 2) / eps⌉₊ + 1

def leastPowerExponent {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (p : ℕ) : ℕ :=
  ⌈Real.logb p ((D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * L.sc.x ^ (3 * Scale.A))⌉₊ + 1

def loG {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (C bo : ℝ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  let s := L.sc
  let pr : ℝ := if D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass then
      (if (p : ℝ) < s.p0 D.R.k then C * (rG s.eps : ℝ) * s.b' * Real.log s.x
       else if (p : ℝ) < s.X' then
         C * (s.b' / s.x + L.etaSz + L.Sc + (s.w'' D.R.n + D.R.t) * Real.log s.x / s.delta1) + bo
       else 0) else 0
  let orr : ℝ := if D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass ∧ (p : ℝ) < s.X' then
      C * Real.log s.x * (L.LaG * s.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) +
        (if (p : ℝ) < s.p0 D.R.k then s.a * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(leastPowerExponent L p : ℝ)) else s.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2))
      else 0
  C * (L.Sc + D.R.k + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * s.a) + pr + orr

def LinkG_at {D : D7} {L : SizeData₂ D} (X : G.Inst D L) (K : G.Consts) (C bo : ℝ) : Prop :=
  (∀ u w, D.what₂.places.resChar w ≠ (D.mem u).A.T →
    DV.LocLe (D.plEq w) ⌊DV.ur D w (L.tau w)⌋₊ (L.NuLat u) (DV.MramL D u) ∧
    (L.lenMN u w : ℝ) ≤ (D.duS u : ℝ) * (L.tau w + L.io w) ∧
    (G.VV X K (G.aL L) (G.bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅ → L.tau w = 0 ∧ L.io w = 0 ∧ L.lenMN u w = 0)) ∧
  (∀ p, ∀ t0 ∈ G.VV X K (G.aL L) (G.bL L) p,
    G.Sv X K (G.aL L) (G.bL L) p t0 ≤ 2 * (D.R.m : ℝ) * (G.Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.a / p) ∧
  (∀ w, InertK D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) → (D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n →
    (∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, 0 ≤ L.gain j w ∧
      L.gain j w ≤ 4 * (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * L.sc.a / (D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℝ)) ∧
    (∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∑ t ∈ L.TTw w,
        (L.vonMangoldtClassSum j t (D.what₂.places.resChar w) - loG L C bo (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) -
      (D.R.m : ℝ) * flG L C (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ≤
      ((∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, L.gain j w) - L.tau w)) ∧
  G.ServedOrbitsContained X K (G.aL L) (G.bL L) ∧ G.MeanKappaBound X K

def LinkG {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  ∃ (C x0 : ℝ) (δ : ℝ → ℝ), Filter.Tendsto δ Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
    ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, LinkG_at (Fm.Gx x i) Fm.GK C ((Fm.L x i).sc.b ^ δ x)


[GLG_global_interface_02] least exponent, exact 4*abs(C)+1 loss transport and finite class sum
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/PlaneCountTheoremWithConstants.lean#L65-L244
Range SHA256: 2995d4c40101eef536fc23a0b9cfa3cc1307e892701d9cbbe7fed2e97ff542d5
theorem exponent_ge_least_exponent (L : SizeData₂ D) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (h0 : (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k) :
    leastPowerExponent L p ≤ L1 D L.sc p := by
  have hx := sc_x_pos L.sc
  have hp1 : (1 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp.one_lt
  have hp0 : (0 : ℝ) < p := lt_trans one_pos hp1
  set y : ℝ := L.sc.x ^ Scale.A with hy
  have hy1 : 1 ≤ y := Real.one_le_rpow L.sc.one_lt_alt.le (by unfold Scale.A; norm_num)
  have hy3 : L.sc.x ^ (3 * Scale.A) = y ^ 3 := by
    rw [hy, ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hx.le]
    congr 1
    push_cast
    ring
  have hk : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) := by
    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos D.R.k with h | h
    · exfalso
      unfold Scale.p0 at h0
      rw [h] at h0
      simp at h0
      linarith
    · exact_mod_cast h
  have hp0' : (p : ℝ) < (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * y := h0
  have hz1 : 1 ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * y ^ 3 := by
    have h1 : 1 ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 := one_le_pow₀ hk
    have h2 : 1 ≤ y ^ 3 := one_le_pow₀ hy1
    nlinarith
  have hlog : Real.logb p ((D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * y ^ 3) + 1 ≤ Real.logb p (((D.R.k : ℝ) * y) ^ 4) := by
    have h1 : Real.logb p ((D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * y ^ 3) + 1 = Real.logb p ((D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * y ^ 3 * p) := by
      rw [Real.logb_mul (by positivity) hp0.ne', Real.logb_self_eq_one hp1]
    rw [h1]
    apply Real.logb_le_logb_of_le hp1 (by positivity)
    have h2 : ((D.R.k : ℝ) * y) ^ 4 = (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * y ^ 3 * ((D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * y) := by ring
    rw [h2]
    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hp0'.le (by positivity)
  unfold leastPowerExponent L1
  rw [hy3, ← Nat.ceil_add_one (Real.logb_nonneg hp1 hz1)]
  exact Nat.ceil_mono hlog

theorem lo_le_loG (K : Consts) (L : SizeData₂ D) (hK : 0 ≤ K.La) (hLa : La K L.sc = L.LaG) (C bo : ℝ) (p : ℕ)
    (hp : p.Prime) : lo K L C bo p ≤ loG L (4 * |C| + 1) bo p := by
  have hx := sc_x_pos L.sc
  have hp1 : (1 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp.one_lt
  have hp0 : (0 : ℝ) < p := lt_trans one_pos hp1
  have habs := abs_nonneg C
  have hle := le_abs_self C
  set C' : ℝ := 4 * |C| + 1 with hC'
  have hCC' : C ≤ C' := by linarith
  have hC'1 : 1 ≤ C' := by linarith
  have hC'0 : 0 ≤ C' := by linarith
  have ha := sc_one_lt_a L.sc
  have hlg : 0 ≤ Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) := Real.logb_nonneg hp1 (by linarith)
  have hlx : 0 ≤ Real.log L.sc.x := Real.log_nonneg L.sc.one_lt_alt.le
  have hb' := sc_b'_nonneg L.sc
  have hw := sc_w''_nonneg L.sc D.R.n
  have hd := sc_delta1_pos L.sc
  have hSc := L.Sc_pos
  have hη := L.etaSz_pos
  have hk : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have ht : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.t : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hLaG : 0 ≤ L.LaG := by
    rw [← hLa]
    unfold La
    exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hK (by positivity)) (Real.log_nonneg ha.le)

  have hcp : C * (cG L + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) ≤
      C' * (L.Sc + D.R.k + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) := by
    apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right _ hlg
    unfold cG
    have h1 : C * (4 * L.Sc + L.etaSz) ≤ |C| * (4 * L.Sc + L.etaSz) :=
      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hle (by linarith)
    have h2 := mul_nonneg habs hη.le
    have h3 := mul_nonneg habs hk
    have h4 := mul_nonneg habs hSc.le
    rw [hC']
    nlinarith

  have hpr : (if IsResidueClassZero D p then
      (if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then C * (rG L.sc : ℝ) * L.sc.b' * Real.log L.sc.x
       else if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' then
         L.sc.b' / L.sc.x + L.etaSz + C * L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 + bo
       else 0) else 0) ≤
      (if D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass then
      (if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then C' * (Formalism.MainNotions.rG L.sc.eps : ℝ) * L.sc.b' * Real.log L.sc.x
       else if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' then
         C' * (L.sc.b' / L.sc.x + L.etaSz + L.Sc + (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + D.R.t) * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) + bo
       else 0) else 0) := by
    by_cases hc : D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass
    · rw [if_pos hc, if_pos hc]
      by_cases h0 : (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k
      · rw [if_pos h0, if_pos h0, ← rG_eq]
        have h1 : 0 ≤ (rG L.sc : ℝ) * L.sc.b' * Real.log L.sc.x :=
          mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (Nat.cast_nonneg _) hb') hlx
        nlinarith [mul_nonneg (sub_nonneg.2 hCC') h1]
      · rw [if_neg h0, if_neg h0]
        by_cases h1 : (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X'
        · rw [if_pos h1, if_pos h1]
          have e1 : 0 ≤ L.sc.b' / L.sc.x := div_nonneg hb' hx.le
          have e2 : 0 ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 := div_nonneg (mul_nonneg hw hlx) hd.le
          have e3 : 0 ≤ (D.R.t : ℝ) * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 := div_nonneg (mul_nonneg ht hlx) hd.le
          have e4 : C * L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 =
              C * (L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1) := by ring
          have e5 : (L.sc.w'' D.R.n + D.R.t) * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 =
              L.sc.w'' D.R.n * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 + (D.R.t : ℝ) * Real.log L.sc.x / L.sc.delta1 := by
            ring
          rw [e4, e5]
          nlinarith [mul_nonneg (sub_nonneg.2 hCC') e2, mul_nonneg (sub_nonneg.2 hC'1) e1,
            mul_nonneg (sub_nonneg.2 hC'1) hη.le, mul_nonneg hC'0 hSc.le, mul_nonneg hC'0 e3]
        · rw [if_neg h1, if_neg h1]
    · rw [if_neg hc, if_neg hc]

  have hor : (if IsResidueClassZero D p ∧ (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' then
      C * Real.log L.sc.x * (La K L.sc * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) +
        (if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then L.sc.a * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(L1 D L.sc p : ℝ)) else L.sc.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2))
      else 0) ≤
      (if D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass ∧ (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' then
      C' * Real.log L.sc.x * (L.LaG * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) +
        (if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then L.sc.a * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(leastPowerExponent L p : ℝ)) else L.sc.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2))
      else 0) := by
    by_cases hc : D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass ∧ (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X'
    · rw [if_pos hc, if_pos hc, hLa]
      have e1 : 0 ≤ L.LaG * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) :=
        div_nonneg (mul_nonneg hLaG (by linarith)) (mul_nonneg hp0.le hk)
      have key : ∀ Z Z' : ℝ, 0 ≤ Z → Z ≤ Z' →
          C * Real.log L.sc.x * (L.LaG * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) + Z) ≤
            C' * Real.log L.sc.x * (L.LaG * L.sc.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) + Z') := by
        intro Z Z' hZ hZZ
        exact mul_le_mul (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hCC' hlx) (by linarith) (by linarith)
          (mul_nonneg hC'0 hlx)
      by_cases h0 : (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k
      · rw [if_pos h0, if_pos h0]
        apply key
        · exact mul_nonneg (by linarith) (Real.rpow_nonneg hp0.le _)
        · apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left _ (by linarith)
          apply Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le hp1.le
          have := exponent_ge_least_exponent L p hp h0
          have h2 : (leastPowerExponent L p : ℝ) ≤ (L1 D L.sc p : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast this
          linarith
      · rw [if_neg h0, if_neg h0]
        exact key _ _ (div_nonneg (by linarith) (by positivity)) le_rfl
    · rw [if_neg hc, if_neg hc]
  unfold lo loG
  exact add_le_add (add_le_add hcp hpr) hor

theorem fl_le_flG (L : SizeData₂ D) (C : ℝ) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) :
    fl D L.sc C p ≤ flG L (4 * |C| + 1) p := by
  have hp1 : (1 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp.one_lt
  have hlg : 0 ≤ Real.logb p L.sc.a := Real.logb_nonneg hp1 (sc_one_lt_a L.sc).le
  have hCC' : C ≤ 4 * |C| + 1 := by linarith [abs_nonneg C, le_abs_self C]
  have hf : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  unfold fl flG Nm
  have e1 : 0 ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a :=
    mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hf (by positivity)) hlg
  have e2 : 0 ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.logb p L.sc.a ^ 2 := mul_nonneg hf (by positivity)
  nlinarith [mul_nonneg (sub_nonneg.2 hCC') e1, mul_nonneg (sub_nonneg.2 hCC') e2]

theorem aL_top (L : SizeData₂ D) (t : D.host.TT) : aL L (t : HH D) = L.aT t := by
  unfold aL
  rw [dif_pos t.2]

theorem ask_eq_askP (X : Inst D L) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hw : ∀ j (t : D.host.TT) e, L.wPart j t p e = fill X (bL L) j (partner D p t e)) (j : ℕ) (t : D.host.TT) :
    L.vonMangoldtClassSum j t p = askP X (aL L) (bL L) p j (t : HH D) := by
  unfold SizeData₂.vonMangoldtClassSum askP
  rw [tsum_eq_sum (s := Finset.range (Nat.log p (L.aT t).toNat + 2))]
  · apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro i _
    rw [aL_top, hw]
  · intro i hi
    rw [Finset.mem_range, not_lt] at hi
    by_cases ho : Odd i
    · rw [if_pos ho, aL_top, ← hw]
      apply max_eq_left
      have h1 : (L.aT t).toNat < p ^ i :=
        lt_of_lt_of_le (Nat.lt_pow_succ_log_self hp.one_lt _) (Nat.pow_le_pow_right hp.pos (by omega))
      have h2 : (L.aT t : ℝ) ≤ ((L.aT t).toNat : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast Int.self_le_toNat (L.aT t)
      have h3 : (((L.aT t).toNat : ℕ) : ℝ) < (p : ℝ) ^ i := by exact_mod_cast h1
      have h4 : (0 : ℝ) < (p : ℝ) ^ i := by have := hp.pos; positivity
      have h5 : (L.aT t : ℝ) / (p : ℝ) ^ i < 1 := by rw [div_lt_one h4]; linarith
      have := L.wPart_ge j t p i
      linarith
    · rw [if_neg ho]


[GLG_global_interface_03] ServedGIdx, SvBound, FrobNe and original sufficient guards
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/PlaneCountTheoremWithConstants.lean#L296-L320
Range SHA256: f1dea0ca41bcea034c2cbe13a40a4b30c71497003a5d930b47327a14f6c15165
def ServedGIdx (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (p : ℕ), VV X K (aL L) (bL L) p ≠ ∅ → p ≠ (D.mem u).A.T → GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T p

def SvBound (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ p, ∀ t0 ∈ VV X K (aL L) (bL L) p,
    Sv X K (aL L) (bL L) p t0 ≤ 2 * (D.R.m : ℝ) * (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.a / p

def FrobNe (D : D7) : Prop := ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → IsResidueClassZero D p → HEv D p → HNeZero D.host p

def LevelPrimesNotClassZero (D : D7) : Prop := ∀ p ∈ D.host.levelPrimes, ¬ IsResidueClassZero D p

theorem servedGIdx_of (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (h4 : 4 ≤ ⌊L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.ez / 5)⌋₊) (hF : FrobNe D)
    (hLv : LevelPrimesNotClassZero D) : ServedGIdx X K := by
  intro u p hV hpT
  obtain ⟨hp, _, hc0, hord, _⟩ := cand_of_VV_ne X K _ _ _ hV
  refine ⟨hp, ?_, hc0, hF p hp hc0 (UpperTheory.even_addOrderOf_hP_of_pow_le.2 D L p hord), fun h => hLv p h hc0, ?_⟩
  · by_contra h
    have hp4 : p ≤ D.host.splitBound := le_trans (by omega) (le_trans h4 L.split_tie)
    have hc0' : primeClass D.host.toHostRaw p = PrimeClass.inertClass := hc0
    obtain ⟨hs, -, -, hj, -⟩ := (primeClass_eq_inert_iff D.host.toHostRaw).1 hc0'
    have hne2 : p ≠ 2 := by
      rintro rfl
      exact hs D.host.two_mem_small
    exact hj (D.host.split_small p hp hne2 hp4)
  · exact fun h => hpT ((Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hp (D.mem u).A.T_prime).1 h)


[GLG_global_interface_04] ceil absorption and exact 2m*Nv*a/p Sv bound
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/PlaneCountTheoremWithConstants.lean#L395-L546
Range SHA256: 5e0e782222d2dca66c1aeb8db772387c63184cda6cafef9e2de7256664eea795
theorem ceil_bound (S B R : ℝ) (hSB : S ≤ B) (h : (B = 0 ∧ R = 0) ∨ B + 1 ≤ R) : ((⌈S⌉ : ℤ) : ℝ) ≤ R := by
  rcases h with ⟨hB, hR⟩ | h
  · have h1 : ⌈S⌉ ≤ 0 := Int.ceil_le.2 (by rw [Int.cast_zero]; linarith)
    have h2 : ((⌈S⌉ : ℤ) : ℝ) ≤ 0 := by exact_mod_cast h1
    linarith
  · have := Int.ceil_lt_add_one S
    linarith

theorem fin_arith (m N q : ℝ) (hm : 2 ≤ m) (hN : 1 ≤ N) (hq : 500 ≤ q) :
    m * (8 / 5 * (N * q)) + 1 ≤ 2 * (m * (N * q)) := by
  have h2 : 500 ≤ N * q := by nlinarith
  have h3 : 1000 ≤ m * (N * q) := by nlinarith
  linarith

theorem svBound_of (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (P : ParameterChoice' D L) : SvBound X K := by
  classical
  intro p t0 ht0
  obtain ⟨hp, hpX, -⟩ := cand_of_VV_ne X K _ _ _ (Finset.ne_empty_of_mem ht0)
  obtain ⟨_, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, hdl1, hdl2, hPr1, _⟩ := hK
  obtain ⟨hk, hN, hSc⟩ := Nm_le_Sc P
  have hn : 1 ≤ D.R.n := D.R.n_pos'
  obtain ⟨hw1, hw2⟩ := w''_le L.sc D.R.n hn
  have hkS := P.kS_le
  have hab := two_b'_le_a L.sc
  have hx := sc_x_pos L.sc
  have ha := sc_one_lt_a L.sc
  have hb' := sc_b'_nonneg L.sc
  have hw0 := sc_w''_nonneg L.sc D.R.n
  have hη := L.etaSz_pos
  have hp1 : (1 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp.one_lt
  have hp2 : (2 : ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast hp.two_le
  have hp0 : (0 : ℝ) < p := by linarith
  have hkSc : 5 * L.Sc ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.Sc := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hk (by linarith)
  have hw500 : 500 ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n := by linarith
  have hlg : 0 ≤ Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) := Real.logb_nonneg hp1 (by linarith)
  have hNm0 : 0 ≤ Nm D := by unfold Nm; exact Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have hcG : 0 ≤ cG L := by unfold cG; linarith
  have hTT : ∀ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, s ∈ D.host.TT := fun s hs => (Finset.mem_filter.1 hs).1

  have hal : ∀ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, 0 ≤ al L (aL L) s ∧ al L (aL L) s ≤ 6 / 5 * L.sc.a := by
    intro s hs
    have h1 : aL L s = L.aT ⟨s, hTT s hs⟩ := by unfold aL; rw [dif_pos (hTT s hs)]
    obtain ⟨h2, h3⟩ := L.aT_win ⟨s, hTT s hs⟩
    have h4 : L.sc.a / ((D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ≤ L.sc.a / 5 := by
      apply div_le_div_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) (by norm_num)
      have : 1 ≤ L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) := Real.one_le_rpow L.sc.one_lt_alt.le (by unfold Scale.A; norm_num)
      nlinarith
    unfold al cG
    rw [h1]
    constructor <;> linarith
  have hdl : 0 ≤ dl X K p t0 := by
    unfold dl
    have h1 : 0 ≤ K.dl1 * (nP D p : ℝ) * Nm D ^ 2 * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) :=
      mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hdl1 (Nat.cast_nonneg _)) (by positivity)) hlg
    have h2 : 0 ≤ K.dl2 * (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * Real.logb p (2 * L.sc.a) ^ 2 :=
      mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hdl2 (Nat.cast_nonneg _)) (by positivity)
    linarith
  have hgU : ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D,
      gU X K (aL L) (bL L) p j t0 ≤ gam p * ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, al L (aL L) s := by
    intro j hj
    unfold gU
    refine le_trans (sub_le_self _ hdl) (sub_le_self _ ?_)
    apply Finset.sum_nonneg
    intro s hs
    apply tsum_nonneg
    intro e
    split_ifs
    · apply le_min
      · exact div_nonneg (hal s (Finset.mem_filter.1 hs).1).1 (by positivity)
      · unfold capU fillG cG
        have := fill_nonneg X j (partner D p s e)
        have := kaJ_le P j hj
        linarith
    · exact le_rfl
  have hbase : 0 ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n + L.etaSz + cG L + Nm D := by linarith
  have hPr : ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, 0 ≤ Pr X K (bL L) p j t0 := by
    intro j hj
    unfold Pr
    split_ifs with h0
    · apply add_nonneg
      · apply Finset.sum_nonneg
        intro s hs
        have hs' := hTT s (Finset.mem_filter.1 hs).1
        have h1 : fill X (bL L) j (false, s) = (bL L s : ℝ) + X.off j (false, s) := by
          unfold fill
          rw [if_pos ⟨rfl, hs'⟩]
        have h2 := (X.off_top j (false, s) ⟨rfl, hs'⟩).1
        have h3 := kaJ_le P j hj
        unfold fillG
        rw [h1]
        linarith
      · exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hPr1 (Nat.cast_nonneg _)) hbase
    · exact mul_nonneg (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
        (add_nonneg (mul_nonneg (Nat.cast_nonneg _) (by linarith)) (mul_nonneg (Nat.cast_nonneg _) hbase))
  have hgam : 0 ≤ gam p ∧ gam p * L.sc.a ≤ 4 / 3 * (L.sc.a / p) := by
    have hp4 : (4 : ℝ) ≤ (p : ℝ) ^ 2 := by nlinarith
    have hd : (0 : ℝ) < (p : ℝ) ^ 2 - 1 := by linarith
    unfold gam
    refine ⟨div_nonneg hp0.le hd.le, ?_⟩
    have h1 : (p : ℝ) / ((p : ℝ) ^ 2 - 1) ≤ 4 / 3 * (1 / p) := by
      rw [div_le_iff₀ hd]
      field_simp
      nlinarith
    have h2 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h1 (by linarith : (0 : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.a)
    calc (p : ℝ) / ((p : ℝ) ^ 2 - 1) * L.sc.a ≤ 4 / 3 * (1 / p) * L.sc.a := h2
      _ = 4 / 3 * (L.sc.a / p) := by ring
  have hsum : ∑ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, al L (aL L) s ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * (6 / 5 * L.sc.a) := by
    unfold Nv
    rw [← nsmul_eq_mul]
    exact Finset.sum_le_card_nsmul _ _ _ (fun s hs => (hal s hs).2)
  set q : ℝ := L.sc.a / p with hq
  have hq0 : L.sc.b' ≤ q := by
    have hX : 0 < L.sc.X' := by unfold Scale.X'; exact Real.exp_pos _
    rw [hq]
    unfold Scale.b'
    exact div_le_div_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) hp0 hpX.le
  have hterm : ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D,
      max 0 (gU X K (aL L) (bL L) p j t0 - Pr X K (bL L) p j t0 - max (g0 X K (aL L) (bL L) p j t0) 0) ≤
        8 / 5 * ((Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * q) := by
    intro j hj
    have hNv : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
    have h1 := hgU j hj
    have h2 := hPr j hj
    have h3 : 0 ≤ max (g0 X K (aL L) (bL L) p j t0) 0 := le_max_right _ _
    have h4 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hsum hgam.1
    have h5 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hgam.2 hNv
    have h6 : 0 ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * q := mul_nonneg hNv (by linarith)
    apply max_le (by linarith)
    nlinarith
  have htot : ∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D,
      max 0 (gU X K (aL L) (bL L) p j t0 - Pr X K (bL L) p j t0 - max (g0 X K (aL L) (bL L) p j t0) 0) ≤
        (D.R.m : ℝ) * (8 / 5 * ((Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * q)) := by
    have h := Finset.sum_le_card_nsmul _ _ _ hterm
    have hc : (SizeData₂.lifts D).card = D.R.m := by unfold SizeData₂.lifts; simp
    rw [hc, nsmul_eq_mul] at h
    exact h
  have hR : 2 * (D.R.m : ℝ) * (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.a / p = 2 * ((D.R.m : ℝ) * ((Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * q)) := by
    rw [hq]; ring
  rw [hR]
  unfold Sv
  apply ceil_bound _ _ _ htot
  rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos (Nv X p t0) with h0 | h0
  · left
    have h0' : (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) = 0 := by exact_mod_cast h0
    rw [h0']
    constructor <;> ring
  · right
    have hm : (2 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast D.R.two_le_m
    have hNv : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast h0
    have hq500 : 500 ≤ q := by linarith
    exact fin_arith _ _ _ hm hNv hq500



[GLG_global_interface_05] actual level-prime exclusion and even-Frobenius nonvanishing

Range SHA256: d7b7cca67d2c0b224879d41ce7b02f37c291a468fa5e1bbd3580860bb5afef14
theorem level_primes_not_class_zero (P : ParameterChoice' D L) : LevelPrimesNotClassZero D := by
  intro p hp hc
  have h := P.level_split p hp
  rw [hc] at h
  exact absurd h (by simp)

theorem frobNe (D : D7) : FrobNe D := by
  intro p hp hc0 hev P _ _ σ hσ
  rw [(UpperTheory.galE_arithFrob_eq_iota_hP D p hp hc0 P σ hσ).2]
  exact hev


[GLG_global_interface_06] full linkG_at_core/of consumers with unchanged five fields
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/PlaneCountTheoremWithConstants.lean#L718-L821
Range SHA256: cff9c46162176e3c9694baad2efd21ed67096caa83a31b61a1850bb693eac74b
theorem linkG_at_core (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (C bo : ℝ) (hT : GatTies D)
    (hfill : ∀ w, ParametersFill X K w)
    (hlat0 : ∀ w (u : D.what₂.Word), VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅ →
      D.what₂.places.resChar w ≠ (D.mem u).A.T → L.lenMN u w = 0)
    (hlen : LenServed X Y K)
    (hsupp : ∀ w, ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n → w ∈ L.supp)
    (hTied : LatTied hT X Y) (hNu : DV.NuDef D L)
    (h1 : AuxBlock8.Conclusion1 D L X K C bo) (h2 : AuxBlock8.Conclusion2 D L X K) (h0 : AuxBlock8.Conclusion0 D L X) (hG1 : AuxBlock8.admG1 D L X Y K)
    (hServed : ServedGIdx X K) (hSv : SvBound X K) (hIncl : NuServed X K) :
    LinkG_at X K (4 * |C| + 1) bo := by
  refine ⟨?_, hSv, ?_, served_orbits_in_set X K _ _, h2⟩
  · intro u w hw
    obtain ⟨_, _, _, _, htau, hio, _⟩ := hfill w
    by_cases hV : VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅
    · have ht0 : L.tau w = 0 := by rw [htau]; unfold tauP; rw [if_pos hV]
      have hi0 : L.io w = 0 := by
        rw [hio]; unfold ioP
        rw [dif_neg (by rw [hV]; exact Finset.not_nonempty_empty)]
      have hl0 := hlat0 w u hV hw
      refine ⟨?_, ?_, fun _ => ⟨ht0, hi0, hl0⟩⟩
      · rw [ht0]
        have h0 : ⌊DV.ur D w 0⌋₊ = 0 := by unfold DV.ur; simp
        rw [h0]
        exact hNu.1 u w hw
      · rw [hl0, ht0, hi0]
        simp
    · refine ⟨by rw [htau]; exact hIncl u w hV hw, ?_, fun h => absurd h hV⟩
      have hg := hServed u _ hV hw
      obtain ⟨_, ⟨l, hl, hle⟩, _⟩ := hG1 hT hTied u w hg
      obtain ⟨l', hl', heq⟩ := hlen u w hg hV
      have hll : l = l' := by
        have := hl.symm.trans hl'
        exact_mod_cast this
      subst hll
      have hv := vUnit_pos (D.gat u _ hg) (Y.vOf u _ hg 0)
      have hd : ((D.gat u _ hg).d : ℝ) = (D.duS u : ℝ) := by rw [D.gat_d]; rfl
      rw [htau, hio]
      rw [heq, hd] at hle
      have h3 : (L.lenMN u w : ℝ) * vUnit (D.gat u _ hg) ≤
          ((D.duS u : ℝ) * (tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) +
            ioP X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) * vUnit (D.gat u _ hg) := by
        linarith
      exact le_of_mul_le_mul_right h3 hv
  · intro w hin hX
    have hp := D.what₂.places.resChar_prime w
    have hws := hsupp w hX
    have hin' : IsResidueClassZero D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ∨ IsSg D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) := hin.symm
    obtain ⟨_, hwp, hTT, hF, htau, _, hLa⟩ := hfill w
    have hgain := hF hws hin'
    obtain ⟨hA, hB⟩ := h1 _ hp hin' hX
    refine ⟨fun j hj => by rw [hgain j hj]; exact hA j hj, ?_⟩
    rw [htau, Finset.sum_congr rfl hgain]
    refine le_trans ?_ hB
    have hlo := lo_le_loG K L hK.1 hLa C bo _ hp
    have hsum : ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D,
        ∑ t ∈ L.TTw w, (L.vonMangoldtClassSum j t (D.what₂.places.resChar w) -
          loG L (4 * |C| + 1) bo (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) ≤
        ∑ s ∈ TTP X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w),
          (askG X (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) j s - lo K L C bo (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) := by
      intro j hj
      rw [← hTT, Finset.sum_map]
      apply Finset.sum_le_sum
      intro t _
      rw [ask_eq_askP X _ hp hwp j t]
      have haw : ∀ s ∈ D.host.TT, (aL L s : ℝ) ≤ 2 * L.sc.a := by
        intro s hs
        have h1 : aL L s = L.aT ⟨s, hs⟩ := by unfold aL; rw [dif_pos hs]
        obtain ⟨_, h2⟩ := L.aT_win ⟨s, hs⟩
        have h3 : L.sc.a / ((D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ≤ L.sc.a := by
          have ha := sc_one_lt_a L.sc
          have hk : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) := by
            have hk1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) := by
              have := D.R.m_add_three_le_k
              exact_mod_cast (show 1 ≤ D.R.k by omega)
            have hx1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) := Real.one_le_rpow L.sc.one_lt_alt.le (by unfold Scale.A; norm_num)
            nlinarith
          exact div_le_self (by linarith) hk
        rw [h1]
        linarith
      have hbw : ∀ s ∈ D.host.TT, (0 : ℝ) ≤ (bL L s : ℝ) := by
        intro s hs
        have h1 : bL L s = L.bT ⟨s, hs⟩ := by unfold bL; rw [dif_pos hs]
        rw [h1]
        exact le_trans (sc_b'_nonneg L.sc) (L.bT_ge ⟨s, hs⟩)
      have hPG := (h0 (aL L) (bL L) haw hbw _ hp hin' j hj t.1 t.2).1
      simp only [Function.Embedding.coe_subtype]
      linarith
    have hfl := fl_le_flG L C _ hp
    have hm : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
    have h4 := Finset.sum_le_sum hsum
    have h5 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hfl hm
    linarith

theorem linkG_at_of (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (C bo : ℝ) (hT : GatTies D)
    (hfill : ∀ w, ParametersFill X K w) (hlat : ∀ w, LatticeLengthsAtPlace X Y K w)
    (hsupp : ∀ w, ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n → w ∈ L.supp)
    (hTied : LatTied hT X Y) (hNu : DV.NuDef D L)
    (h1 : AuxBlock8.Conclusion1 D L X K C bo) (h2 : AuxBlock8.Conclusion2 D L X K) (h0 : AuxBlock8.Conclusion0 D L X) (hG1 : AuxBlock8.admG1 D L X Y K)
    (hServed : ServedGIdx X K) (hSv : SvBound X K) (hIncl : NuServed X K) :
    LinkG_at X K (4 * |C| + 1) bo :=
  linkG_at_core X Y K hK C bo hT hfill (fun w => (hlat w).2)
    (fun u w hg hV => (hlat w).1 (hsupp w (le_trans (cand_of_VV_ne X K _ _ _ hV).2.1.le
      (X'_le_X'' L.sc D.R.n D.R.n_pos'))) u hg)
    hsupp hTied hNu h1 h2 h0 hG1 hServed hSv hIncl


[GLG_global_interface_07] eventual first-growth, NPDescF, finite length and uniform LinkG installation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/PlaneCountTheoremWithConstants.lean#L823-L878

def Ev {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (P : ∀ x, Fm.Ix x → Prop) : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i, P x i

theorem Ev.and {eps : ℝ} {Fm : FamP eps} {P Q : ∀ x, Fm.Ix x → Prop} (hP : Ev Fm P) (hQ : Ev Fm Q) :
    Ev Fm (fun x i => P x i ∧ Q x i) := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := hP
  obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := hQ
  exact ⟨max a b, fun x hx i => ⟨ha x (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hx) i, hb x (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) hx) i⟩⟩

theorem Ev.x1 {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Ev Fm (fun x _ => Fm.x1 < x) :=
  ⟨Fm.x1 + 1, fun x hx _ => by linarith⟩

def FamilyFirstGrowthBound {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.admG1 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) (Fm.Gy x i) Fm.GK

theorem servedGIdx_ev {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Ev Fm fun x i => ServedGIdx (Fm.Gx x i) Fm.GK := by
  obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := Ev.x1 Fm
  refine ⟨max x0 (((4 : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ (5 / Fm.ez)), fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  have hx1 := h x (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hx) i
  have he : 0 < Fm.ez := by rw [← Fm.L_ez x i]; exact (Fm.L x i).sc.ez_pos
  apply servedGIdx_of _ _ _ (frobNe _) (level_primes_not_class_zero (Fm.params x hx1 i))
  refine UpperTheory.Host.le_floor_ez _ he (Fm.L_ez x i).ge 4 ?_
  rw [Fm.L_x x i hx1]
  exact le_trans (le_max_right _ _) hx

theorem svBound_ev {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Ev Fm fun x i => SvBound (Fm.Gx x i) Fm.GK := by
  obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := Ev.x1 Fm
  exact ⟨x0, fun x hx i => svBound_of _ _ Fm.GK_nonneg (Fm.params x (h x hx i) i)⟩

theorem nuServed_ev {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Ev Fm fun x i => NuServed (Fm.Gx x i) Fm.GK := by
  obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := (servedGIdx_ev Fm).and (Ev.x1 Fm)
  exact ⟨x0, fun x hx i => nuServed_of _ (Fm.Gy x i) _ (Fm.ex x (h x hx i).2 i).cmp (Fm.ex x (h x hx i).2 i).lat.served
    (h x hx i).1⟩

def NPDescF {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  Ev Fm fun x i => ∀ hx : Fm.x1 < x, (Fm.ex x hx i).cmp.NPDesc (Fm.Gx x i) (Fm.Gy x i) Fm.GK

theorem sizeDataLat_ev {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (hd : NPDescF Fm) :
    Ev Fm fun x i => ∀ w, LatticeLengthsAtPlace (Fm.Gx x i) (Fm.Gy x i) Fm.GK w := by
  obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := hd.and (Ev.x1 Fm)
  refine ⟨x0, fun x hx i w => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨hdd, hx1⟩ := h x hx i
  exact LatCmp.sizeDataLat_of_latDefs (Fm.ex x hx1 i).cmp (Fm.gatT x i) _ _ _ (Fm.nuDef x hx1 i) (Fm.ex x hx1 i).lat
    (Fm.ex x hx1 i).nu_unserved (hdd hx1) w

theorem link_of_family_theorem {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (hP : AuxBlock8.ConclusionOnFamily Fm Fm.GK Fm.Gx) (hG1 : FamilyFirstGrowthBound Fm)
    (hFl : NPDescF Fm) : LinkG Fm := by
  obtain ⟨C, δ, hδ, hP'⟩ := hP
  have hP'' : Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.Conclusion₂ (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) Fm.GK C ((Fm.L x i).sc.b ^ δ x) := hP'
  obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := (((((hP''.and hG1).and (servedGIdx_ev Fm)).and (svBound_ev Fm)).and (nuServed_ev Fm)).and
    (sizeDataLat_ev Fm hFl)).and (Ev.x1 Fm)
  refine ⟨4 * |C| + 1, x0, δ, hδ, fun x hx i => ?_⟩

  exact linkG_at_of (Fm.Gx x i) (Fm.Gy x i) Fm.GK Fm.GK_nonneg C _ (Fm.gatT x i) (Fm.fills x hx1 i)

structure GatTies (D : D7) where

  gat_k : ∀ u w h, (D.gat u w h).P.m + 1 = D.R.k

  gat_n : ∀ u w h (v : Fin (D.gat u w h).P.nV) (P : Ideal (𝓞 D.host.E)) [P.IsPrime]
    [P.LiesOver (Ideal.span {((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : ℤ)})] (σ : D.host.E ≃ₐ[ℚ] D.host.E), IsArithFrobAt ℤ σ P →
    (D.gat u w h).P.n' v = orderOf (D.host.galE σ).2

  gat_pos : ∀ u w h, (Σ v : Fin (D.gat u w h).P.nV, Fin ((D.gat u w h).P.n' v)) ≃ D.what₂.Tau

  gat_step : ∀ u w h (v : Fin (D.gat u w h).P.nV) (s : Fin ((D.gat u w h).P.n' v))
    (P : Ideal (𝓞 D.host.E)) [P.IsPrime] [P.LiesOver (Ideal.span {((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : ℤ)})]
    (σ : D.host.E ≃ₐ[ℚ] D.host.E), IsArithFrobAt ℤ σ P →
    ((D.eTau (gat_pos u w h ⟨v, posAdd s 1⟩) : D.R.Psi) : D.R.GG) =
      ((D.eTau (gat_pos u w h ⟨v, s⟩) : D.R.Psi) : D.R.GG) *
        D.host.galE σ * D.R.cc

  gat_lam : ∀ u w h (v : Fin (D.gat u w h).P.nV) (s : Fin ((D.gat u w h).P.n' v))
    (i : Fin ((D.gat u w h).P.m + 1)),
    (D.gat u w h).P.lam0 v s i =
      ((D.fm u).lam 0 (gat_pos u w h ⟨v, s⟩)).w
        ⟨i.1, by have h1 := gat_k u w h; have h2 := i.2; simp only [Ranks.N]; omega⟩

end Formalism.BasicNotions

def InLoc {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module (𝓞 K) V]
    (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) (A : Submodule (𝓞 K) V) (x : V) : Prop :=
  ∃ s : 𝓞 K, s ∉ w.asIdeal ∧ s • x ∈ A

variable {D : D7} (c : LatCmp D)

def VramDef (Y : LatInst D) : Prop :=
  ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    (Y.Vram u w hg : Set (D.gat u w hg).Fn) =
      Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).Kf (c.cmp u w hg '' ((D.fm u).ramSub : Set ((D.fm u).lat 0).V))

def NuServedDef {L : SizeData₂ D} (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ≠ ∅ →
    ∀ x : (D.div.Nu u).V, InLoc (D.plEq w) (L.NuLat u) x ↔ c.cmp u w hg (D.nuIn u x) ∈ NP X Y K u w hg

end LatCmp

      LocEq (D.plEq w) (L.NuLat u) (MramL D u)) ∧

    (l : ℝ) = (L.lenMN u w : ℝ) * (D.what₂.places.ramIdx w : ℝ))

section Descent

open IsDedekindDomain

variable {A K : Type} [CommRing A] [IsDedekindDomain A] [Field K] [Algebra A K] [IsFractionRing A K]
  (w : HeightOneSpectrum A)
  {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module K V] [Module A V] [IsScalarTower A K V]
  {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (b : Module.Basis ι K V)
  {O Kf Fn : Type} [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf] [AddCommGroup Fn] [Module Kf Fn] [Module O Fn]
  [IsScalarTower O Kf Fn] (φ : K →+* Kf) (cmp : V →+ Fn)

abbrev locW : Subalgebra A K := Localization.subalgebra.ofField K _ w.asIdeal.primeCompl_le_nonZeroDivisors

theorem exists_smul_mem_of_mem_span (Lat : Submodule A V) (v : V)
    (hv : v ∈ Submodule.span (locW (K := K) w) (Lat : Set V)) : ∃ s : A, s ∉ w.asIdeal ∧ s • v ∈ Lat := by
  induction hv using Submodule.span_induction with
  | mem x hx => exact ⟨1, (Ideal.ne_top_iff_one _).1 w.isPrime.ne_top, by rwa [one_smul]⟩
  | zero => exact ⟨1, (Ideal.ne_top_iff_one _).1 w.isPrime.ne_top, by rw [smul_zero]; exact Lat.zero_mem⟩
  | add x y _ _ hx hy =>
    obtain ⟨s, hs, hsx⟩ := hx
    obtain ⟨t, ht, hty⟩ := hy
    refine ⟨s * t, fun h => (w.isPrime.mem_or_mem h).elim hs ht, ?_⟩
    rw [smul_add]
    refine Lat.add_mem ?_ ?_
    · rw [mul_comm, mul_smul]; exact Lat.smul_mem t hsx
    · rw [mul_smul]; exact Lat.smul_mem s hty
  | smul r x _ hx =>
    obtain ⟨s, hs, hsx⟩ := hx
    obtain ⟨r, a, t, ht, rfl⟩ := r
    have ht0 : algebraMap A K t ≠ 0 := by
      intro h0
      have : (t : A) = 0 := (IsFractionRing.injective A K) (by rw [h0, map_zero])
      exact ht (by rw [this]; exact w.asIdeal.zero_mem)
    refine ⟨t * s, fun h => (w.isPrime.mem_or_mem h).elim ht hs, ?_⟩
    have : (t * s) • ((⟨algebraMap A K a * (algebraMap A K t)⁻¹, a, t, ht, rfl⟩ : locW (K := K) w) • x) =
        a • (s • x) := by
      rw [Subalgebra.smul_def]
      simp only
      rw [← algebraMap_smul K (t * s), ← algebraMap_smul K a, ← algebraMap_smul K s, smul_smul, smul_smul, map_mul]
      congr 1
      field_simp
    rw [this]
    exact Lat.smul_mem a hsx

include b in
theorem cmp_expand (hs : ∀ a x, cmp (a • x) = φ a • cmp x) (x : V) :
    cmp x = ∑ a, φ (b.repr x a) • cmp (b a) := by
  conv_lhs => rw [← b.sum_repr x]
  rw [map_sum]
  exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => hs _ _

theorem cmp_inj (hs : ∀ a x, cmp (a • x) = φ a • cmp x) (hli : LinearIndependent Kf (fun a => cmp (b a))) :
    Function.Injective cmp := by
  rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
  intro x hx
  rw [cmp_expand b φ cmp hs x] at hx
  have h := Fintype.linearIndependent_iff.1 hli _ hx
  apply b.repr.injective
  ext a
  simpa using h a

theorem descent (hφ : ∀ x : K, w.valuation K x ≤ 1 ↔ φ x ∈ Set.range (algebraMap O Kf))
    (hs : ∀ a x, cmp (a • x) = φ a • cmp x) (hli : LinearIndependent Kf (fun a => cmp (b a)))
    (Lat : Submodule A V) (hfg : Lat.FG) (v : V) (hv : cmp v ∈ Submodule.span O (cmp '' (Lat : Set V))) :
    ∃ s : A, s ∉ w.asIdeal ∧ s • v ∈ Lat := by
  exact Bl.general_b_fact_upper_bis.goal1 w b φ cmp hφ hs hli Lat hfg v hv

end Descent



[GLG_local_depth_06] residue-prime valuation, original depth inclusion and LenServed
