theorem val_le_one_iff (D : D7) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (x : D.what₂.LL) :
    (D.plEq w).valuation D.what₂.LL x ≤ 1 ↔ (0 : WithTop ℤ) ≤ D.what₂.places.val w x := by
  by_cases hx : x = 0
  · subst hx; simp
  · have hv0 : (D.plEq w).valuation D.what₂.LL x ≠ 0 := (Valuation.ne_zero_iff _).mpr hx
    rw [D.val_eq w x hx]
    unfold vAdd
    have h1 : ((0 : WithTop ℤ) ≤ ((-WithZero.log ((D.plEq w).valuation D.what₂.LL x) : ℤ) : WithTop ℤ)) ↔
        WithZero.log ((D.plEq w).valuation D.what₂.LL x) ≤ 0 := by
      rw [← WithTop.coe_zero, WithTop.coe_le_coe]; omega
    rw [h1]
    conv_lhs => rw [← WithZero.exp_log hv0, ← WithZero.exp_zero, WithZero.exp_le_exp]

theorem resChar_mem_pow (D : D7) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) :
    ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : 𝓞 D.what₂.LL) ∈ (D.plEq w).asIdeal ^ D.what₂.places.ramIdx w := by
  have hv := D.what₂.places.val_resChar w
  have hne : ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : D.what₂.LL) ≠ 0 := by
    exact_mod_cast (D.what₂.places.resChar_prime w).ne_zero
  rw [D.val_eq w _ hne] at hv
  have hv' : vAdd (D.plEq w) ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : D.what₂.LL) =
      ((D.what₂.places.ramIdx w : ℕ) : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hv
  rw [← IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum.intValuation_le_pow_iff_mem, ← IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum.valuation_of_algebraMap (K := D.what₂.LL)]
  have hx : algebraMap (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) D.what₂.LL ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : 𝓞 D.what₂.LL) =
      ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : D.what₂.LL) := by simp
  change (D.plEq w).valuation D.what₂.LL (algebraMap (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) D.what₂.LL _) ≤ _
  rw [hx]
  have hv0 : (D.plEq w).valuation D.what₂.LL ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : D.what₂.LL) ≠ 0 :=
    (Valuation.ne_zero_iff _).mpr hne
  unfold vAdd at hv'
  rw [← WithZero.exp_log hv0, WithZero.exp_le_exp]
  omega

theorem nuServed_of (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (c : LatCmp D) (hdef : c.NuServedDef X Y K)
    (hServed : ServedGIdx X K) : NuServed X K := by
  intro u w hV hw x hx
  have hg := hServed u _ hV hw
  have h1 : c.cmp u w hg (D.nuIn u x) ∈ NP X Y K u w hg :=
    (hdef u w hg hV x).1 ⟨1, (Ideal.ne_top_iff_one _).1 (D.plEq w).isPrime.ne_top, by rwa [one_smul]⟩
  unfold NP at h1
  obtain ⟨⟨_, y, hy, hyx⟩, _⟩ := h1
  set T : ℕ := ⌈tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w)⌉.toNat with hT
  have hpL : ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : D.what₂.LL) ≠ 0 := by
    exact_mod_cast (D.what₂.places.resChar_prime w).ne_zero
  have hpK : ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : (D.gat u w hg).Kf) ≠ 0 := by
    rw [← map_natCast (c.embW u w hg)]
    exact (map_ne_zero _).2 hpL
  have hyx' : ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : (D.gat u w hg).Kf) ^ T • y = c.cmp u w hg (D.nuIn u x) := by
    rw [← hyx]
    simp only [LinearMap.smul_apply, LinearMap.id_apply]
    rw [← map_natCast (algebraMap (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf), ← map_pow, algebraMap_smul]
  set x' : (D.div.Nu u).V := (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : D.what₂.LL) ^ T)⁻¹ • x with hx'
  have hz : c.cmp u w hg (D.nuIn u x') = y := by
    rw [hx', map_smul, c.cmp_smul, ← hyx', map_inv₀, map_pow, map_natCast, inv_smul_smul₀ (pow_ne_zero _ hpK)]
  rw [SetLike.mem_coe, ← c.cmp_M u w hg, ← hz] at hy
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hsz⟩ := descent (D.plEq w) ((D.fm u).lat 0).b (c.embW u w hg) (c.cmp u w hg)
    (fun a => (val_le_one_iff D w a).trans (c.embW_int u w hg a)) (c.cmp_smul u w hg) (c.cmp_li u w hg)
    ((D.fm u).lat 0).L ((D.fm u).lat_good 0).2.1 _ hy
  refine ⟨s, hs, ?_⟩
  have hsx : s • x = (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : 𝓞 D.what₂.LL) ^ T) • (s • x') := by
    rw [smul_comm, hx', ← algebraMap_smul D.what₂.LL ((((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : 𝓞 D.what₂.LL)) ^ T), map_pow,
      map_natCast, smul_inv_smul₀ (pow_ne_zero _ hpL)]
  rw [hsx]
  refine Submodule.smul_mem_smul ?_ ?_
  · have hle : ⌊DV.ur D w (tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w))⌋₊ ≤ D.what₂.places.ramIdx w * T := by
      apply Nat.floor_le_of_le
      unfold DV.ur
      push_cast
      apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left _ (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
      have h2 : ((⌈tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w)⌉ : ℤ) : ℝ) ≤ (T : ℝ) := by
        exact_mod_cast Int.self_le_toNat _
      exact le_trans (Int.le_ceil _) h2
    apply Ideal.pow_le_pow_right hle
    rw [pow_mul]
    exact Ideal.pow_mem_pow (resChar_mem_pow D w) T
  · change (D.nuIn u).restrictScalars (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) (s • x') ∈ ((D.fm u).lat 0).L
    rw [map_smul]
    exact hsz

def LenServed (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ≠ ∅ →
    ∃ l : ℕ, Module.length (D.gat u w hg).O
        (↥((D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg) ⧸
          (NP X Y K u w hg).comap ((D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg).subtype) = l ∧
      (l : ℝ) = (L.lenMN u w : ℝ) * vUnit (D.gat u w hg)


[GLG_integral_operator_01] actual Weyl, dual-Weyl, component function lattices and incoming-neighbor p^ArV operator
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/DataG.lean#L11-L199
Range SHA256: 7cfbab1bad83e6925412f51c60708eba10a9c256912cc35c851b2cb63f3e0f98
def deltaL (R K : Type*) [CommRing R] [Field K] [Algebra R K] (k : ℕ) (ν : Fin k → ℕ)
    (g : Matrix (Fin k) (Fin k) K) : Submodule R (MvPolynomial (Fin k × Fin k) K) :=
  Submodule.comap ((rowAct K (n := k) g).toLinearMap.restrictScalars R)
    (Submodule.map (mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId R K)).toLinearMap (weyl R k k le_rfl ν))

def deltaLTw (R K : Type*) [CommRing R] [Field K] [Algebra R K] (m : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (g : Matrix (Fin (m + 1)) (Fin (m + 1)) K) : Submodule R (MvPolynomial (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1)) K) :=
  Submodule.map ((LinearMap.lsmul K (MvPolynomial (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1)) K)
      (g.det ^ (-(lam (Fin.last m))))).restrictScalars R)
    (deltaL R K (m + 1) (nuOf lam) g)

def Dl0 (O : Type*) {Kf : Type*} [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf] (m : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (Λ : Submodule O (Fin (m + 1) → Kf)) : Submodule O (MvPolynomial (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1)) Kf) :=
  if Antitone lam then
    ⨅ (g : Matrix (Fin (m + 1)) (Fin (m + 1)) Kf) (_ : IsUnit g.det ∧ Λ = colSpan O g), deltaLTw O Kf m lam g
  else ⊥

def HtenW (O : Type*) {Kf : Type*} [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf] {n' m : ℕ}
    (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) (Λ : Fin n' → Submodule O (Fin (m + 1) → Kf)) :
    Submodule O (MvPolynomial (Fin n' × (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1))) Kf) :=
  tensorLat O Kf fun s => Dl0 O m (lamAt lam0 s) (Λ s)

structure Places (p : ℕ) (O Kf : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Field Kf]
    [Algebra O Kf] where
  w : O
  nV : ℕ
  n' : Fin nV → ℕ
  W : Fin nV → Type
  Qq : Fin nV → Type
  [ringW : ∀ v, CommRing (W v)]
  [domW : ∀ v, IsDomain (W v)]
  [dvrW : ∀ v, IsDiscreteValuationRing (W v)]
  [fieldQ : ∀ v, Field (Qq v)]
  [algWQ : ∀ v, Algebra (W v) (Qq v)]
  ι : ∀ v, Qq v →+* Kf
  σ : ∀ v, Qq v ≃+* Qq v
  j : ∀ v, W v →+* O
  setting : ∀ v, HidaSetting p (n' v) (W v) (Qq v) O Kf (ι v) (σ v) (j v) w
  m : ℕ
  r : ℕ
  lam0 : ∀ v, Fin (n' v) → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ

  dom : ∀ v, IsDominant (lam0 v)

attribute [instance] Places.ringW Places.domW Places.dvrW Places.fieldQ Places.algWQ

namespace Places

variable {p : ℕ} {O Kf : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
  (P : Places p O Kf)

def Tup : Type := ∀ v, Submodule (P.W v) (Fin (P.m + 1) → P.Qq v)

def IsLatTup (L : P.Tup) : Prop := ∀ v, IsLattice (P.W v) (L v)

abbrev Xsp : Type := MvPolynomial (Σ v : Fin P.nV, Fin (P.n' v) × (Fin (P.m + 1) × Fin (P.m + 1))) Kf

def vEmb (v : Fin P.nV) : MvPolynomial (Fin (P.n' v) × (Fin (P.m + 1) × Fin (P.m + 1))) Kf →ₐ[Kf] P.Xsp :=
  rename fun x => ⟨v, x⟩

def tenV (M : ∀ v, Submodule O (MvPolynomial (Fin (P.n' v) × (Fin (P.m + 1) × Fin (P.m + 1))) Kf)) :
    Submodule O P.Xsp :=
  Submodule.span O {X | ∃ F : ∀ v, MvPolynomial (Fin (P.n' v) × (Fin (P.m + 1) × Fin (P.m + 1))) Kf,
    (∀ v, F v ∈ M v) ∧ X = ∏ v, P.vEmb v (F v)}

def Htup (L : P.Tup) : Submodule O P.Xsp :=
  P.tenV fun v => Hten O (P.lam0 v) fun s => extO O (P.ι v) (P.σ v) s.val (L v)

def HtupW (L : P.Tup) : Submodule O P.Xsp :=
  P.tenV fun v => HtenW O (P.lam0 v) fun s => extO O (P.ι v) (P.σ v) s.val (L v)

def sys (Φ : ∀ v, Submodule (P.W v) (Fin (P.m + 1) → P.Qq v) →
    Submodule O (MvPolynomial (Fin (P.n' v) × (Fin (P.m + 1) × Fin (P.m + 1))) Kf)) (L : P.Tup) :
    Submodule O P.Xsp :=
  P.tenV fun v => Φ v (L v)

def Vtup : Submodule Kf P.Xsp :=
  Submodule.span Kf {X | ∃ F : ∀ v, MvPolynomial (Fin (P.n' v) × (Fin (P.m + 1) × Fin (P.m + 1))) Kf,
    (∀ v, F v ∈ Vsp Kf (P.lam0 v)) ∧ X = ∏ v, P.vEmb v (F v)}

def ArV (v : Fin P.nV) : ℤ := Ar P.r (P.lam0 v)

def gPos (v : Fin P.nV) (s : Fin (P.n' v)) (A : Matrix (Fin (P.m + 1)) (Fin (P.m + 1)) (P.Qq v)) :
    Matrix (Fin (P.m + 1)) (Fin (P.m + 1)) Kf :=
  fun a b => P.ι v ((P.σ v ^ s.val) (A a b))

def subst (G : ∀ v, Fin (P.n' v) → Matrix (Fin (P.m + 1)) (Fin (P.m + 1)) Kf) : P.Xsp →ₐ[Kf] P.Xsp :=
  aeval fun x => ∑ b : Fin (P.m + 1), C (G x.1 x.2.1 x.2.2.1 b) * X ⟨x.1, (x.2.1, (b, x.2.2.2))⟩

def natAct (A : ∀ v, GL (Fin (P.m + 1)) (P.Qq v)) : P.Xsp →ₗ[Kf] P.Xsp :=
  (∏ v, ∏ s : Fin (P.n' v), (P.gPos v s (A v : Matrix _ _ _)).det ^ (-(lamAt (P.lam0 v) s (Fin.last P.m)))) •
    (P.subst fun v s => P.gPos v s ((A v)⁻¹ : GL (Fin (P.m + 1)) (P.Qq v))).toLinearMap

end Places

structure Level {p : ℕ} {O Kf : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Field Kf]
    [Algebra O Kf] (P : Places p O Kf) where
  Γ : Type
  [grp : Group Γ]
  act : ∀ v, Γ →* GL (Fin (P.m + 1)) (P.Qq v)
  χ : Γ →* Oˣ

attribute [instance] Level.grp

namespace Level

variable {p : ℕ} {O Kf : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
  {P : Places p O Kf} (C : Level P)

def smul (γ : C.Γ) (L : P.Tup) : P.Tup :=
  fun v => (L v).map ((Matrix.mulVecLin ((C.act v γ : GL (Fin (P.m + 1)) (P.Qq v)) :
    Matrix (Fin (P.m + 1)) (Fin (P.m + 1)) (P.Qq v))).restrictScalars (P.W v))

def actX (γ : C.Γ) : P.Xsp →ₗ[Kf] P.Xsp := P.natAct fun v => C.act v γ

def ActH : Prop := ∀ (γ : C.Γ) (L : P.Tup), P.IsLatTup L →
  (P.Htup L).map ((C.actX γ).restrictScalars O) = P.Htup (C.smul γ L)

def ActHW : Prop := ∀ (γ : C.Γ) (L : P.Tup), P.IsLatTup L →
  (P.HtupW L).map ((C.actX γ).restrictScalars O) = P.HtupW (C.smul γ L)

def funMod (Hv : P.Tup → Submodule O P.Xsp) : Submodule O (P.Tup → P.Xsp) :=
  (⨅ (L : P.Tup) (_ : P.IsLatTup L), (Hv L).comap (LinearMap.proj L)) ⊓
  (⨅ (L : P.Tup) (_ : ¬ P.IsLatTup L), LinearMap.ker (LinearMap.proj (R := O) (φ := fun _ : P.Tup => P.Xsp) L)) ⊓
  ⨅ (γ : C.Γ) (L : P.Tup), LinearMap.ker
    (LinearMap.proj (R := O) (φ := fun _ : P.Tup => P.Xsp) (C.smul γ L) -
      ((C.χ γ : O) • ((C.actX γ).restrictScalars O)) ∘ₗ
        LinearMap.proj (R := O) (φ := fun _ : P.Tup => P.Xsp) L)

def Mna : Submodule O (P.Tup → P.Xsp) := C.funMod P.Htup

def M : Submodule O (P.Tup → P.Xsp) := C.funMod P.HtupW

def dna : ℕ := Module.finrank O C.Mna

def d : ℕ := Module.finrank O C.M

def yv (v : Fin P.nV) (F : P.Tup → P.Xsp) : P.Tup → P.Xsp :=
  fun L => ((p : Kf) ^ P.ArV v) •
    ∑ᶠ (L'' : Submodule (P.W v) (Fin (P.m + 1) → P.Qq v)) (_ : IsEdge p (P.n' v) P.r ⟨L'', L v⟩),
      F (Function.update L v L'')

def Sandwich (e : ℕ) : Prop :=
  C.Mna.map (((p : O) ^ e) • LinearMap.id) ≤ C.M ∧ C.M ≤ C.Mna

def TrivStab : Prop := ∀ (γ : C.Γ) (L : P.Tup), P.IsLatTup L → C.smul γ L = L → γ = 1

def FinClasses : Prop :=
  ∃ R : Finset P.Tup, (∀ L ∈ R, P.IsLatTup L) ∧ ∀ L, P.IsLatTup L → ∃ L₀ ∈ R, ∃ γ : C.Γ, L = C.smul γ L₀

end Level

structure GAtP (p : ℕ) where
  O : Type
  Kf : Type
  [ringO : CommRing O]
  [domO : IsDomain O]
  [dvrO : IsDiscreteValuationRing O]
  [fieldK : Field Kf]
  [algOK : Algebra O Kf]
  P : Places p O Kf
  comp : Type
  [finComp : Fintype comp]
  lev : comp → Level P
  e : ℕ

attribute [instance] GAtP.ringO GAtP.domO GAtP.dvrO GAtP.fieldK GAtP.algOK GAtP.finComp

namespace GAtP

variable {p : ℕ} (G : GAtP p)

abbrev Fn : Type := G.comp → G.P.Tup → G.P.Xsp

def funMod (Hv : G.P.Tup → Submodule G.O G.P.Xsp) : Submodule G.O G.Fn :=
  Submodule.pi Set.univ fun i => (G.lev i).funMod Hv

def Mna : Submodule G.O G.Fn := G.funMod G.P.Htup

def M : Submodule G.O G.Fn := G.funMod G.P.HtupW

def d : ℕ := Module.finrank G.O G.M

def dna : ℕ := Module.finrank G.O G.Mna

def yv (v : Fin G.P.nV) (F : G.Fn) : G.Fn := fun i => Level.yv (P := G.P) v (F i)

def Sandwich : Prop :=
  G.Mna.map (((p : G.O) ^ G.e) • LinearMap.id) ≤ G.M ∧ G.M ≤ G.Mna


[GLG_integral_operator_02] original coefficient DVR and fraction fields, unramified uniformizer and z^2=p setting
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/UnramifiedQuadraticSetting.lean#L12-L28

structure HidaSetting (p n' : ℕ) (W Qq O Kf : Type) [CommRing W] [IsDomain W] [IsDiscreteValuationRing W] [Field Qq]
    [Algebra W Qq] [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) (j : W →+* O) (w : O) : Prop where
  prime : p.Prime
  five_le : 5 ≤ p
  two_le : 2 ≤ n'
  even : Even n'
  frac_alt : IsFractionRing W Qq
  frac_O : IsFractionRing O Kf
  p_unif : Irreducible (p : W)
  res_card : Nat.card (W ⧸ Ideal.span {(p : W)}) = p ^ n'
  eq_sum_herm_smul_frame_of_inW : IsAdicComplete (Ideal.span {(p : W)}) W
  frob : ∀ x : W, ∃ y : W, σ (algebraMap W Qq x) = algebraMap W Qq (x ^ p + (p : W) * y)
  period : σ ^ n' = 1
  ι_comp : ∀ x : W, ι (algebraMap W Qq x) = algebraMap O Kf (j x)
  finite : j.Finite
  complete_O : IsAdicComplete (IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal O) O


[GLG_integral_operator_03] signed weights, dual-Weyl model, actual edges and their elementary-divisor basis
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/UnramifiedQuadraticSetting.lean#L70-L112
Range SHA256: cbd5d3dd8fdbd6a92365ab4fedfea62d1b5963351bf8ab93148acd45d1a6ffe5
def H0 (O : Type*) {Kf : Type*} [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf] (m : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (Λ : Submodule O (Fin (m + 1) → Kf)) : Submodule O (MvPolynomial (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1)) Kf) :=
  if Antitone lam then
    ⨅ (g : Matrix (Fin (m + 1)) (Fin (m + 1)) Kf) (_ : IsUnit g.det ∧ Λ = colSpan O g), nablaLTw O Kf m lam g
  else ⊥

def Hten (O : Type*) {Kf : Type*} [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf] {n' m : ℕ}
    (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) (Λ : Fin n' → Submodule O (Fin (m + 1) → Kf)) :
    Submodule O (MvPolynomial (Fin n' × (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1))) Kf) :=
  tensorLat O Kf fun s => H0 O m (lamAt lam0 s) (Λ s)

def nuOf {m : ℕ} (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) : Fin (m + 1) → ℕ :=
  fun t => (lam t - lam (Fin.last m)).toNat

def Vsp (Kf : Type*) [Field Kf] {n' m : ℕ} (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) :
    Submodule Kf (MvPolynomial (Fin n' × (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1))) Kf) :=
  tensorLat Kf Kf fun s => if Antitone (lamAt lam0 s) then dualWeyl Kf (m + 1) (m + 1) (nuOf (lamAt lam0 s)) else ⊥

def dimV (Kf : Type*) [Field Kf] {n' m : ℕ} (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) : ℕ :=
  Module.finrank Kf (Vsp Kf lam0)

def toV {O Kf : Type*} [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf] {n' m : ℕ} (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (M : Submodule O (MvPolynomial (Fin n' × (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1))) Kf)) : Submodule O (Vsp Kf lam0) :=
  M.comap ((Vsp Kf lam0).subtype.restrictScalars O)

def HL {W Qq O Kf : Type*} [CommRing W] [Field Qq] [Algebra W Qq] [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) {n' m : ℕ} (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (L : Submodule W (Fin (m + 1) → Qq)) : Submodule O (Vsp Kf lam0) :=
  toV lam0 (Hten O lam0 fun s => extO O ι σ s.val L)

structure Edge (W Qq : Type*) [CommRing W] [Field Qq] [Algebra W Qq] (k : ℕ) where
  src : Submodule W (Fin k → Qq)
  tgt : Submodule W (Fin k → Qq)

def IsEdge {W Qq : Type*} [CommRing W] [Field Qq] [Algebra W Qq] (p n' r : ℕ) {k : ℕ} (e : Edge W Qq k) : Prop :=
  IsLattice W e.src ∧ IsLattice W e.tgt ∧ e.src ≤ e.tgt ∧ (∀ x ∈ e.tgt, (p : W) • x ∈ e.src) ∧
    Nat.card (↥e.tgt ⧸ Submodule.comap e.tgt.subtype e.src) = p ^ (n' * r)

def IsEdgeBasis {W Qq : Type*} [CommRing W] [Field Qq] [Algebra W Qq] (p r : ℕ) {k : ℕ} (e : Edge W Qq k)
    (g : Matrix (Fin k) (Fin k) Qq) : Prop :=
  IsUnit g.det ∧ e.src = colSpan W g ∧
    e.tgt = colSpan W (g * Matrix.diagonal fun c => if c.val < r then ((p : Qq))⁻¹ else 1)

  (∀ v, ∀ F ∈ G.Mna, G.yv v F ∈ G.Mna) ∧ ∀ v, ∀ F ∈ G.M, G.yv v F ∈ G.M

[GLG_integral_operator_05] row homogeneity, dominant support and integral Weyl diagonal scaling
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/GroupLattices/FockModels/OperatorsPreserveLattices.lean#L15-L126
Range SHA256: eae780a461bb259f8ff7a23b8b926c9dc05ad50818a563218606f0b8caf58652
def Wc {r n : ℕ} (c : Fin r) : Fin r × Fin n → ℤ := fun v => ((lgraded_wa (n := n) c v : ℕ) : ℤ)

theorem weight_wc {r n : ℕ} (c : Fin r) (d : Fin r × Fin n →₀ ℕ) :
    Finsupp.weight (Wc c) d = ((Finsupp.weight (rowW r n) d c : ℕ) : ℤ) := by
  rw [Finsupp.weight_apply, Finsupp.weight_apply, Finsupp.sum, Finsupp.sum, Finset.sum_apply,
    Nat.cast_sum]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun v _ => ?_
  simp only [Wc, lgraded_wa, rowW, Pi.smul_apply, Pi.single_apply, smul_eq_mul]
  split_ifs with h1 h2 h2 <;> simp_all

theorem rowDiv_hom (R : Type) [CommRing R] {r n : ℕ} (a b : Fin r) (k : ℕ) (μ : Fin r → ℕ)
    (y : MvPolynomial (Fin r × Fin n) R) (hy : IsWeightedHomogeneous (rowW r n) y μ) :
    ∃ μ', IsWeightedHomogeneous (rowW r n) (rowDiv R a b k y) μ' := by
  classical
  set δ : Fin r → ℤ := fun c => ((if a = c then 1 else 0 : ℕ) : ℤ) - ((if b = c then 1 else 0 : ℕ) : ℤ)
    with hδ
  have hc : ∀ c : Fin r, IsWeightedHomogeneous (Wc c) y (μ c : ℤ) := by
    intro c d hd
    rw [weight_wc, hy hd]
  have hs : ∀ c : Fin r, ∀ s : Fin n, Wc c (a, s) = Wc c (b, s) + δ c := by
    intro c s
    simp only [hδ, Wc, lgraded_wa]
    ring
  refine ⟨fun c => ((μ c : ℤ) + (k : ℤ) * δ c).toNat, ?_⟩
  intro d hd
  funext c
  have h1 := lehw_rowDiv_shift R (Wc c) (δ c) a b (hs c) (μ c) k y (hc c) hd
  rw [weight_wc] at h1
  omega

theorem applyWord_hom (R : Type) [CommRing R] {r n : ℕ} (hn : n ≤ r) (ν : Fin n → ℕ) (hν : Antitone ν)
    (wd : List (Fin r × Fin r × ℕ)) : ∃ μ, IsWeightedHomogeneous (rowW r n) (applyWord R wd (hw R hn ν)) μ := by
  have hwhom : ∃ μ0, IsWeightedHomogeneous (rowW r n) (hw R hn ν) μ0 :=
    ⟨_, IsWeightedHomogeneous.prod _ _ _ fun s _ => (lpMinor_rowHom R (s.val + 1) _ _).pow _⟩
  induction wd with
  | nil => exact hwhom
  | cons x wd ih =>
    obtain ⟨μ, hμ⟩ := ih
    exact rowDiv_hom R x.1 x.2.1 x.2.2 μ _ hμ

theorem dom_support (R : Type) [CommRing R] [IsDomain R] (k n : ℕ) (ν : Fin n → ℕ) (r : ℕ) :
    dualWeyl R k n ν ≤ restrictSupport R
      {m | (∑ a : Fin k with a.val < r, rowDeg m a) ≤ ∑ t : Fin n with t.val < r, ν t} := by
  obtain ⟨b, hb, h2⟩ := sSBasis R k n ν
  rw [← hb.2.1, Submodule.span_le]
  rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨-, -, K, sz, hsz, I, hshape, -, hbm⟩ := h2 i
  rw [SetLike.mem_coe, mem_restrictSupport_iff]
  intro m hm
  have h3 := (bidetDominance R k n K sz hsz I m (by rw [← hbm]; exact hm)).1 r
  rw [hshape] at h3
  exact h3

theorem hom_map {R S σ M : Type} [CommRing R] [CommRing S] [AddCommMonoid M] (φ : R →+* S) (wt : σ → M)
    (f : MvPolynomial σ R) (μ : M) (h : IsWeightedHomogeneous wt f μ) : IsWeightedHomogeneous wt (map φ f) μ := by
  intro d hd
  apply h
  intro h0
  apply hd
  rw [MvPolynomial.coeff_map, h0, map_zero]

theorem weyl_scale (O Kf : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf] (k r : ℕ) (ν : Fin k → ℕ)
    (hν : Antitone ν) (w : O) (hw0 : algebraMap O Kf w ≠ 0) :
    ∀ f ∈ weyl O k k le_rfl ν,
      (algebraMap O Kf w ^ (∑ t : Fin k with t.val < r, ν t)) •
          rowAct Kf (lb_D k r (algebraMap O Kf w)) (map (algebraMap O Kf) f) ∈
        Submodule.map (mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId O Kf)).toLinearMap (weyl O k k le_rfl ν) := by
  classical
  set c : Kf := algebraMap O Kf w with hc
  set N : ℕ := ∑ t : Fin k with t.val < r, ν t with hN
  let T : MvPolynomial (Fin k × Fin k) O →ₗ[O] MvPolynomial (Fin k × Fin k) Kf :=
    ((LinearMap.lsmul Kf _ (c ^ N)).restrictScalars O) ∘ₗ
      ((rowAct Kf (n := k) (lb_D k r c)).toLinearMap.restrictScalars O) ∘ₗ
        (mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId O Kf)).toLinearMap
  have hT : ∀ x, T x = c ^ N • rowAct Kf (lb_D k r c) (map (algebraMap O Kf) x) := fun x => rfl
  have key : weyl O k k le_rfl ν ≤
      (Submodule.map (mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId O Kf)).toLinearMap (weyl O k k le_rfl ν)).comap T := by
    unfold weyl
    rw [Submodule.span_le]
    rintro _ ⟨wd, rfl⟩
    set f := applyWord O wd (hw O le_rfl ν) with hf
    have hfW : f ∈ weyl O k k le_rfl ν := Submodule.subset_span ⟨wd, rfl⟩
    show T f ∈ Submodule.map (mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId O Kf)).toLinearMap (weyl O k k le_rfl ν)
    by_cases h0 : f = 0
    · rw [h0, map_zero]; exact Submodule.zero_mem _
    obtain ⟨μ, hμ⟩ := applyWord_hom O le_rfl ν hν wd
    obtain ⟨d0, hd0⟩ := MvPolynomial.support_nonempty.mpr h0
    have hdom := ((mem_restrictSupport_iff O).1 (dom_support O k k ν r (weylLeDual O k k le_rfl ν hν hfW))) hd0
    have hwt : ∀ a, rowDeg d0 a = μ a := by
      intro a
      rw [← lehw_weight_rowW, hμ (mem_support_iff.mp hd0)]
    set π' : ℕ := ∑ a : Fin k with a.val < r, μ a with hπ
    have hle : π' ≤ N := by
      have h1 : (∑ a : Fin k with a.val < r, rowDeg d0 a) = π' := Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => hwt a
      rw [← h1]
      exact hdom
    refine ⟨w ^ (N - π') • f, Submodule.smul_mem _ _ hfW, ?_⟩
    rw [hT]
    unfold lb_D
    rw [rowActDiagWeight Kf k k _ μ _ (hom_map _ _ _ _ hμ), smul_smul]
    have h2 : ∏ a : Fin k, (if a.val < r then c⁻¹ else 1) ^ μ a = (c⁻¹) ^ π' := by
      rw [hπ, ← Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum, Finset.prod_filter]
      apply Finset.prod_congr rfl
      intro a _
      split_ifs <;> simp
    rw [h2]
    have h4 : c ^ N * (c⁻¹) ^ π' = c ^ (N - π') := by
      rw [inv_pow, pow_sub₀ c hw0 hle]
    rw [h4]
    show map (algebraMap O Kf) (w ^ (N - π') • f) = _
    rw [smul_eq_C_mul, map_mul, map_C, map_pow, ← smul_eq_C_mul]
  exact fun f hf => key hf


[GLG_integral_operator_06] signed determinant twist and basis-independent Weyl model
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/GroupLattices/FockModels/OperatorsPreserveLattices.lean#L128-L234
Range SHA256: 293f44b3781eb88a26af836ad3a7c652f98de2ece658fb22cac82f4ab8d5abbe
theorem dl_P (O Kf : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (m r : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) (hlam : Antitone lam)
    (w : O) (hw : algebraMap O Kf w ≠ 0) (g : Matrix (Fin (m + 1)) (Fin (m + 1)) Kf)
    (F : MvPolynomial (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1)) Kf) (hF : F ∈ deltaLTw O Kf m lam g) :
    (algebraMap O Kf w ^ (∑ i : Fin (m + 1) with i.val < r, lam i)) • F ∈
      deltaLTw O Kf m lam (g * lb_D (m + 1) r (algebraMap O Kf w)) := by
  classical
  set c : Kf := algebraMap O Kf w with hc
  set e : ℤ := lam (Fin.last m) with he
  set A : ℤ := ∑ i : Fin (m + 1) with i.val < r, lam i with hA
  set N : ℕ := ∑ t : Fin (m + 1) with t.val < r, nuOf lam t with hN
  obtain ⟨F0, hF0, rfl⟩ := hF
  obtain ⟨f, hf, hfe⟩ := hF0
  have hfe' : map (algebraMap O Kf) f = rowAct Kf g F0 := hfe
  refine ⟨c ^ N • F0, ?_, ?_⟩
  · show rowAct Kf (g * lb_D (m + 1) r c) (c ^ N • F0) ∈
      Submodule.map (mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId O Kf)).toLinearMap (weyl O (m + 1) (m + 1) le_rfl (nuOf lam))
    have h1 := rowActMul Kf (m + 1) (m + 1) (lb_D (m + 1) r c) g
    rw [← h1, map_smul, AlgHom.comp_apply, ← hfe']
    exact weyl_scale O Kf (m + 1) r (nuOf lam) (kp_nuOf_antitone lam hlam) w hw f hf
  · show (g * lb_D (m + 1) r c).det ^ (-e) • (c ^ N • F0) = c ^ A • (g.det ^ (-e) • F0)
    rw [smul_smul, smul_smul]
    congr 1
    set ρ : ℕ := (Finset.univ.filter fun a : Fin (m + 1) => a.val < r).card with hρ
    have h1 : (g * lb_D (m + 1) r c).det = g.det * (c⁻¹) ^ ρ := by
      rw [Matrix.det_mul, lb_D, Matrix.det_diagonal, Finset.prod_ite, Finset.prod_const,
        Finset.prod_const_one, mul_one]
    have h3 : A = (N : ℤ) + ρ * e := by
      rw [hA, hN, hρ]
      push_cast
      rw [Finset.card_eq_sum_ones]
      push_cast
      rw [Finset.sum_mul, ← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
      apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
      intro a _
      have : 0 ≤ lam a - lam (Fin.last m) := sub_nonneg.2 (hlam (Fin.le_last a))
      simp only [nuOf, Int.toNat_of_nonneg this, he]
      ring
    have h5 : ((c⁻¹) ^ ρ) ^ (-e) = c ^ ((ρ : ℤ) * e) := by
      rw [inv_pow, inv_zpow, zpow_neg, inv_inv, ← zpow_natCast c ρ, ← zpow_mul]
    rw [h1, h3, mul_zpow, h5, zpow_add₀ hw, zpow_natCast]
    ring

theorem dl_indep {O Kf : Type} [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (hinj : Function.Injective (algebraMap O Kf)) (m : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (g h : Matrix (Fin (m + 1)) (Fin (m + 1)) Kf) (hg : IsUnit g.det) (heq : colSpan O g = colSpan O h) :
    deltaLTw O Kf m lam g ≤ deltaLTw O Kf m lam h := by
  obtain ⟨U, hU⟩ := la_factor g h heq.ge
  obtain ⟨U2, hU2⟩ := la_factor h g heq.le
  have hUU : U * U2 = 1 := by
    have h1 : g * (U * U2).map (algebraMap O Kf) = g * 1 := by
      rw [Matrix.mul_one, Matrix.map_mul, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, ← hU, ← hU2]
    have hgu : IsUnit g := (Matrix.isUnit_iff_isUnit_det g).2 hg
    have h2 : (U * U2).map (algebraMap O Kf) = 1 := hgu.mul_left_cancel h1
    apply Matrix.map_injective hinj
    beta_reduce
    rw [h2]
    simp
  have hdet : U.det * U2.det = 1 := by rw [← Matrix.det_mul, hUU, Matrix.det_one]
  let u : Oˣ := ⟨U.det, U2.det, hdet, by rw [mul_comm]; exact hdet⟩
  let γ : GL (Fin (m + 1)) O := ⟨U2, U, mul_eq_one_comm.1 hUU, hUU⟩
  have hdU : (U.map (algebraMap O Kf)).det = algebraMap O Kf U.det := by
    rw [RingHom.map_det]; rfl
  have hc : algebraMap O Kf U.det ≠ 0 := by
    intro h0
    have := congrArg (algebraMap O Kf) hdet
    rw [map_mul, h0, zero_mul, map_one] at this
    exact zero_ne_one this
  have hz : ∀ e : ℤ, algebraMap O Kf ((u ^ e : Oˣ) : O) = (algebraMap O Kf U.det) ^ e := by
    intro e
    have h1 : ((Units.map (algebraMap O Kf : O →* Kf) u ^ e : Kfˣ) : Kf) = (algebraMap O Kf U.det) ^ e := by
      rw [Units.val_zpow_eq_zpow_val]; rfl
    rw [← h1, ← map_zpow]
    rfl
  rintro _ ⟨F, hF, rfl⟩
  have hFh : F ∈ deltaL O Kf (m + 1) (nuOf lam) h := by
    obtain ⟨f, hf, hfe⟩ := hF
    have hst : rowAct O U f ∈ weyl O (m + 1) (m + 1) le_rfl (nuOf lam) :=
      Formalism.UG.P.weyl_module_GL_stable O (m + 1) (m + 1) le_rfl (nuOf lam) γ f hf
    refine ⟨rowAct O U f, hst, ?_⟩
    have h3 : rowAct Kf (n := m + 1) h = (rowAct Kf (U.map (algebraMap O Kf))).comp (rowAct Kf g) := by
      rw [rowActMul, hU]
    change map (algebraMap O Kf) (rowAct O U f) = rowAct Kf h F
    rw [h3, AlgHom.comp_apply, ← la_rowAct_map]
    congr 1
  refine ⟨((u ^ (lam (Fin.last m)) : Oˣ) : O) • F, Submodule.smul_mem _ _ hFh, ?_⟩
  change h.det ^ (-(lam (Fin.last m))) • (((u ^ (lam (Fin.last m)) : Oˣ) : O) • F) =
    g.det ^ (-(lam (Fin.last m))) • F
  rw [← algebraMap_smul Kf (((u ^ (lam (Fin.last m)) : Oˣ) : O)) F, hz, smul_smul, hU, Matrix.det_mul, hdU,
    mul_zpow, mul_assoc, ← zpow_add₀ hc, neg_add_cancel, zpow_zero, mul_one]

theorem dl_pos (O Kf : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (hinj : Function.Injective (algebraMap O Kf)) (m r : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) (hlam : Antitone lam)
    (w : O) (hw : algebraMap O Kf w ≠ 0) (g : Matrix (Fin (m + 1)) (Fin (m + 1)) Kf) (hg : IsUnit g.det)
    (F : MvPolynomial (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1)) Kf) (hF : F ∈ Dl0 O m lam (colSpan O g)) :
    (algebraMap O Kf w ^ (∑ i : Fin (m + 1) with i.val < r, lam i)) • F ∈
      Dl0 O m lam (colSpan O (g * lb_D (m + 1) r (algebraMap O Kf w))) := by
  have hgD : IsUnit (g * lb_D (m + 1) r (algebraMap O Kf w)).det := by
    rw [Matrix.det_mul]; exact hg.mul (lb_D_det _ _ _ hw)
  unfold Dl0 at hF ⊢
  rw [if_pos hlam] at hF ⊢
  have h1 : F ∈ deltaLTw O Kf m lam g := (iInf₂_le g ⟨hg, rfl⟩ : _ ≤ deltaLTw O Kf m lam g) hF
  have h2 := dl_P O Kf m r lam hlam w hw g F h1
  exact (le_iInf₂ fun h hh => dl_indep hinj m lam _ h hgD hh.2 :
    deltaLTw O Kf m lam (g * lb_D (m + 1) r (algebraMap O Kf w)) ≤ _) h2

variable {p : ℕ}


[GLG_integral_operator_07] two original edge steps, exact p^ArV factor, and both full operator endpoints
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/GroupLattices/FockModels/OperatorsPreserveLattices.lean#L236-L344
Range SHA256: 3e8a5537d7672f6af19850d35d28e7d42ea4c69241f3481a52ce15a34b624551
theorem steps_gen (G : GAtP p) (v : Fin G.P.nV)
    (Φ : (Fin (G.P.m + 1) → ℤ) → Submodule G.O (Fin (G.P.m + 1) → G.Kf) →
      Submodule G.O (MvPolynomial (Fin (G.P.m + 1) × Fin (G.P.m + 1)) G.Kf))
    (hΦ : ∀ (lam : Fin (G.P.m + 1) → ℤ), Antitone lam → ∀ g : Matrix (Fin (G.P.m + 1)) (Fin (G.P.m + 1)) G.Kf,
      IsUnit g.det → ∀ F ∈ Φ lam (colSpan G.O g),
        (algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w ^ (∑ i : Fin (G.P.m + 1) with i.val < G.P.r, lam i)) • F ∈
          Φ lam (colSpan G.O (g * lb_D (G.P.m + 1) G.P.r (algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w))))
    (e : Edge (G.P.W v) (G.P.Qq v) (G.P.m + 1)) (he : IsEdge p (G.P.n' v) G.P.r e) :
    ∀ P ∈ tensorLat G.O G.Kf (fun s : Fin (G.P.n' v) =>
        Φ (lamAt (G.P.lam0 v) s) (extO G.O (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s.val e.src)),
      ((p : G.Kf) ^ G.P.ArV v) • P ∈ tensorLat G.O G.Kf (fun s : Fin (G.P.n' v) =>
        Φ (lamAt (G.P.lam0 v) s) (extO G.O (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s.val e.tgt)) := by
  obtain ⟨gs, hgs, h1, -, h3⟩ := lb_edgeLat_of_basis G v (restEdgeBasis G v) e he
  have hw := ch_w_ne_zero G v
  set c : G.Kf := algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w with hc
  have hgD : ∀ s, IsUnit (gs s * lb_D (G.P.m + 1) G.P.r c).det := by
    intro s
    rw [Matrix.det_mul]; exact (hgs s).mul (lb_D_det _ _ _ hw)
  have hA : ∏ s : Fin (G.P.n' v), (c ^ (∑ i : Fin (G.P.m + 1) with i.val < G.P.r, lamAt (G.P.lam0 v) s i) *
      c ^ (∑ i : Fin (G.P.m + 1) with i.val < G.P.r, lamAt (G.P.lam0 v) s i)) = (p : G.Kf) ^ G.P.ArV v := by
    rw [Finset.prod_mul_distrib, lb_zpow_sum _ hw, nt_p_eq G v, ← hc]
    unfold Places.ArV Ar
    rw [← zpow_natCast c 2, ← zpow_mul, ← zpow_add₀ hw]
    congr 1
    ring
  intro P hP
  rw [← hA]
  refine lb_tensor G.O G.Kf _ _ _ (fun s F hF => ?_) P hP
  beta_reduce at hF ⊢
  rw [h1] at hF
  rw [h3, mul_smul]
  have hl := kp_lamAt_antitone _ (G.P.dom v) s
  exact hΦ _ hl _ (hgD _) _ (hΦ _ hl _ (hgs _) F hF)

theorem yv_pres (G : GAtP p) (v : Fin G.P.nV)
    (Φ : ∀ u, Submodule (G.P.W u) (Fin (G.P.m + 1) → G.P.Qq u) →
      Submodule G.O (MvPolynomial (Fin (G.P.n' u) × (Fin (G.P.m + 1) × Fin (G.P.m + 1))) G.Kf))
    (hloc : ∀ e : Edge (G.P.W v) (G.P.Qq v) (G.P.m + 1), IsEdge p (G.P.n' v) G.P.r e →
      ∀ z ∈ Φ v e.src, ((p : G.Kf) ^ G.P.ArV v) • z ∈ Φ v e.tgt) :
    ∀ x ∈ G.funMod (G.P.sys Φ), G.yv v x ∈ G.funMod (G.P.sys Φ) := by
  classical
  intro x hx
  unfold GAtP.funMod at hx ⊢
  rw [Submodule.mem_pi] at hx ⊢
  intro i hi
  have h0 := hx i hi
  unfold Level.funMod at h0 ⊢
  rw [Submodule.mem_inf, Submodule.mem_inf] at h0 ⊢
  obtain ⟨⟨hA, hB⟩, hC⟩ := h0
  have hA' := fun L hL => (Submodule.mem_iInf _).1 ((Submodule.mem_iInf _).1 hA L) hL
  have hB' : ∀ L : G.P.Tup, ¬ G.P.IsLatTup L → x i L = 0 :=
    fun L hL => (Submodule.mem_iInf _).1 ((Submodule.mem_iInf _).1 hB L) hL
  have hne : ∀ (L : G.P.Tup) (L'' : Submodule (G.P.W v) (Fin (G.P.m + 1) → G.P.Qq v)) (u : Fin G.P.nV),
      u ≠ v → Function.update (β := fun v => Submodule (G.P.W v) (Fin (G.P.m + 1) → G.P.Qq v)) L v L'' u = L u :=
    fun L L'' u hu => Function.update_of_ne hu L'' L
  have hself : ∀ (L : G.P.Tup) (L'' : Submodule (G.P.W v) (Fin (G.P.m + 1) → G.P.Qq v)),
      Function.update (β := fun v => Submodule (G.P.W v) (Fin (G.P.m + 1) → G.P.Qq v)) L v L'' v = L'' :=
    fun L L'' => Function.update_self v L'' L
  refine ⟨⟨?_, ?_⟩, nt_equiv G v i _ hC⟩
  · refine (Submodule.mem_iInf _).2 fun L => (Submodule.mem_iInf _).2 fun hL => ?_
    change Level.yv (P := G.P) v (x i) L ∈ G.P.sys Φ L
    rw [ef_yv_eq, Finset.smul_sum]
    refine Submodule.sum_mem _ fun L'' hL'' => ?_
    have hE : IsEdge p (G.P.n' v) G.P.r ⟨L'', L v⟩ :=
      (Set.Finite.mem_toFinset (ef_places_finite G.P v (L v))).1 hL''
    have hlat : G.P.IsLatTup
        (Function.update (β := fun v => Submodule (G.P.W v) (Fin (G.P.m + 1) → G.P.Qq v)) L v L'') := by
      intro u
      by_cases huv : u = v
      · subst huv
        rw [hself]
        exact hE.1
      · rw [hne L L'' u huv]
        exact hL u
    have hval := hA' _ hlat
    refine nt_tenV_smulK G v _ _ _ ?_ ?_ _ hval
    · intro z hz
      rw [hself] at hz
      exact hloc ⟨L'', L v⟩ hE z hz
    · intro u hu
      rw [hne L L'' u hu]
  · refine (Submodule.mem_iInf _).2 fun L => (Submodule.mem_iInf _).2 fun hL => ?_
    change Level.yv (P := G.P) v (x i) L = 0
    rw [ef_yv_eq, Finset.sum_eq_zero, smul_zero]
    intro L'' hL''
    have hE : IsEdge p (G.P.n' v) G.P.r ⟨L'', L v⟩ :=
      (Set.Finite.mem_toFinset (ef_places_finite G.P v (L v))).1 hL''
    apply hB'
    intro hlat
    apply hL
    intro u
    by_cases huv : u = v
    · subst huv
      exact hE.2.1
    · have := hlat u
      rw [hne L L'' u huv] at this
      exact this

theorem operators_preserve_lattices (D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) (G : GAtP p) : Formalism.MainNotions.FormalGroupSystem.adm12 D G := by
  refine ⟨fun v => ?_, fun v => ?_⟩
  · exact yv_pres G v (fun u Lu => Hten G.O (G.P.lam0 u) fun s => extO G.O (G.P.ι u) (G.P.σ u) s.val Lu)
      (fun e he => steps_gen G v (fun lam Λ => H0 G.O G.P.m lam Λ)
        (fun lam hl g hg F hF => lb_pos G.O G.Kf (lb_inj G v) G.P.m G.P.r lam hl G.P.w (ch_w_ne_zero G v) g hg F hF)
        e he)
  · exact yv_pres G v (fun u Lu => HtenW G.O (G.P.lam0 u) fun s => extO G.O (G.P.ι u) (G.P.σ u) s.val Lu)
      (fun e he => steps_gen G v (fun lam Λ => Dl0 G.O G.P.m lam Λ)
        (fun lam hl g hg F hF => dl_pos G.O G.Kf (lb_inj G v) G.P.m G.P.r lam hl G.P.w (ch_w_ne_zero G v) g hg F hF)
        e he)



[GLG_integral_operator_08] dual-Weyl diagonal step and tensor/basis model
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/LatticeSystems.lean#L1598-L1775
Range SHA256: 47967541264334c80e4ee8e0d0c64a5cda98919ef428addcb9723281801048ad
def lb_D {K : Type*} [Field K] (k r : ℕ) (c : K) : Matrix (Fin k) (Fin k) K :=
  Matrix.diagonal fun a => if a.val < r then c⁻¹ else 1

theorem lb_P (O Kf : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (hinj : Function.Injective (algebraMap O Kf)) (m r : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) (hlam : Antitone lam)
    (w : O) (hw : algebraMap O Kf w ≠ 0) (g : Matrix (Fin (m + 1)) (Fin (m + 1)) Kf) (hg : IsUnit g.det)
    (F : MvPolynomial (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1)) Kf) (hF : F ∈ nablaLTw O Kf m lam g) :
    (algebraMap O Kf w ^ (∑ i : Fin (m + 1) with i.val < r, lam i)) • F ∈
      nablaLTw O Kf m lam (g * lb_D (m + 1) r (algebraMap O Kf w)) := by
  classical
  set c : Kf := algebraMap O Kf w with hc
  set ν : Fin (m + 1) → ℕ := fun t => (lam t - lam (Fin.last m)).toNat with hν
  set e : ℤ := lam (Fin.last m) with he
  obtain ⟨b, hb, hwt⟩ := lb_basis O (m + 1) (m + 1) ν
  set t : Fin (m + 1) → Kf := fun a => if a.val < r then c⁻¹ else 1 with ht
  have ht0 : ∀ a, t a ≠ 0 := by
    intro a
    simp only [ht]
    split_ifs
    · exact inv_ne_zero hw
    · exact one_ne_zero
  have hB := (nablaLBasis O Kf hinj (m + 1) (m + 1) ν b _ hb g hg).2
  have hD := (nablaLDiag O Kf hinj (m + 1) (m + 1) ν b _ hb g t hg ht0).2
  obtain ⟨F0, hF0, rfl⟩ := hF
  have hF0' : F0 ∈ nablaL O Kf (m + 1) (m + 1) ν g := hF0
  rw [← hB] at hF0'
  set A : ℤ := ∑ i : Fin (m + 1) with i.val < r, lam i with hA
  set T : MvPolynomial (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1)) Kf →ₗ[O] MvPolynomial (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1)) Kf :=
    (LinearMap.lsmul Kf _ (c ^ A * g.det ^ (-e))).restrictScalars O with hT
  have key : Submodule.span O (Set.range fun i => rowAct Kf g⁻¹ (map (algebraMap O Kf) (b i))) ≤
      (nablaLTw O Kf m lam (g * lb_D (m + 1) r c)).comap T := by
    rw [Submodule.span_le]
    rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩
    set Fi := rowAct Kf g⁻¹ (map (algebraMap O Kf) (b i)) with hFi
    set π' : ℕ := ∑ a : Fin (m + 1) with a.val < r, rowWt i.1 a with hπ
    set Nν : ℕ := ∑ t : Fin (m + 1) with t.val < r, ν t with hNν
    have hle : π' ≤ Nν := hwt i r
    refine ⟨w ^ (Nν - π') • ((∏ a, t a ^ rowWt i.1 a)⁻¹ • Fi), ?_, ?_⟩
    · apply Submodule.smul_mem
      show _ ∈ nablaL O Kf (m + 1) (m + 1) ν (g * Matrix.diagonal t)
      rw [← hD]
      exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨i, rfl⟩
    · show (g * lb_D (m + 1) r c).det ^ (-e) • (w ^ (Nν - π') • ((∏ a, t a ^ rowWt i.1 a)⁻¹ • Fi)) =
        (c ^ A * g.det ^ (-e)) • Fi
      rw [← algebraMap_smul Kf (w ^ (Nν - π')), smul_smul, smul_smul, map_pow, ← hc]
      congr 1
      set ρ : ℕ := (Finset.univ.filter fun a : Fin (m + 1) => a.val < r).card with hρ
      have h1 : (g * lb_D (m + 1) r c).det = g.det * (c⁻¹) ^ ρ := by
        rw [Matrix.det_mul, lb_D, Matrix.det_diagonal, Finset.prod_ite, Finset.prod_const,
          Finset.prod_const_one, mul_one]
      have h2 : ∏ a, t a ^ rowWt i.1 a = (c⁻¹) ^ π' := by
        rw [hπ, ← Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum, Finset.prod_filter]
        apply Finset.prod_congr rfl
        intro a _
        simp only [ht]
        split_ifs <;> simp
      have h3 : A = (Nν : ℤ) + ρ * e := by
        rw [hA, hNν, hρ]
        push_cast
        rw [Finset.card_eq_sum_ones]
        push_cast
        rw [Finset.sum_mul, ← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
        apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
        intro a _
        have : 0 ≤ lam a - lam (Fin.last m) := sub_nonneg.2 (hlam (Fin.le_last a))
        simp only [hν, Int.toNat_of_nonneg this, he]
        ring
      have h4 : c ^ Nν = c ^ (Nν - π') * c ^ π' := by
        rw [← pow_add, Nat.sub_add_cancel hle]
      have h5 : ((c⁻¹) ^ ρ) ^ (-e) = c ^ ((ρ : ℤ) * e) := by
        rw [inv_pow, inv_zpow, zpow_neg, inv_inv, ← zpow_natCast c ρ, ← zpow_mul]
      have h6 : ((c⁻¹) ^ π')⁻¹ = c ^ π' := by rw [inv_pow, inv_inv]
      rw [h1, h2, h3, mul_zpow, h5, h6, zpow_add₀ hw, zpow_natCast, h4]
      ring
  have h5 := key hF0'
  have h6 : T F0 = c ^ A • (LinearMap.restrictScalars O ((LinearMap.lsmul Kf _) (g.det ^ (-e)))) F0 := by
    simp only [hT, LinearMap.restrictScalars_apply, LinearMap.lsmul_apply, smul_smul]
  rw [← h6]
  exact h5

theorem lb_tensor (O Kf : Type) [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf] {S : Type} [Fintype S] {k n : ℕ}
    (M M' : S → Submodule O (MvPolynomial (Fin k × Fin n) Kf)) (c : S → Kf)
    (h : ∀ s, ∀ F ∈ M s, c s • F ∈ M' s) :
    ∀ P ∈ tensorLat O Kf M, (∏ s, c s) • P ∈ tensorLat O Kf M' := by
  have key : tensorLat O Kf M ≤ (tensorLat O Kf M').comap
      ((LinearMap.lsmul Kf (MvPolynomial (S × (Fin k × Fin n)) Kf) (∏ s, c s)).restrictScalars O) := by
    unfold tensorLat
    rw [Submodule.span_le]
    rintro _ ⟨F, hF, rfl⟩
    apply Submodule.subset_span
    refine ⟨fun s => c s • F s, fun s => h s _ (hF s), ?_⟩
    simp only [LinearMap.restrictScalars_apply, LinearMap.lsmul_apply, map_smul]
    rw [Finset.prod_smul]
  exact fun P hP => key hP

theorem lb_indep (O Kf : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (hinj : Function.Injective (algebraMap O Kf)) (m : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (g h : Matrix (Fin (m + 1)) (Fin (m + 1)) Kf) (hg : IsUnit g.det) (hh : IsUnit h.det)
    (hs : colSpan O g = colSpan O h) : nablaLTw O Kf m lam g ≤ nablaLTw O Kf m lam h := by
  classical
  have hcol : ∀ (g h : Matrix (Fin (m + 1)) (Fin (m + 1)) Kf), colSpan O g = colSpan O h →
      ∃ U : Matrix (Fin (m + 1)) (Fin (m + 1)) O, h = g * U.map (algebraMap O Kf) := by
    intro g h hs
    have h1 : ∀ j, ∃ c : Fin (m + 1) → O, ∑ i, c i • (fun a => g a i) = fun a => h a j := by
      intro j
      have hm : (fun a => h a j) ∈ colSpan O g := by
        rw [hs]; exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨j, rfl⟩
      exact (Submodule.mem_span_range_iff_exists_fun O).1 hm
    choose c hc using h1
    refine ⟨fun i j => c j i, ?_⟩
    ext a j
    have h2 := congrFun (hc j) a
    rw [Matrix.mul_apply, ← h2, Finset.sum_apply]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro i _
    show c j i • g a i = g a i * algebraMap O Kf (c j i)
    rw [Algebra.smul_def, mul_comm]
  obtain ⟨U, hU⟩ := hcol g h hs
  obtain ⟨U', hU'⟩ := hcol h g hs.symm
  have hdU : ∀ V : Matrix (Fin (m + 1)) (Fin (m + 1)) O,
      (V.map (algebraMap O Kf)).det = algebraMap O Kf V.det :=
    fun V => (RingHom.map_det (algebraMap O Kf) V).symm
  have hdet : U.det * U'.det = 1 := by
    apply hinj
    have h1 : g.det * (algebraMap O Kf U.det * algebraMap O Kf U'.det) = g.det * 1 := by
      conv_rhs => rw [mul_one, hU', hU, Matrix.det_mul, Matrix.det_mul, hdU, hdU]
      ring
    rw [map_mul, map_one]
    exact mul_left_cancel₀ hg.ne_zero h1
  let u : Oˣ := ⟨U.det, U'.det, hdet, by rw [mul_comm]; exact hdet⟩
  set e : ℤ := lam (Fin.last m) with he
  have hz : algebraMap O Kf ((u ^ e : Oˣ) : O) = (algebraMap O Kf U.det) ^ e := by
    calc algebraMap O Kf ((u ^ e : Oˣ) : O)
        = ((Units.map (algebraMap O Kf : O →* Kf) (u ^ e) : Kfˣ) : Kf) := rfl
      _ = (((Units.map (algebraMap O Kf : O →* Kf) u) ^ e : Kfˣ) : Kf) := by rw [map_zpow]
      _ = ((Units.map (algebraMap O Kf : O →* Kf) u : Kfˣ) : Kf) ^ e := Units.val_zpow_eq_zpow_val _ _
      _ = (algebraMap O Kf U.det) ^ e := rfl
  intro F hF
  rw [nablaLTwMem O Kf m lam h hh]
  rw [nablaLTwMem O Kf m lam g hg] at hF
  obtain ⟨f, hf, hfe⟩ := hF
  refine ⟨((u ^ e : Oˣ) : O) • rowAct O U f,
    Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (dualWeylRowStable O _ _ _ U f hf), ?_⟩
  have hmap : ∀ f : MvPolynomial (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1)) O,
      (mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId O Kf)).toLinearMap f = map (algebraMap O Kf) f := fun f => rfl
  rw [hmap] at hfe
  rw [map_smul, hmap, ldtwist_map_rowAct, hfe, map_smul, ← algebraMap_smul Kf ((u ^ e : Oˣ) : O), hz,
    smul_smul]
  have h4 : rowAct Kf (U.map (algebraMap O Kf)) (rowAct Kf g F) = rowAct Kf h F := by
    have h5 := rowActMul Kf (m + 1) (m + 1) (U.map (algebraMap O Kf)) g
    rw [hU, ← h5]
    rfl
  rw [h4, hU, Matrix.det_mul, hdU, mul_zpow, mul_comm]

theorem lb_D_det {K : Type*} [Field K] (k r : ℕ) (c : K) (hc : c ≠ 0) : IsUnit (lb_D k r c).det := by
  rw [lb_D, Matrix.det_diagonal, isUnit_iff_ne_zero, Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff]
  intro a _
  split_ifs
  · exact inv_ne_zero hc
  · exact one_ne_zero

theorem lb_pos (O Kf : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (hinj : Function.Injective (algebraMap O Kf)) (m r : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) (hlam : Antitone lam)
    (w : O) (hw : algebraMap O Kf w ≠ 0) (g : Matrix (Fin (m + 1)) (Fin (m + 1)) Kf) (hg : IsUnit g.det)
    (F : MvPolynomial (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1)) Kf) (hF : F ∈ H0 O m lam (colSpan O g)) :
    (algebraMap O Kf w ^ (∑ i : Fin (m + 1) with i.val < r, lam i)) • F ∈
      H0 O m lam (colSpan O (g * lb_D (m + 1) r (algebraMap O Kf w))) := by
  have hgD : IsUnit (g * lb_D (m + 1) r (algebraMap O Kf w)).det := by
    rw [Matrix.det_mul]; exact hg.mul (lb_D_det _ _ _ hw)
  unfold H0 at hF ⊢
  rw [if_pos hlam] at hF ⊢
  have h1 : F ∈ nablaLTw O Kf m lam g := (iInf₂_le g ⟨hg, rfl⟩ : _ ≤ nablaLTw O Kf m lam g) hF
  have h2 := lb_P O Kf hinj m r lam hlam w hw g hg F h1
  exact (le_iInf₂ fun h hh => lb_indep O Kf hinj m lam _ h hgD hh.1 hh.2 :
    nablaLTw O Kf m lam (g * lb_D (m + 1) r (algebraMap O Kf w)) ≤ _) h2

variable {p : ℕ}



[GLG_integral_operator_09] exact original edge and midpoint basis interfaces

theorem lb_inj (G : GAtP p) (v : Fin G.P.nV) : Function.Injective (algebraMap G.O G.Kf) :=
  haveI := (G.P.setting v).frac_O
  IsFractionRing.injective G.O G.Kf

theorem lb_steps (G : GAtP p) (v : Fin G.P.nV) (hE : RestEdgeLat G v)
    (e : Edge (G.P.W v) (G.P.Qq v) (G.P.m + 1)) (he : IsEdge p (G.P.n' v) G.P.r e) :
    (∀ P ∈ Hten G.O (G.P.lam0 v) (fun s => extO G.O (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s.val e.src),
      (algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w ^ Ar G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v)) • P ∈
        Hten G.O (G.P.lam0 v) (fun s => LeAt p (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) G.P.w s.val e)) ∧
    (∀ P ∈ Hten G.O (G.P.lam0 v) (fun s => LeAt p (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) G.P.w s.val e),
      (algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w ^ Ar G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v)) • P ∈
        Hten G.O (G.P.lam0 v) (fun s => extO G.O (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s.val e.tgt)) := by
  obtain ⟨gs, hgs, h1, h2, h3⟩ := hE e he
  have hw := ch_w_ne_zero G v
  have hA : ∏ s : Fin (G.P.n' v), algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w ^
      (∑ i : Fin (G.P.m + 1) with i.val < G.P.r, lamAt (G.P.lam0 v) s i) =
      algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w ^ Ar G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v) := lb_zpow_sum _ hw _ _
  have hgD : ∀ s, IsUnit (gs s * lb_D (G.P.m + 1) G.P.r (algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w)).det := by
    intro s
    rw [Matrix.det_mul]; exact (hgs s).mul (lb_D_det _ _ _ hw)
  constructor
  · intro P hP
    rw [← hA]
    refine lb_tensor G.O G.Kf _ _ _ (fun s F hF => ?_) P hP
    beta_reduce at hF ⊢
    rw [h1] at hF
    rw [h2]
    exact lb_pos G.O G.Kf (lb_inj G v) G.P.m G.P.r _ (kp_lamAt_antitone _ (G.P.dom v) s) G.P.w hw _ (hgs _) F hF
  · intro P hP
    rw [← hA]
    refine lb_tensor G.O G.Kf _ _ _ (fun s F hF => ?_) P hP
    beta_reduce at hF ⊢
    rw [h2] at hF
    rw [h3]
    exact lb_pos G.O G.Kf (lb_inj G v) G.P.m G.P.r _ (kp_lamAt_antitone _ (G.P.dom v) s) G.P.w hw _ (hgD _) F hF

theorem lb_H_le_Re (G : GAtP p) (v : Fin G.P.nV) (hE : RestEdgeLat G v)
    (e : Edge (G.P.W v) (G.P.Qq v) (G.P.m + 1)) (he : IsEdge p (G.P.n' v) G.P.r e) :
    HL (O := G.O) (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) (G.P.lam0 v) e.src ≤
      Re p (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) G.P.w G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v) e := by
  intro x hx
  have hw := ch_w_ne_zero G v
  refine ⟨(algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w ^ Ar G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v)) • x, (lb_steps G v hE e he).1 _ hx, ?_⟩
  show (algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w ^ (-Ar G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v))) •
    (algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w ^ Ar G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v)) • x = x
  rw [smul_smul, ← zpow_add₀ hw, neg_add_cancel, zpow_zero, one_smul]

theorem lb_Hdual_le_Re' (G : GAtP p) (v : Fin G.P.nV) (hE : RestEdgeLat G v)
    (e : Edge (G.P.W v) (G.P.Qq v) (G.P.m + 1)) (he : IsEdge p (G.P.n' v) G.P.r e) :
    dualLat G.O G.Kf (HL (O := G.O) (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) (G.P.lam0 v) e.tgt) ≤
      Re' p (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) G.P.w G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v) e := by
  intro φ hφ
  have hw := ch_w_ne_zero G v
  refine ⟨(algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w ^ Ar G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v)) • φ, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro z hz
    have h1 := hφ ((algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w ^ Ar G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v)) • z) ((lb_steps G v hE e he).2 _ hz)
    rw [map_smul] at h1
    exact h1
  · show (algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w ^ (-Ar G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v))) •
      (algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w ^ Ar G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v)) • φ = φ
    rw [smul_smul, ← zpow_add₀ hw, neg_add_cancel, zpow_zero, one_smul]

theorem lb_col {O Kf : Type} [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf] {k : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin k) (Fin k) Kf)
    (d : Fin k → Kf) (o : Fin k → O) (a : O) (h : ∀ j, algebraMap O Kf a * d j = algebraMap O Kf (o j)) :
    ∀ x ∈ colSpan O (M * Matrix.diagonal d), a • x ∈ colSpan O M := by
  have key : colSpan O (M * Matrix.diagonal d) ≤
      (colSpan O M).comap (a • (LinearMap.id : (Fin k → Kf) →ₗ[O] (Fin k → Kf))) := by
    unfold colSpan
    rw [Submodule.span_le]
    rintro _ ⟨j, rfl⟩
    have h1 : a • (fun i => (M * Matrix.diagonal d) i j) = o j • (fun i => M i j) := by
      funext i
      simp only [Pi.smul_apply, Matrix.mul_diagonal, Algebra.smul_def]
      rw [← h j]; ring
    show a • (fun i => (M * Matrix.diagonal d) i j) ∈
      Submodule.span O (Set.range fun c : Fin k => fun i => M i c)
    rw [h1]
    exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (Submodule.subset_span ⟨j, rfl⟩)
  exact fun x hx => key hx

theorem lb_tw_apply {Qq Kf : Type} [Field Qq] [Field Kf] (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) (s : ℕ) {k : ℕ}
    (g : Matrix (Fin k) (Fin k) Qq) (i j : Fin k) : la_tw ι σ s g i j = ι ((σ ^ s) (g i j)) := rfl

theorem lb_tw_p (G : GAtP p) (v : Fin G.P.nV) (s : ℕ) (x : Fin (G.P.m + 1) → G.P.Qq v) (i : Fin (G.P.m + 1)) :
    twistVec (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s ((p : G.P.W v) • x) i = (p : G.Kf) * G.P.ι v ((G.P.σ v ^ s) (x i)) := by
  show G.P.ι v ((G.P.σ v ^ s) ((p : G.P.W v) • x i)) = _
  rw [Algebra.smul_def, map_natCast, map_mul, map_mul, map_natCast, map_natCast]

def RestEdgeBasis (G : GAtP p) (v : Fin G.P.nV) : Prop :=
  ∀ e : Edge (G.P.W v) (G.P.Qq v) (G.P.m + 1), IsEdge p (G.P.n' v) G.P.r e →
    ∃ g : Matrix (Fin (G.P.m + 1)) (Fin (G.P.m + 1)) (G.P.Qq v), IsEdgeBasis p G.P.r e g

theorem lb_edgeLat_of_basis (G : GAtP p) (v : Fin G.P.nV) (hB : RestEdgeBasis G v) : RestEdgeLat G v := by
  intro e he
  obtain ⟨g, hg, hsrc, htgt⟩ := hB e he
  unfold lb_D
  set c : G.Kf := algebraMap G.O G.Kf G.P.w with hc
  have hw : c ≠ 0 := ch_w_ne_zero G v
  have hpK : (p : G.Kf) = c ^ 2 := nt_p_eq G v
  have hpK0 : (p : G.Kf) ≠ 0 := by rw [hpK]; exact pow_ne_zero _ hw
  have hpQ0 : (p : G.P.Qq v) ≠ 0 := by
    intro h0
    apply hpK0
    have := congrArg (G.P.ι v) h0
    simpa using this
  set dK : Fin (G.P.m + 1) → G.Kf := fun a => if a.val < G.P.r then c⁻¹ else 1 with hdK
  set dQ : Fin (G.P.m + 1) → G.P.Qq v := fun a => if a.val < G.P.r then ((p : G.P.Qq v))⁻¹ else 1 with hdQ
  have hDD : ∀ s, la_tw (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s (g * Matrix.diagonal dQ) =
      la_tw (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s g * Matrix.diagonal dK * Matrix.diagonal dK := by
    intro s
    ext i j
    rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal, Matrix.mul_diagonal, lb_tw_apply, lb_tw_apply,
      Matrix.mul_diagonal, map_mul, map_mul]
    congr 1
    simp only [hdQ, hdK]
    split_ifs
    · rw [map_inv₀, map_inv₀, map_natCast, map_natCast, hpK, pow_two, mul_inv]
    · simp
  have e1 : ∀ s, extO G.O (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s e.src = colSpan G.O (la_tw (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s g) :=
    fun s => by rw [hsrc]; exact la_extO_G G v s g
  have e3 : ∀ s, extO G.O (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s e.tgt =
      colSpan G.O (la_tw (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s g * Matrix.diagonal dK * Matrix.diagonal dK) :=
    fun s => by rw [htgt, la_extO_G, hDD]
  refine ⟨fun s => la_tw (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s g, fun s => la_tw_det _ _ s g hg, e1, ?_, e3⟩
  intro s
  set gK := la_tw (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s g with hgK
  apply le_antisymm
  · unfold LeAt
    refine sup_le ?_ ?_
    · rw [e1]
      intro x hx
      have h1 : gK = gK * Matrix.diagonal dK *
          Matrix.diagonal (fun a : Fin (G.P.m + 1) => if a.val < G.P.r then c else 1) := by
        rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal]
        have h2 : (fun a : Fin (G.P.m + 1) => dK a * (if a.val < G.P.r then c else 1)) = fun _ => 1 := by
          funext a
          simp only [hdK]
          split_ifs
          · exact inv_mul_cancel₀ hw
          · simp
        rw [h2, Matrix.diagonal_one, Matrix.mul_one]
      have hx' : x ∈ colSpan G.O gK := hx
      rw [h1] at hx'
      have h3 := lb_col (gK * Matrix.diagonal dK) _
        (fun a : Fin (G.P.m + 1) => if a.val < G.P.r then G.P.w else 1) 1
        (by intro j; split_ifs <;> simp [hc]) x hx'
      rw [one_smul] at h3
      exact h3
    · rw [Submodule.span_le]
      rintro _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
      have h1 : c⁻¹ • twistVec (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s ((p : G.P.W v) • x) =
          G.P.w • twistVec (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s x := by
        funext i
        rw [Pi.smul_apply, Pi.smul_apply, lb_tw_p, smul_eq_mul, Algebra.smul_def, ← hc, hpK]
        show c⁻¹ * (c ^ 2 * _) = c * (G.P.ι v) ((G.P.σ v ^ s) (x i))
        field_simp
      have h2 : twistVec (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s x ∈
          colSpan G.O (gK * Matrix.diagonal dK * Matrix.diagonal dK) := by
        rw [← e3]; exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
      show c⁻¹ • twistVec (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s ((p : G.P.W v) • x) ∈ _
      rw [h1]
      exact lb_col (gK * Matrix.diagonal dK) dK
        (fun a : Fin (G.P.m + 1) => if a.val < G.P.r then 1 else G.P.w) G.P.w
        (by
          intro j
          simp only [hdK]
          split_ifs
          · rw [← hc, mul_inv_cancel₀ hw, map_one]
          · rw [mul_one]) _ h2
  · unfold colSpan
    rw [Submodule.span_le]
    rintro _ ⟨j, rfl⟩
    unfold LeAt
    by_cases hj : j.val < G.P.r
    · apply Submodule.mem_sup_right


[GLG_rational_descent_01] general separating-evaluation retraction and rational linear operator descent
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/GroupLattices/ComparisonMaps/YvRatOfValRat.lean#L13-L119
Range SHA256: 425e2ef0a3631f6c2ebc5e366c86fd5da9a5076a180835bc6854894c04f7a1fa

variable {LL Kf : Type} [Field LL] [Field Kf] (e : LL →+* Kf)

theorem exists_fun_ne {c : Kf} (hc : c ≠ 0) : ∃ π : Kf →+ LL, (∀ a x, π (e a * x) = a * π x) ∧ π c ≠ 0 := by
  letI : Module LL Kf := e.toModule
  have hne : ¬ ∀ φ : Module.Dual LL Kf, φ c = 0 := fun h => hc ((Module.forall_dual_apply_eq_zero_iff LL c).1 h)
  push_neg at hne
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ⟩ := hne
  exact ⟨φ.toAddMonoidHom, fun a x => φ.map_smul a x, hφ⟩

theorem exists_retr : ∃ π : Kf →+ LL, (∀ a x, π (e a * x) = a * π x) ∧ ∀ a, π (e a) = a := by
  obtain ⟨π, hπ, h1⟩ := exists_fun_ne e (one_ne_zero : (1 : Kf) ≠ 0)
  refine ⟨(AddMonoidHom.mulLeft (π 1)⁻¹).comp π, fun a x => ?_, fun a => ?_⟩
  · show (π 1)⁻¹ * π (e a * x) = a * ((π 1)⁻¹ * π x)
    rw [hπ]
    ring
  · show (π 1)⁻¹ * π (e a) = a
    have h2 := hπ a 1
    rw [mul_one] at h2
    rw [h2, mul_left_comm, inv_mul_cancel₀ h1, mul_one]

variable {V W T : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module LL V] [AddCommGroup W] [Module Kf W]
  (ev : T → W →ₗ[Kf] Kf) (cmp : V →+ W)

def IsRat (F : W) : Prop := ∀ t, ev t F ∈ Set.range e

theorem ev_pi (hsm : ∀ (a : LL) x, cmp (a • x) = e a • cmp x) (hval : ∀ x, IsRat e ev (cmp x))
    (π : Kf →+ LL) (hπ : ∀ a x, π (e a * x) = a * π x) (c : V →₀ Kf) (t : T) :
    ev t (cmp (c.sum fun x a => π a • x)) = e (π (ev t (c.sum fun x a => a • cmp x))) := by
  simp only [Finsupp.sum, map_sum, hsm, map_smul, smul_eq_mul]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun x _ => ?_
  obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := hval x t
  rw [← hr, mul_comm (c x), hπ, map_mul, mul_comm]

theorem rat_mem (hsep : ∀ F : W, (∀ t, ev t F = 0) → F = 0)
    (hsm : ∀ (a : LL) x, cmp (a • x) = e a • cmp x) (hval : ∀ x, IsRat e ev (cmp x))
    {F : W} (hF : F ∈ Submodule.span Kf (Set.range cmp)) (hr : IsRat e ev F) : ∃ x, cmp x = F := by
  obtain ⟨c, rfl⟩ := Finsupp.mem_span_range_iff_exists_finsupp.1 hF
  obtain ⟨π, hπ, hπe⟩ := exists_retr e
  refine ⟨c.sum fun x a => π a • x, sub_eq_zero.1 (hsep _ fun t => ?_)⟩
  rw [map_sub, ev_pi e ev cmp hsm hval π hπ c t, sub_eq_zero]
  obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := hr t
  rw [← hr, hπe]

theorem li_of_rat (hsep : ∀ F : W, (∀ t, ev t F = 0) → F = 0)
    (hsm : ∀ (a : LL) x, cmp (a • x) = e a • cmp x) (hval : ∀ x, IsRat e ev (cmp x))
    (hinj : Function.Injective cmp) {ι : Type} (b : ι → V) (hb : LinearIndependent LL b) :
    LinearIndependent Kf fun a => cmp (b a) := by
  rw [linearIndependent_iff'] at hb ⊢
  intro s g hg i hi
  by_contra hgi
  obtain ⟨π, hπ, hπi⟩ := exists_fun_ne e hgi
  refine hπi (hb s (fun j => π (g j)) ?_ i hi)
  apply hinj
  rw [map_zero]
  apply hsep
  intro t
  have key : ∀ j, e (π (g j)) * ev t (cmp (b j)) = e (π (g j * ev t (cmp (b j)))) := by
    intro j
    obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := hval (b j) t
    rw [← hr, mul_comm (g j), hπ, map_mul, mul_comm]
  have h0 := congrArg (fun F => e (π (ev t F))) hg
  simp only [map_sum, map_smul, smul_eq_mul, map_zero] at h0
  simp only [map_sum, hsm, map_smul, smul_eq_mul, key]
  exact h0

def descOp (hsm : ∀ (a : LL) x, cmp (a • x) = e a • cmp x) (hinj : Function.Injective cmp) (Tp : W →ₗ[Kf] W)
    (h : ∀ x, ∃ y, cmp y = Tp (cmp x)) : V →ₗ[LL] V where
  toFun x := Classical.choose (h x)
  map_add' x y := by
    apply hinj
    have h1 := Classical.choose_spec (h (x + y))
    have h2 := Classical.choose_spec (h x)
    have h3 := Classical.choose_spec (h y)
    rw [map_add cmp (Classical.choose (h x)), h1, h2, h3, map_add, map_add]
  map_smul' a x := by
    apply hinj
    have h1 := Classical.choose_spec (h (a • x))
    have h2 := Classical.choose_spec (h x)
    rw [RingHom.id_apply, hsm a (Classical.choose (h x)), h1, h2, hsm, map_smul]

theorem descOp_spec (hsm : ∀ (a : LL) x, cmp (a • x) = e a • cmp x) (hinj : Function.Injective cmp) (Tp : W →ₗ[Kf] W)
    (h : ∀ x, ∃ y, cmp y = Tp (cmp x)) (x : V) : cmp (descOp e cmp hsm hinj Tp h x) = Tp (cmp x) := Classical.choose_spec (h x)

end Gen

section Pl

variable {p : ℕ} {O Kf : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
  (P : Places p O Kf) (α : Type) {LL : Type} [Field LL] (e : LL →+* Kf)

abbrev FnP : Type := α → P.Tup → P.Xsp

def evP (t : α × P.Tup × ((Σ v : Fin P.nV, Fin (P.n' v) × (Fin (P.m + 1) × Fin (P.m + 1))) →₀ ℕ)) :
    FnP P α →ₗ[Kf] Kf :=
  (MvPolynomial.lcoeff Kf t.2.2) ∘ₗ (LinearMap.proj t.2.1) ∘ₗ (LinearMap.proj t.1)

theorem localComponent_apply₂ (i : α) (L : P.Tup) (m) (F : FnP P α) : evP P α (i, L, m) F = coeff m (F i L) := rfl

theorem eq_zero_of_forall_evalCoeff_eq_zero (F : FnP P α) (h : ∀ t, evP P α t F = 0) : F = 0 := by
  funext i L
  refine MvPolynomial.ext _ _ fun m => ?_
  have h1 := h (i, L, m)
  rw [localComponent_apply₂] at h1
  simpa using h1

def RatFnP (F : FnP P α) : Prop := ∀ i L, ∃ Q : MvPolynomial _ LL, MvPolynomial.map e Q = F i L

section Loc

variable {p : ℕ} (G : GAtP p) {LL : Type} [Field LL] (e : LL →+* G.Kf)

abbrev evG := evP G.P G.comp

theorem evG_apply (i : G.comp) (L : G.P.Tup) (m) (F : G.Fn) : evG G (i, L, m) F = coeff m (F i L) := rfl

theorem evG_sep (F : G.Fn) (h : ∀ t, evG G t F = 0) : F = 0 := eq_zero_of_forall_evalCoeff_eq_zero G.P G.comp F h

abbrev RatFn (F : G.Fn) : Prop := RatFnP G.P G.comp e F

theorem ratFn_iff (F : G.Fn) : RatFn G e F ↔ IsRat e (evG G) F := isRational_iff G.P G.comp e F

def yK (v : Fin G.P.nV) : G.Fn →ₗ[G.Kf] G.Fn where
  toFun := G.yv v
  map_add' F F' := (LatticeSystems.yEnd G v).map_add F F'
  map_smul' c F := by
    funext i L
    show Level.yv (P := G.P) v ((c • F) i) L = c • Level.yv (P := G.P) v (F i) L
    rw [LatticeSystems.ef_yv_eq, LatticeSystems.ef_yv_eq, smul_comm c]
    congr 1
    rw [Finset.smul_sum]
    rfl

@[simp] theorem yK_apply (v : Fin G.P.nV) (F : G.Fn) : yK G v F = G.yv v F := rfl

theorem yv_rat (v : Fin G.P.nV) (F : G.Fn) (h : IsRat e (evG G) F) : IsRat e (evG G) (G.yv v F) := by
  rintro ⟨i, L, m⟩
  have hF : ∀ L', coeff m (F i L') ∈ e.range := fun L' => by
    obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := h (i, L', m)
    exact RingHom.mem_range.2 ⟨r, hr.trans (evG_apply G i L' m F)⟩
  have hp : ((p : G.Kf) ^ G.P.ArV v) ∈ e.range := RingHom.mem_range.2 ⟨(p : LL) ^ G.P.ArV v, by simp⟩
  show coeff m (Level.yv (P := G.P) v (F i) L) ∈ Set.range e
  rw [LatticeSystems.ef_yv_eq, MvPolynomial.coeff_smul, MvPolynomial.coeff_sum, smul_eq_mul]
  exact RingHom.mem_range.1 (Subring.mul_mem _ hp (Subring.sum_mem _ fun L'' _ => hF _))

theorem span_M_zero (F : G.Fn) (hF : F ∈ Submodule.span G.Kf (G.M : Set G.Fn)) (i : G.comp) (L : G.P.Tup)
    (hL : ¬ G.P.IsLatTup L) : F i L = 0 := by
  have key : ∀ F' ∈ (G.M : Set G.Fn), F' i L = 0 := by
    intro F' hF'
    have h0 : F' ∈ Submodule.pi Set.univ (fun j => (G.lev j).funMod G.P.HtupW) := hF'
    have h1 := (Submodule.mem_pi.1 h0) i (Set.mem_univ i)
    have h2 := (Submodule.mem_inf.1 (Submodule.mem_inf.1 h1).1).2
    have h3 := (Submodule.mem_iInf _).1 h2 L
    have h4 := (Submodule.mem_iInf _).1 h3 hL
    exact LinearMap.mem_ker.1 h4
  induction hF using Submodule.span_induction with
  | mem x hx => exact key x hx
  | zero => rfl
  | add x y _ _ hx hy => simp [hx, hy]
  | smul a x _ hx => simp [hx]

end Loc

section Rec

variable {D : D7} (c : LatCmp D)

def ValRat : Prop :=
  ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) (x : ((D.fm u).lat 0).V)
    (i : (D.gat u w hg).comp) (L : (D.gat u w hg).P.Tup), (D.gat u w hg).P.IsLatTup L →
    ∃ Q : MvPolynomial _ D.what₂.LL, MvPolynomial.map (c.embW u w hg) Q = c.cmp u w hg x i L

theorem cmp_inj u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) :
    Function.Injective (c.cmp u w hg) := by
  refine (injective_iff_map_eq_zero (c.cmp u w hg)).2 fun x hx => ?_
  have hli := c.cmp_li u w hg
  rw [linearIndependent_iff'] at hli
  have hsum : ∑ a, (fun a => c.embW u w hg (((D.fm u).lat 0).b.repr x a)) a •
      c.cmp u w hg (((D.fm u).lat 0).b a) = 0 := by
    have h1 := congrArg (c.cmp u w hg) (((D.fm u).lat 0).b.sum_repr x)
    rw [map_sum, hx] at h1
    simpa only [c.cmp_smul] using h1
  have hz : ∀ a, ((D.fm u).lat 0).b.repr x a = 0 := fun a =>
    (c.embW u w hg).injective ((hli Finset.univ _ hsum a (Finset.mem_univ a)).trans (map_zero _).symm)
  exact ((D.fm u).lat 0).b.ext_elem fun a => by simp [hz a]

theorem cmp_mem_span u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w))
    (x : ((D.fm u).lat 0).V) :
    c.cmp u w hg x ∈ Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).Kf ((D.gat u w hg).M : Set (D.gat u w hg).Fn) := by
  have hx : x ∈ Submodule.span D.what₂.LL (((D.fm u).lat 0).L : Set ((D.fm u).lat 0).V) := by
    rw [((D.fm u).lat_good 0).2.2]; trivial
  induction hx using Submodule.span_induction with
  | mem y hy =>
    apply Submodule.subset_span
    rw [← c.cmp_M u w hg]
    exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩
  | zero => rw [map_zero]; exact Submodule.zero_mem _
  | add y z _ _ h1 h2 => rw [map_add]; exact Submodule.add_mem _ h1 h2
  | smul a y _ h1 => rw [c.cmp_smul]; exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ h1

theorem ValRat.all (h : ValRat c) u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w))
    (x : ((D.fm u).lat 0).V) : RatFn (D.gat u w hg) (c.embW u w hg) (c.cmp u w hg x) := by
  intro i L
  by_cases hL : (D.gat u w hg).P.IsLatTup L
  · exact h u w hg x i L hL
  · refine ⟨0, ?_⟩
    rw [map_zero, span_M_zero (D.gat u w hg) _ (cmp_mem_span c u w hg x) i L hL]

theorem span_M_le u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) :
    Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).Kf ((D.gat u w hg).M : Set (D.gat u w hg).Fn) ≤
      Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).Kf (Set.range (c.cmp u w hg)) := by
  rw [Submodule.span_le]
  intro F hF
  rw [← c.cmp_M u w hg] at hF
  exact Submodule.span_le_restrictScalars (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf _
    (Submodule.span_mono (Set.image_subset_range _ _) hF)

theorem range_cmp (h : ValRat c) u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w))
    (F : (D.gat u w hg).Fn) :
    (∃ x, c.cmp u w hg x = F) ↔
      F ∈ Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).Kf ((D.gat u w hg).M : Set (D.gat u w hg).Fn) ∧
        RatFn (D.gat u w hg) (c.embW u w hg) F := by
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨x, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨cmp_mem_span c u w hg x, ValRat.all c h u w hg x⟩
  · rintro ⟨hF, hr⟩
    exact rat_mem (c.embW u w hg) (evG (D.gat u w hg)) (c.cmp u w hg) (evG_sep _) (c.cmp_smul u w hg)
      (fun x => (ratFn_iff _ _ _).1 (ValRat.all c h u w hg x)) (span_M_le c u w hg hF) ((ratFn_iff _ _ _).1 hr)

theorem yv_span {p : ℕ} (G : GAtP p) (v : Fin G.P.nV) (F : G.Fn)
    (hF : F ∈ Submodule.span G.Kf (G.M : Set G.Fn)) (D' : D7) : G.yv v F ∈ Submodule.span G.Kf (G.M : Set G.Fn) := by
  induction hF using Submodule.span_induction with
  | mem y hy => exact Submodule.subset_span ((Model.operators_preserve_lattices D' G).2 v y hy)
  | zero => rw [← yK_apply, map_zero]; exact Submodule.zero_mem _
  | add y z _ _ h1 h2 => rw [← yK_apply, map_add]; exact Submodule.add_mem _ h1 h2
  | smul a y _ h1 => rw [← yK_apply, map_smul]; exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ h1

theorem yvRat_of_valRat (h : ValRat c) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) (v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV) :
    ∃ yL : ((D.fm u).lat 0).V →ₗ[D.what₂.LL] ((D.fm u).lat 0).V,
      ∀ x, (D.gat u w hg).yv v (c.cmp u w hg x) = c.cmp u w hg (yL x) := by
  have hex : ∀ x, ∃ y, c.cmp u w hg y = yK (D.gat u w hg) v (c.cmp u w hg x) := by
    intro x
    rw [yK_apply]
    refine (range_cmp c h u w hg _).2 ⟨yv_span (D.gat u w hg) v _ (cmp_mem_span c u w hg x) D, ?_⟩
    exact (ratFn_iff _ _ _).2 (yv_rat _ _ v _ ((ratFn_iff _ _ _).1 (ValRat.all c h u w hg x)))
  exact ⟨descOp (c.embW u w hg) (c.cmp u w hg) (c.cmp_smul u w hg) (cmp_inj c u w hg) (yK (D.gat u w hg) v) hex,
    fun x => by rw [descOp_spec, yK_apply]⟩

end Rec

end Formalism.L2.G.Cmp

def ValRatF {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  Ev Fm fun x i => ∀ hx : Fm.x1 < x, ValRat (Fm.ex x hx i).cmp

theorem npDescF_of_valRatF {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (h : ValRatF Fm) : NPDescF Fm := by
  obtain ⟨x0, hx0⟩ := h
  refine ⟨x0, fun x hx i hx1 => ?_⟩
  refine npDesc_of_valRat _ (Fm.gatT x i) (Fm.Gx x i) (Fm.Gy x i) Fm.GK ?_ (hx0 x hx i hx1)
  exact (Fm.ex x hx1 i).lat.vram

theorem CmpTied.valRat {D : D7} {X : Formalism.L2.Herm.Herm0 D} {T : ∀ u : D.what₂.Word, Forms0 D u} {Gt : GatTies D}
    {c : LatCmp D} (K : CmpTied D X T Gt c) : ValRat c := by
  intro u w hg x i L hL
  exact ⟨_, ((K.tie u w hg).val_cmp x i L hL).symm⟩



[GLG_rational_descent_05] entire GroupCompatibilityAll and its eventual family predicate
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/GroupLattices/StdSubAtOfRing.lean#L76-L89

structure GroupCompatibilityAll (D : D7) (Gt : GatTies D) (c : LatCmp D) where

  herm : Formalism.L2.Herm.Herm0 D

  forms : ∀ u : D.what₂.Word, Forms0 D u

  cmp : Cmp.CmpTied D herm forms Gt c

  ring : ∀ u w hg v, RingCompatibleAt D u w hg v

  gat : ∀ u w hg, GatIs D herm.lev0 u w hg

def GroupCompatibilityFamily {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  Ev Fm fun x i => ∀ hx : Fm.x1 < x, Nonempty (GroupCompatibilityAll (Fm.D x i) (Fm.gatT x i) (Fm.ex x hx i).cmp)


[GLG_rational_descent_06] ordered rational products and literal NPDesc_of
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/JointsLit.lean#L178-L346
