def YvRat (c : LatCmp D) : Prop :=
  ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w))
    (v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV),
    ∃ yL : ((D.fm u).lat 0).V →ₗ[D.what₂.LL] ((D.fm u).lat 0).V,
      ∀ x, (D.gat u w hg).yv v (c.cmp u w hg x) = c.cmp u w hg (yL x)

theorem iter_rat {α β : Type*} (f : α → α) (g : β → β) (φ : β → α) (h : ∀ x, f (φ x) = φ (g x)) (k : ℕ) (x : β) :
    f^[k] (φ x) = φ (g^[k] x) := by
  induction k generalizing x with
  | zero => rfl
  | succ k ih => simp only [Function.iterate_succ_apply', ih, h]

theorem foldr_rat {α β ι : Type*} (F : ι → α → α) (G : ι → β → β) (φ : β → α) (h : ∀ i x, F i (φ x) = φ (G i x))
    (l : List ι) (x : β) :
    (l.map F).foldr (fun f g => f ∘ g) id (φ x) = φ ((l.map G).foldr (fun f g => f ∘ g) id x) := by
  induction l with
  | nil => rfl
  | cons i l ih => simp only [List.map_cons, List.foldr_cons, Function.comp_apply, ih, h]

theorem span_image_spanA (c : LatCmp D) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w))
    [IsFractionRing (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf] (T : Set ((D.fm u).lat 0).V) :
    Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).O (c.cmp u w hg '' (Submodule.span (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) T : Set ((D.fm u).lat 0).V)) =
      Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).O (c.cmp u w hg '' T) := by
  refine le_antisymm ?_ (Submodule.span_mono (Set.image_mono Submodule.subset_span))
  rw [Submodule.span_le]
  rintro _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
  induction hx using Submodule.span_induction with
  | mem x hx => exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
  | zero => rw [map_zero]; exact Submodule.zero_mem _
  | add x y _ _ hx hy => rw [map_add]; exact Submodule.add_mem _ hx hy
  | smul a x _ hx =>
    have e : a • x = (algebraMap (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) D.what₂.LL a) • x := (algebraMap_smul D.what₂.LL a x).symm
    rw [e, c.cmp_smul]
    have e2 : c.embW u w hg (algebraMap (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) D.what₂.LL a) =
        algebraMap (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf
          (FBC.toO (D.plEq w) (c.embW u w hg) (c.embW_val u w hg) a) :=
      (FBC.toO_spec (D.plEq w) (c.embW u w hg) (c.embW_val u w hg) a).symm
    rw [e2, algebraMap_smul]
    exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ hx

def yProdL {p : ℕ} (G : GAtP p) (vs : List (Fin G.P.nV)) (m : ℕ) : Module.End G.O G.Fn :=
  (vs.map (fun v => yEnd G v ^ m)).prod

theorem yProdL_apply {p : ℕ} (G : GAtP p) (vs : List (Fin G.P.nV)) (m : ℕ) (F : G.Fn) :
    yProdL G vs m F = (vs.map (fun v => (G.yv v)^[m])).foldr (fun f g => f ∘ g) id F := by
  have hp : ∀ (v : Fin G.P.nV) (k : ℕ) (F : G.Fn), (yEnd G v ^ k) F = (G.yv v)^[k] F := by
    intro v k
    induction k with
    | zero => intro F; rfl
    | succ k ih =>
      intro F
      rw [pow_succ', Function.iterate_succ_apply', ← ih]
      rfl
  unfold yProdL
  induction vs with
  | nil => rfl
  | cons v vs ih =>
    simp only [List.map_cons, List.prod_cons, List.foldr_cons, Function.comp_apply, ← ih, ← hp]
    all_goals rfl

theorem iter_smul {R M : Type*} [Semiring R] [AddCommMonoid M] [Module R M] (f : M →ₗ[R] M) (k : ℕ) (a : R) (x : M) :
    (⇑f)^[k] (a • x) = a • (⇑f)^[k] x := by
  induction k generalizing x with
  | zero => rfl
  | succ k ih => simp only [Function.iterate_succ_apply', ih, map_smul]

theorem foldr_smul {R M ι : Type*} [Semiring R] [AddCommMonoid M] [Module R M] (f : ι → M →ₗ[R] M) (k : ℕ)
    (l : List ι) (a : R) (x : M) :
    (l.map (fun i => (⇑(f i))^[k])).foldr (fun g h => g ∘ h) id (a • x) =
      a • (l.map (fun i => (⇑(f i))^[k])).foldr (fun g h => g ∘ h) id x := by
  induction l with
  | nil => rfl
  | cons i l ih => simp only [List.map_cons, List.foldr_cons, Function.comp_apply, ih, iter_smul]

set_option maxHeartbeats 1000000 in
open Classical in

theorem NPDesc_of (c : LatCmp D) {L : SizeData₂ D} (hT : GatTies D) (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts)
    (hv : c.VramDef Y) (hy : YvRat c) : c.NPDesc X Y K := by
  intro u w hg _
  haveI := LatCmp.frac_of_ties hT u w hg
  choose yL hyL using hy u w hg
  have hpow : ∀ (k : ℕ) (x : ((D.fm u).lat 0).V),
      c.cmp u w hg (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : D.what₂.LL) ^ k) • x) =
        (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ k)) • c.cmp u w hg x := by
    intro k x
    rw [c.cmp_smul, ← algebraMap_smul (D.gat u w hg).Kf ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ k)]
    congr 1
    simp
  let yLP : Finset (HH D) → ((D.fm u).lat 0).V → ((D.fm u).lat 0).V := fun VV' =>
    (VV'.toList.map (fun t0 => (⇑(yL (Y.vOf u w hg t0)))^[D.R.m])).foldr (fun f g => f ∘ g) id
  have hrat : ∀ VV' x, yProd Y u w hg VV' (c.cmp u w hg x) = c.cmp u w hg (yLP VV' x) := by
    intro VV' x
    unfold yProd
    exact foldr_rat _ _ (c.cmp u w hg) (fun t0 x => iter_rat _ _ _ (hyL (Y.vOf u w hg t0)) D.R.m x) VV'.toList x
  have hyLsmul : ∀ VV' (a : D.what₂.LL) x, yLP VV' (a • x) = a • yLP VV' x := fun VV' a x =>
    foldr_smul (fun t0 => yL (Y.vOf u w hg t0)) D.R.m VV'.toList a x
  have hyP : ∀ VV' F, yProd Y u w hg VV' F = yProdL (D.gat u w hg) (VV'.toList.map (Y.vOf u w hg)) D.R.m F := by
    intro VV' F
    rw [yProdL_apply, List.map_map]
    rfl
  have hM : (D.gat u w hg).M =
      Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).O (c.cmp u w hg '' (((D.fm u).lat 0).L : Set ((D.fm u).lat 0).V)) :=
    (c.cmp_M u w hg).symm
  let s : Finset (HH D) → ℕ := fun VV' =>
    ∑ t0 ∈ VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) \ VV', ⌈Sv X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0⌉.toNat
  let τ : ℕ := ⌈tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w)⌉.toNat
  let T1 : Set ((D.fm u).lat 0).V := {z | ∃ VV' ⊆ VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w),
    ∃ x ∈ ((D.fm u).lat 0).L, z = yLP VV' (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : D.what₂.LL) ^ s VV') • x)}
  let T2 : Set ((D.fm u).lat 0).V := {z | ∃ x ∈ ((D.fm u).lat 0).L, z = ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : D.what₂.LL) ^ τ) • x}
  have hS1 : Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).O {F' | ∃ VV' ⊆ VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w),
      ∃ F ∈ (D.gat u w hg).M,
      F' = yProd Y u w hg VV' (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ s VV') • F)} =
      Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).O (c.cmp u w hg '' T1) := by
    apply le_antisymm
    · rw [Submodule.span_le]
      rintro _ ⟨VV', hVV', F, hF, rfl⟩
      let Q : Module.End (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Fn :=
        ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ s VV') •
          yProdL (D.gat u w hg) (VV'.toList.map (Y.vOf u w hg)) D.R.m
      have hQ : yProd Y u w hg VV' (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ s VV') • F) = Q F := by
        rw [hyP, map_smul]; rfl
      rw [SetLike.mem_coe, hQ]
      rw [hM] at hF
      have := Submodule.mem_map_of_mem (f := Q) hF
      rw [Submodule.map_span] at this
      refine Submodule.span_mono ?_ this
      rintro _ ⟨_, ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩, rfl⟩
      refine ⟨yLP VV' (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : D.what₂.LL) ^ s VV') • x), ⟨VV', hVV', x, hx, rfl⟩, ?_⟩
      show c.cmp u w hg _ = Q (c.cmp u w hg x)
      rw [hyLsmul, hpow, ← hrat, hyP]
      rfl
    · rw [Submodule.span_le]
      rintro _ ⟨z, ⟨VV', hVV', x, hx, rfl⟩, rfl⟩
      refine Submodule.subset_span ⟨VV', hVV', c.cmp u w hg x, ?_, ?_⟩
      · rw [hM]; exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
      · rw [hyLsmul, hpow, ← hrat, hyP, hyP, map_smul]
  have hS2 : (D.gat u w hg).M.map (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ τ) • LinearMap.id) =
      Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).O (c.cmp u w hg '' T2) := by
    rw [hM, Submodule.map_span]
    congr 1
    ext F
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨_, ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩, rfl⟩
      exact ⟨_, ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩, by rw [hpow]; rfl⟩
    · rintro ⟨_, ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩, rfl⟩
      exact ⟨_, ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩, by rw [hpow]; rfl⟩
  have hVr : Y.Vram u w hg = (Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).Kf
      (c.cmp u w hg '' ((D.fm u).ramSub : Set ((D.fm u).lat 0).V))).restrictScalars (D.gat u w hg).O :=
    SetLike.coe_injective (hv u w hg)
  have hflat := FBC.flat_ext (D.plEq w) (c.embW u w hg) (c.embW_val u w hg) (c.cmp u w hg) (c.cmp_smul u w hg)
    ((D.fm u).lat 0).b (c.cmp_li u w hg)
  have hinf := span_inf_span (D.plEq w) (c.embW u w hg) (c.embW_val u w hg) (c.cmp u w hg) (c.cmp_smul u w hg)
    ((D.fm u).lat 0).b (c.cmp_li u w hg) (Submodule.span (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) T1) (Submodule.span (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) T2)
  have hNP : NP X Y K u w hg = Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).O (c.cmp u w hg ''
      (((Submodule.span (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) T1 ⊓ Submodule.span (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) T2) ⊓
        (D.fm u).ramSub.restrictScalars (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) : Submodule (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) ((D.fm u).lat 0).V) :
          Set ((D.fm u).lat 0).V)) := by
    show Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).O _ ⊓ _ ⊓ _ = _
    rw [hS1, hS2, hVr, ← span_image_spanA c u w hg T1, ← span_image_spanA c u w hg T2, hinf]
    exact hflat.2 _ _
  calc NP X Y K u w hg = _ := hNP
    _ ≤ _ := by
      apply Submodule.span_mono
      rintro _ ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩
      refine ⟨y, ⟨hy.2, ?_⟩, rfl⟩
      rw [hNP]
      exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩


[GLG_rational_descent_07] M_inf_Vram, NPDesc and original rational-preimage NP_eq_span
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalRingCardinality/Lemmas.lean#L968-L1030
Range SHA256: 424679185b36389afd01e0d483352310f67cd4ea2b9f093383c17a61da8b62f7
theorem M_inf_Vram (Y : LatInst D) (hv : c.VramDef Y) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w))
    [IsFractionRing (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf] :
    (D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg =
      Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).O (c.cmpN u w hg '' (DV.MramL D u : Set (D.div.Nu u).V)) := by
  have h2 := (FBC.flat_ext (D.plEq w) (c.embW u w hg) (c.embW_val u w hg) (c.cmp u w hg) (c.cmp_smul u w hg)
    ((D.fm u).lat 0).b (c.cmp_li u w hg)).2 ((D.fm u).lat 0).L (D.fm u).ramSub
  have hV : Y.Vram u w hg = (Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).Kf
      (c.cmp u w hg '' ((D.fm u).ramSub : Set ((D.fm u).lat 0).V))).restrictScalars (D.gat u w hg).O :=
    SetLike.coe_injective (hv u w hg)
  rw [← c.cmp_M u w hg, hV, h2]
  have h3 : (((D.fm u).lat 0).L ⊓ (D.fm u).ramSub.restrictScalars (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) :
      Submodule (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) ((D.fm u).lat 0).V) = (D.fm u).ramL := rfl
  rw [h3, ← D.MramL_map u, Submodule.map_coe, Set.image_image]
  rfl

def NPDesc {L : SizeData₂ D} (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ≠ ∅ →
    NP X Y K u w hg ≤ Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).O
      (c.cmp u w hg '' {y | y ∈ (D.fm u).ramSub ∧ c.cmp u w hg y ∈ NP X Y K u w hg})

theorem NP_eq_span {L : SizeData₂ D} (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (hNu : DV.NuDef D L)
    (hs : c.NuServedDef X Y K) (hd : c.NPDesc X Y K) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w))
    [IsFractionRing (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf]
    (hVV : VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ≠ ∅) :
    NP X Y K u w hg = Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).O
      (c.cmpN u w hg '' ((L.NuLat u ⊓ DV.MramL D u : Submodule (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) (D.div.Nu u).V) : Set (D.div.Nu u).V)) := by
  have hT : D.what₂.places.resChar w ≠ (D.mem u).A.T := by
    intro h
    exact hg.2.2.2.2.2 (h ▸ dvd_rfl)
  apply le_antisymm
  · refine (hd u w hg hVV).trans ?_
    have h1 : c.cmp u w hg '' {y | y ∈ (D.fm u).ramSub ∧ c.cmp u w hg y ∈ NP X Y K u w hg} =
        c.cmpN u w hg '' {x | InLoc (D.plEq w) (L.NuLat u) x} := by
      ext z
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨y, ⟨hy1, hy2⟩, rfl⟩
        rw [← D.nuIn_range u] at hy1
        obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := hy1
        exact ⟨x, (hs u w hg hVV x).2 hy2, rfl⟩
      · rintro ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
        refine ⟨D.nuIn u x, ⟨?_, (hs u w hg hVV x).1 hx⟩, rfl⟩
        rw [← D.nuIn_range u]
        exact ⟨x, rfl⟩
    rw [h1]
    apply FBC.span_image_le_of_loc (D.plEq w) (c.embW u w hg) (c.embW_val u w hg) (c.cmpN u w hg) (c.cmpN_smul u w hg)
    rintro x ⟨s, hs1, hs2⟩
    obtain ⟨s', hs1', hs2'⟩ := hNu.1 u w hT _ hs2
    rw [pow_zero, Ideal.one_eq_top, Submodule.top_smul] at hs2'
    refine ⟨s' * s, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · exact fun h => ((D.plEq w).isPrime.mem_or_mem h).elim hs1' hs1
    · rw [mul_smul]; exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ hs2
    · rw [mul_smul]; exact hs2'
  · rw [Submodule.span_le]
    rintro _ ⟨x, ⟨hx, -⟩, rfl⟩
    exact (hs u w hg hVV x).1 ⟨1, fun h => (D.plEq w).isPrime.ne_top ((Ideal.eq_top_iff_one _).2 h), by rwa [one_smul]⟩

end LatCmp

theorem FBC.length_congr {O W : Type*} [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup W] [Module O W] {P₁ P₁' P₂ P₂' : Submodule O W}
    (h₁ : P₁ = P₁') (h₂ : P₂ = P₂') :


[GLG_lengths_01] LatTied and literal LatticeLengthsAtPlace
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/IsResidueClassZero.lean#L430-L483
Range SHA256: b1d4eb58964bbafa8b16c7c286715803f9e840052d774fabae17568fb77b87a3
def LatTied (hT : GatTies D) (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) : Prop :=
  ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    let p := D.what₂.places.resChar w
    (Y.Fl u w hg).mm = D.R.m ∧ (Y.Fl u w hg).t = D.R.t ∧
    ((Y.Fl u w hg).c : ℝ) ≤ cG L ∧
    (D.gat u w hg).P.r = (if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then rG L.sc else 1) ∧
    Set.InjOn (Y.vOf u w hg) (reps X p) ∧
    (∀ (v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV) (s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)) (t : HH D),
      ((D.eTau (hT.gat_pos u w hg ⟨v, s⟩) : D.R.Psi) : D.R.GG) = D.R.tau2 t →
        s ∉ (Y.Fl u w hg).TT v ∧
        ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ((Y.Fl u w hg).w v j s : ℝ) = fillG X (bL L) j (true, t)) ∧
    ∀ t0 ∈ reps X p,
      (D.gat u w hg).P.n' (Y.vOf u w hg t0) = addOrderOf (hP D p) ∧
      ∀ s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' (Y.vOf u w hg t0)),
        ((D.eTau (hT.gat_pos u w hg ⟨Y.vOf u w hg t0, s⟩) : D.R.Psi) : D.R.GG) =
          D.R.tau1 (t0 + s.val • hP D p) ∧
        (s ∈ (Y.Fl u w hg).TT (Y.vOf u w hg t0) ↔ t0 + s.val • hP D p ∈ D.host.TT) ∧
        (s ∈ (Y.Fl u w hg).TT (Y.vOf u w hg t0) →
          (Y.Fl u w hg).a (Y.vOf u w hg t0) s = aL L (t0 + s.val • hP D p) ∧
          (Y.Fl u w hg).b (Y.vOf u w hg t0) s = bL L (t0 + s.val • hP D p)) ∧
        ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D,
          ((Y.Fl u w hg).w (Y.vOf u w hg t0) j s : ℝ) = fillG X (bL L) j (false, t0 + s.val • hP D p)

def yProd (Y : LatInst D) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w))
    (VV' : Finset (HH D)) (F : (D.gat u w hg).Fn) : (D.gat u w hg).Fn :=
  (VV'.toList.map (fun t0 => ((D.gat u w hg).yv (Y.vOf u w hg t0))^[D.R.m])).foldr
    (fun f g => f ∘ g) id F

def lowFE (L : SizeData₂ D) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) : ℝ :=
  ∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ((L.F j w : ℝ) - (L.E u j w : ℝ))

open Classical in

def NP (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) :
    Submodule (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Fn :=
  let p := D.what₂.places.resChar w
  let G := D.gat u w hg
  let VVp := VV X K (aL L) (bL L) p
  Submodule.span G.O {F' | ∃ VV' ⊆ VVp, ∃ F ∈ G.M,
      F' = yProd Y u w hg VV' (((p : G.O) ^ (∑ t0 ∈ VVp \ VV', ⌈Sv X K (aL L) (bL L) p t0⌉.toNat)) • F)} ⊓
    G.M.map (((p : G.O) ^ ⌈tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) p⌉.toNat) • LinearMap.id) ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg

def LatticeLengthsAtPlace (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) : Prop :=
  (w ∈ L.supp → ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    ∃ l : ℕ, Module.length (D.gat u w hg).O
        (↥((D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg) ⧸
          (NP X Y K u w hg).comap ((D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg).subtype) = l ∧
      (l : ℝ) = (L.lenMN u w : ℝ) * vUnit (D.gat u w hg)) ∧
  ∀ u : D.what₂.Word, VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅ →
    D.what₂.places.resChar w ≠ (D.mem u).A.T → L.lenMN u w = 0

end Formalism.MainNotions.G


[GLG_lengths_02] exact ramification-normalized length transport and full empty-served case
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalRingCardinality/Lemmas.lean#L1061-L1182
Range SHA256: 7419f718505c53442045fb2c3f6f595474726db963f7ebc9033ee6fadf229bde
theorem length_quotient_of_nonempty {L : SizeData₂ D} (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (hNu : DV.NuDef D L)
    (hv : c.VramDef Y) (hs : c.NuServedDef X Y K) (hd : c.NPDesc X Y K) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w))
    [IsFractionRing (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf]
    (hVV : VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ≠ ∅)
    (hfin : Module.length (D.gat u w hg).O (↥((D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg) ⧸
      (NP X Y K u w hg).comap ((D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg).subtype) ≠ ⊤) :
    ∃ l : ℕ, Module.length (D.gat u w hg).O (↥((D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg) ⧸
          (NP X Y K u w hg).comap ((D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg).subtype) = l ∧
      (l : ℝ) = (L.lenMN u w : ℝ) * vUnit (D.gat u w hg) := by
  have key := (FBC.flat_ext (D.plEq w) (c.embW u w hg) (c.embW_val u w hg) (c.cmpN u w hg) (c.cmpN_smul u w hg)
    (D.div.Nu u).b (c.cmpN_li u w hg)).1 (L.NuLat u ⊓ DV.MramL D u) (DV.MramL D u) inf_le_right
    ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : 𝓞 D.what₂.LL) (D.what₂.places.ramIdx w) (D.val_resChar' w)
  rw [← FBC.length_congr (c.M_inf_Vram Y hv u w hg) (c.NP_eq_span X Y K hNu hs hd u w hg hVV), map_natCast] at key
  have hcomap : (L.NuLat u ⊓ DV.MramL D u).comap (DV.MramL D u).subtype = (L.NuLat u).comap (DV.MramL D u).subtype := by
    rw [Submodule.comap_inf, Submodule.comap_subtype_self, inf_top_eq]
  rw [hcomap] at key
  have key' : _ = DV.lenLoc (D.plEq w) (L.NuLat u) (DV.MramL D u) * _ := key
  obtain ⟨l, hl⟩ := ENat.ne_top_iff_exists.1 hfin
  have hp0 : ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : (D.gat u w hg).O) ≠ 0 := by
    intro h0
    have h1 : FBC.toO (D.plEq w) (c.embW u w hg) (c.embW_val u w hg) ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : 𝓞 D.what₂.LL) = 0 := by
      rw [map_natCast, h0]
    have h2 := FBC.toO_injective (D.plEq w) (c.embW u w hg) (c.embW_val u w hg) (h1.trans (map_zero _).symm)
    exact (D.what₂.places.resChar_prime w).ne_zero (by exact_mod_cast h2)
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := ENat.ne_top_iff_exists.1 (fun h => hp0 (addVal_eq_top_iff.1 h))
  have hU : vUnit (D.gat u w hg) = (n : ℝ) := by
    unfold vUnit
    rw [← hn]
    simp
  have hram : 0 < D.what₂.places.ramIdx w := D.what₂.places.ramIdx_pos w
  refine ⟨l, hl.symm, ?_⟩
  rw [← hl, ← hn] at key'
  rw [hU]
  by_cases hn0 : n = 0
  · subst hn0
    have : (l : ℕ∞) * (D.what₂.places.ramIdx w : ℕ∞) = 0 := by rw [key']; simp
    have h2 : l * D.what₂.places.ramIdx w = 0 := by exact_mod_cast this
    have h3 : l = 0 := (Nat.mul_eq_zero.1 h2).resolve_right hram.ne'
    simp [h3]
  · have hlt : DV.lenLoc (D.plEq w) (L.NuLat u) (DV.MramL D u) ≠ ⊤ := by
      intro h
      rw [h, ENat.top_mul (by exact_mod_cast hn0)] at key'
      exact ENat.natCast_ne_top _ (by exact_mod_cast key')
    obtain ⟨l', hl'⟩ := ENat.ne_top_iff_exists.1 hlt
    have h1 := hNu.2.2 u w l' hl'.symm
    rw [← hl'] at key'
    have h2 : l * D.what₂.places.ramIdx w = l' * n := by exact_mod_cast key'
    have h3 : (l : ℝ) * (D.what₂.places.ramIdx w : ℝ) = (l' : ℝ) * (n : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast h2
    rw [h1] at h3
    have hr : (0 : ℝ) < (D.what₂.places.ramIdx w : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast hram
    have h4 : ((l : ℝ) - (L.lenMN u w : ℝ) * (n : ℝ)) * (D.what₂.places.ramIdx w : ℝ) = 0 := by
      rw [sub_mul, h3]; ring
    rcases mul_eq_zero.1 h4 with h5 | h5
    · linarith
    · exact absurd h5 hr.ne'

end LatCmp

theorem DV.lenLoc_eq_zero {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module (𝓞 K) V]
    (w : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) (A B : Submodule (𝓞 K) V) (h : DV.LocLe w 0 B A) :
    DV.lenLoc w A B = 0 := by
  unfold DV.lenLoc
  rw [Module.length_eq_zero_iff, LocalizedModule.subsingleton_iff]
  intro m
  obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := Submodule.mkQ_surjective _ m
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hsx⟩ := h x.1 x.2
  rw [pow_zero, Ideal.one_eq_top, Submodule.top_smul] at hsx
  refine ⟨s, hs, ?_⟩
  rw [← map_smul, Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero]
  exact hsx

open Formalism.MainNotions.G IsDedekindDomain IsDiscreteValuationRing

variable {D : D7}

theorem inf_le_localSubmodule_of_empty {L : SizeData₂ D} (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (u : D.what₂.Word)
    (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w))
    (hVV : VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅) :
    (D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg ≤ NP X Y K u w hg := by
  rintro F ⟨hF1, hF2⟩
  unfold NP
  refine ⟨⟨?_, ?_⟩, hF2⟩
  · apply Submodule.subset_span
    refine ⟨∅, Finset.empty_subset _, F, hF1, ?_⟩
    simp [hVV, yProd]
  · refine ⟨F, hF1, ?_⟩
    simp [tauP, hVV]

theorem lenMN_unserved {L : SizeData₂ D} (hNu : DV.NuDef D L) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (h : DV.LocEq (D.plEq w) (L.NuLat u) (DV.MramL D u)) : L.lenMN u w = 0 := by
  have h1 := hNu.2.2 u w 0 (by rw [DV.lenLoc_eq_zero _ _ _ h.2]; rfl)
  have h2 : L.lenMN u w = 0 ∨ D.what₂.places.ramIdx w = 0 := by simpa using h1.symm
  exact h2.resolve_right (D.what₂.places.ramIdx_pos w).ne'

theorem sizeDataLat_of_fin (c : LatCmp D) {L : SizeData₂ D} (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (hNu : DV.NuDef D L)
    (hl : LatDefs D L X Y K c) (hun : NuUnserved X K) (hd : c.NPDesc X Y K)
    (hfr : ∀ u w hg, IsFractionRing (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf)
    (hfin : ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
      VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ≠ ∅ →
      Module.length (D.gat u w hg).O (↥((D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg) ⧸
        (NP X Y K u w hg).comap ((D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg).subtype) ≠ ⊤)
    (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) : LatticeLengthsAtPlace X Y K w := by
  refine ⟨fun _ u hg => ?_, fun u hVV hT => lenMN_unserved hNu u w (hun u w hT hVV)⟩
  by_cases hVV : VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅
  · have hT : D.what₂.places.resChar w ≠ (D.mem u).A.T := fun h => hg.2.2.2.2.2 (h ▸ dvd_rfl)
    refine ⟨0, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [Submodule.comap_subtype_eq_top.2 (inf_le_localSubmodule_of_empty X Y K u w hg hVV)]
      simp
    · rw [lenMN_unserved hNu u w (hun u w hT hVV)]
      simp
  · have := hfr u w hg
    exact c.length_quotient_of_nonempty X Y K hNu hl.vram hl.served hd u w hg hVV (hfin u w hg hVV)

end LatCmp

theorem frac_of_ties (hT : GatTies D) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) :

    unfold Ranks.f Ranks.n
    positivity
  obtain ⟨τ⟩ := Fintype.card_pos_iff.1 h
  exact ((D.gat u w hg).P.setting ((hT.gat_pos u w hg).symm τ).1).frac_O

[GLG_selected_tops_01] actual ChTT/ChBad pointwise and eventual selected-top predicates
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GlobalSchurBounds/Chosen.lean#L15-L70
Range SHA256: 494b55f806b558c7fec84af897813cf4f72cd5935b9932ffa93284a1edc61a43

def ThickSetLowerBound (_L : SizeData₂ D) : Prop := D.host.secondSet_card_lower_bound

def ChTT_at : Prop :=
  L.sc.delta1 * D.R.n / 2 ≤ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ∧ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ≤ 2 * L.sc.delta1 * D.R.n

open Classical in

def largeOrderResiduePrimes : Finset ℕ :=
  (Finset.range ⌈L.sc.X'⌉₊).filter (fun p =>
    p.Prime ∧ G.IsResidueClassZero D p ∧ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) ≤ (addOrderOf (G.hP D p) : ℝ))

variable {L}

open Classical in

def badTops (X : G.Inst D L) (p : ℕ) : Finset (G.HH D) :=
  D.host.TT.filter (fun t => ¬ G.Good X p (X.bs (G.stepP D p) t))

def ChBad_at (X : G.Inst D L) (Cb : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∑ p ∈ largeOrderResiduePrimes L, Real.log p / p * ((badTops X p).card : ℝ) ≤
    4 * (Cb * L.sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ)) * L.sc.delta1 * D.R.n * ∑ p ∈ largeOrderResiduePrimes L, Real.log p / p

def BoundsAtX (x eps C : ℝ) (f : ℕ → ℂ) (M : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N), GlobalTheoryTheory.Induces χ f M →
    ‖GlobalTheoryTheory.gChi χ x‖ ≤ x ^ ((1 : ℝ) / 2 + eps) ∧ ‖GlobalTheoryTheory.logDerivativeAtOne χ‖ ≤ C * Real.log x

def ChQD_at (D : D7) (x eps C : ℝ) : Prop :=
  BoundsAtX x eps C (GlobalTheoryTheory.psDir D.host.q) (8 * D.host.q * D.host.d) ∧
  BoundsAtX x eps C (GlobalTheoryTheory.chDir D.host.d) (8 * D.host.q * D.host.d) ∧
  BoundsAtX x eps C (fun a => GlobalTheoryTheory.chDir D.host.d a * GlobalTheoryTheory.psDir D.host.q a) (8 * D.host.q * D.host.d)

def ChThin_at (D : D7) (x eps c : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N), GlobalTheoryTheory.IsCharED D χ →
    ∀ y : ℝ, Real.exp (x ^ (eps / 3)) / 3 ≤ y → ‖GlobalTheoryTheory.gChi χ y‖ ≤ y * Real.exp (-(c * x ^ (eps / 8)))

variable (L)

def ChProviso_at (C0 : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∀ u, C0⁻¹ ≤ (D.duS u : ℝ) * GamL L ^ 2 / D.what₂.Dstar ∧ (D.duS u : ℝ) * GamL L ^ 2 / D.what₂.Dstar ≤ C0

def ChCode_at (C0 CH : ℝ) : Prop :=
  C0 * GamL L ^ 2 * L.sc.x ^ CH ≤ (Fintype.card D.what₂.Word : ℝ)

end AtRecord

section Family

variable {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps)

def ThickSetEventually : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ThickSetLowerBound (Fm.L x i)

def ChosenTT : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ChTT_at (Fm.L x i)

def ChosenBad (Cb : ℝ) : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ChBad_at (Fm.Gx x i) Cb



[GLG_selected_tops_02] full TopsF-to-Adm2F finite harmonic consumer
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/SmoothDesignExistence/AdmissibleTopsMertens.lean#L15-L146
Range SHA256: 32d4c794a3f9b5d575c5d5eb1051f6a9142ad56517db326a11d3a436ab3935a1
def TopsF {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop := ChosenTT Fm ∧ ∃ Cb : ℝ, 0 < Cb ∧ ChosenBad Fm Cb

theorem topsF_of_chosenE {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) {C0 CH : ℝ} (h : ChosenE Fm C0 CH) : TopsF Fm := by
  obtain ⟨C5, c6, Cb, -, -, hCb, -, hTT, hBad, -⟩ := h
  exact ⟨hTT, Cb, hCb, hBad⟩

private lemma sum_log_div_le_two_mul_mul_add_two (s : Finset ℕ) (T : ℝ) (hT : 0 ≤ T)
    (hs : ∀ p ∈ s, 1 ≤ p ∧ p < ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊) :
    ∑ p ∈ s, Real.log p / p ≤ 2 * T * (T + 2) := by
  have hterm : ∀ p ∈ s, Real.log p / p ≤ T * (2 / ((p : ℝ) + 1)) := by
    intro p hp
    obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := hs p hp
    have hp1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast h1
    have hp0 : (0 : ℝ) < p := by linarith
    have hpe : (p : ℝ) < Real.exp T := Nat.lt_ceil.1 h2
    have hlog : Real.log p ≤ T := by
      have h := Real.log_le_log hp0 hpe.le
      rwa [Real.log_exp] at h
    have e1 : Real.log p / p ≤ T / p := by gcongr
    have h3 : 1 / (p : ℝ) ≤ 2 / ((p : ℝ) + 1) := by
      first
      | (rw [div_le_div_iff₀ hp0 (by linarith)]; linarith)
      | (rw [div_le_div_iff hp0 (by linarith)]; linarith)
    have e2 : T / p ≤ T * (2 / ((p : ℝ) + 1)) := by
      calc T / p = T * (1 / p) := by ring
        _ ≤ T * (2 / ((p : ℝ) + 1)) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h3 hT
    linarith
  have hsub : s ⊆ Finset.range ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ := fun p hp => Finset.mem_range.2 (hs p hp).2
  have hh : ∑ p ∈ Finset.range ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊, ((p : ℝ) + 1)⁻¹ = (harmonic ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ : ℝ) := by
    first
    | simp [harmonic]
    | (rw [harmonic]; push_cast; rfl)
  have hlN : Real.log (⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ : ℝ) ≤ T + 1 := by
    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ with h0 | h0
    · rw [h0]
      simp only [Nat.cast_zero, Real.log_zero]
      linarith
    · have hN0 : (0 : ℝ) < (⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast h0
      have hE : (1 : ℝ) ≤ Real.exp T := Real.one_le_exp hT
      have hc := Nat.ceil_lt_add_one (Real.exp_pos T).le
      have hNle : (⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ : ℝ) ≤ 2 * Real.exp T := by linarith
      have h2 := Real.log_le_log hN0 hNle
      rw [Real.log_mul (by norm_num) (Real.exp_pos T).ne', Real.log_exp] at h2
      have hl2 : Real.log 2 ≤ 1 := by
        have h := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos (show (0 : ℝ) < 2 by norm_num)
        linarith
      linarith
  calc ∑ p ∈ s, Real.log p / p ≤ ∑ p ∈ s, T * (2 / ((p : ℝ) + 1)) := Finset.sum_le_sum hterm
    _ ≤ ∑ p ∈ Finset.range ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊, T * (2 / ((p : ℝ) + 1)) :=
        Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg hsub (fun p _ _ => by positivity)
    _ = 2 * T * ∑ p ∈ Finset.range ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊, ((p : ℝ) + 1)⁻¹ := by
        rw [Finset.mul_sum]
        exact Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun p _ => by ring)
    _ ≤ 2 * T * (1 + Real.log (⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ : ℝ)) := by
        rw [hh]
        exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (harmonic_le_one_add_log _) (by positivity)
    _ ≤ 2 * T * (T + 2) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) (by positivity)

theorem admissible_tops_mertens {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (h : TopsF Fm) : G.Adm2F Fm := by
  obtain ⟨⟨xa, hTT⟩, Cb, hCb, xb, hBad⟩ := h
  unfold G.Adm2F G.Ev
  refine ⟨max (max xa xb) (max (Fm.x1 + 1) ((16 * Cb + 2) ^ (3 / eps))), fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  have hxa : xa ≤ x := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) (le_max_left _ _)) hx
  have hxb : xb ≤ x := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) (le_max_left _ _)) hx
  have hx1' : Fm.x1 + 1 ≤ x := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) (le_max_right _ _)) hx
  have hx1 : Fm.x1 < x := by linarith
  have hxc : (16 * Cb + 2) ^ (3 / eps) ≤ x := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) (le_max_right _ _)) hx
  obtain ⟨hT1, hT2⟩ := hTT x hxa i
  have hB := hBad x hxb i
  unfold ChBad_at at hB
  have hLx : (Fm.L x i).sc.x = x := Fm.L_x x i hx1
  have hLe : (Fm.L x i).sc.eps = eps := Fm.L_eps x i
  have he' : 0 < (Fm.L x i).sc.eps := (Fm.L x i).sc.eps_pos
  have he : 0 < eps := by rw [← hLe]; exact he'
  have hy1 : 1 < (Fm.L x i).sc.x := (Fm.L x i).sc.one_lt_alt
  have hy0 : 0 < (Fm.L x i).sc.x := by linarith
  have hT0 : 0 ≤ (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3) := by positivity
  have hTbig : 16 * Cb + 2 ≤ (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3) := by
    rw [hLx, hLe]
    have hc : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 16 * Cb + 2 := by linarith
    have hne : eps ≠ 0 := he.ne'
    have hm : 3 / eps * (eps / 3) = 1 := by
      first
      | (field_simp; ring)
      | field_simp
    calc 16 * Cb + 2 = ((16 * Cb + 2) ^ (3 / eps)) ^ (eps / 3) := by
          rw [← Real.rpow_mul hc, hm, Real.rpow_one]
      _ ≤ x ^ (eps / 3) := Real.rpow_le_rpow (by positivity) hxc (by positivity)
  have hS := sum_log_div_le_two_mul_mul_add_two (largeOrderResiduePrimes (Fm.L x i)) ((Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3)) hT0
    (fun p hp => by
      simp only [largeOrderResiduePrimes, Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_range] at hp
      exact ⟨hp.2.1.one_lt.le, hp.1⟩)
  have hBTT : AuxBlock8.BTT (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) ≤
      ∑ p ∈ largeOrderResiduePrimes (Fm.L x i), Real.log p / p * ((badTops (Fm.Gx x i) p).card : ℝ) := by
    unfold AuxBlock8.BTT
    apply Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg
    · intro p hp
      simp only [AuxBlock8.primesResidueBelow, largeOrderResiduePrimes, Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_range] at hp ⊢
      exact ⟨hp.1.1, hp.1.2.1, hp.1.2.2.2, hp.2⟩
    · intro p _ _
      have hl : 0 ≤ Real.log (p : ℝ) := by
        rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos p with h0 | h0
        · simp [h0]
        · exact Real.log_nonneg (by exact_mod_cast h0)
      exact mul_nonneg (div_nonneg hl (Nat.cast_nonneg p)) (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
  have hdn : 0 ≤ (Fm.L x i).sc.delta1 * (Fm.D x i).R.n := by
    have hc0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ ((Fm.D x i).host.TT.card : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
    linarith
  have hA : 0 < (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ) := by positivity
  have hpow : (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ (-2 + (Fm.L x i).sc.eps) =
      (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ) * ((Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3)) ^ 3 := by
    have h3 : (Fm.L x i).sc.eps = (Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3 * ((3 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by push_cast; ring
    rw [Real.rpow_add hy0, ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hy0.le, ← h3]
  unfold AuxBlock8.adm2
  refine ⟨⟨by linarith, by linarith⟩, ?_⟩
  rw [hpow]
  obtain ⟨T, hTe⟩ : ∃ T, T = (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3) := ⟨_, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨A, hAe⟩ : ∃ A, A = (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ) := ⟨_, rfl⟩
  rw [← hTe] at hS hTbig hT0 ⊢
  rw [← hAe] at hA hB ⊢
  have k1 : 16 * Cb * (T + 2) ≤ T ^ 2 := by
    nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hTbig hT0]
  have k2 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left k1
    (div_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hdn hA.le) hT0) (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 2))
  have k3 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hS
    (mul_nonneg (by positivity : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 4 * (Cb * A)) hdn)
  have k4 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hT1 (by positivity : (0 : ℝ) ≤ A * T ^ 3)
  linarith [hBTT, hB, k2, k3, k4]

end Formalism.MainNotions.Pk

end


[GLG_selected_tops_03] BTT and exact full adm2 inequality
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/SmallOrderPrimes.lean#L85-L99
Range SHA256: 0aa1a4cb8319b3488b30216eacf8e1f684d5c691cb51aabc36bbc7af8ba82bca
def primesResidueBelow (D : D7) (y : ℝ) : Finset ℕ :=
  (Finset.range ⌈y⌉₊).filter (fun p => p.Prime ∧ (p : ℝ) < y ∧ IsResidueClassZero D p)

open Classical in

def BTT (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) : ℝ :=
  ∑ p ∈ (primesResidueBelow D L.sc.X').filter (fun p => L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) ≤ (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ)),
    Real.log p / p * ((D.host.TT.filter (fun t => ¬ Good X p (X.bs (stepP D p) t))).card : ℝ)

def adm2 (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) : Prop :=
  ((D.R.n : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1 / 2 ≤ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ∧ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ≤ 2 * (D.R.n : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1) ∧
  BTT D L X ≤ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-2 + L.sc.eps)

open Classical in
